# Neo Miniapp Platform — UI Design Briefs

Functional + interface descriptions for the host shell and all 60 miniapps, written for a UI designer. Each app lists its purpose, user flow, the actual rendered layout, the distinct screens/states to mock, and its visual identity. Hand any section (plus the design-system block below) to a design tool to generate mockups.

_Generated 2026-06-19 from the live source + rendered screenshots of all 62 surfaces._

## Design system — "Neo Soft" (apply to every screen)

- **Theme**: light. Surfaces = white `#ffffff` (cards) and subtle `#f7f8fa` (insets/fields/inputs). Hairline borders `rgba(20,22,38,0.06)`. Soft shadows `0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,0.06)`.
- **Text**: titles ink `#1e1e2e`; body `#2a2d3d` / `#5b6478`; muted `#6b7180`. **Eyebrow labels** = 11px, 700, UPPERCASE, letter-spacing .12em, muted gray.
- **Brand green accent**: `#16c784` bright (decorative — badges, icon fills, soft chips), `#0a8050` for filled CTAs with white text (hover `#086a45`), `#0b5e3e` deep green for green text on light surfaces, soft fill `#e4f8f0`. WCAG AA throughout (never white-on-bright-green, never bright-green text on soft-green).
- **Shape**: rounded cards radius 12 / 16 / 20 / 24px; pill buttons & tabs (radius 9999px); 40-48px circular icon badges.
- **Type scale**: hero titles 24–30px / 800, letter-spacing -0.02em; section headings ~18px/700; stat figures 18–24px / 800 tabular-nums; body 13–15px.
- **Layout**: desktop ~1200–1365px — typically a **hero band** on top (circular badge + eyebrow + H2 title + subtitle + a row of stat tiles, often with a wallet/status card on the right) then a **2-column workspace** (a form/command column + a state/results column). Mobile = 390px, single column.
- **Empty / first-run states**: a friendly robot mascot illustration (or a line icon) + heading + one-line hint; a few apps use a themed line icon.
- **A few apps carry an intentional secondary theme** (noted per-app under Visual identity): otherwise everything is the single green system.

# Host Shell (the OS / platform)

## Neo Miniapp Platform (Host Shell)  `host`
*Category: host*

> The Neo Soft OS shell where users browse a catalog of on-chain miniapps, connect a Neo wallet once, and run any miniapp inside a framed workspace with a live action console.

**Purpose.** The host shell is the operating-system layer that wraps ~60 individual Neo miniapps. A user opens it to discover apps (a launcher grid with categories, search, and featured/operator-tool rows), connect a Neo N3 wallet or social login once at the platform level, and then launch any miniapp into a consistent framed surface where the embedded app's PlayArea runs alongside host-provided chrome (sticky app header, wallet/network status, a focus-action console, transaction confirmation, reference/diagnostics, and docs). It also hosts account management (wallet + linked socials), a developer portal for templating and publishing new miniapps, and global concerns: top nav, search, network (mainnet/testnet) switching, notifications, and locale (EN/中).

**Functional description.** BROWSE/LAUNCH: On the launcher home a user sees a hero card (network badge + NEP-21 Ready / OneGate Export chips, mascot, title, "Browse Catalog" + "Developer" CTAs, and a 3-figure Platform Status panel: Enabled apps / Featured / Operator tools), a Featured apps aside (compact rows with status dot + open arrow), then a catalog block with a search input, a left category sidebar (All, Gaming, DeFi, Social, NFT, Governance, Utility — each with icon + count), a Featured/Recent/Name sort pill row, a "showing N" count, and a responsive grid of HomeMiniAppRow cards (logo, name, live/pending/tool/other-network status pill, 2-line description). Below are Operator Tools, a Features grid, and a CTA band. The /miniapps catalog/browse view repeats the pattern with a larger hero (live network pill, big search, three big quick-filter tiles Live/Tools/Coming soon with counts, and a gradient "lead preview" card spotlighting the top result), filter chips (status + category) and a single-column list of rich market rows (gradient accent rail, logo, animated live dot, category chip, "Open app" pill).
CONNECT WALLET: A Connect button in the top nav opens a centered modal (white, radius-24, dark blur backdrop) with two groups — "Email & social" (continue-with provider buttons) and "Neo ecosystem" wallet options (NeoLine, O3, OneGate, etc., each a card with icon, name, optional Recommended tag, description, and a NEP-21 protocol chip); an optional dev-only WIF paste field (amber). Once connected the button becomes a wallet chip (provider icon + green live dot, truncated address, GAS balance) with a dropdown (copy address, view on explorer, account settings, disconnect); a persisted-but-not-restored session shows an amber "Reconnect" resume chip.
RUN A MINIAPP (embedded surface): launching routes to the miniapp detail page. A sticky compact app header (back arrow, app icon, name, category, colored status dot+label Online/Beta/Maintenance/Pending, Flagship tag, Contract/MiniApp surface chip) sits under the global nav. The body is a 2-column workspace: LEFT = the MiniAppPlayfield (the embedded app's PlayArea, fed live on-chain stats + activity, polled every 15s; render crashes degrade to a refresh card) plus a collapsed "Reference and diagnostics" accordion (status board: contract hash, runtime, network, activity feed + news/notifications; and the app's detail tabs/docs). RIGHT (sticky aside) = the Focus action console: "Focus action" eyebrow + operation title, a status pill (Wallet Ready / Wallet Required / Workspace Preview / API Ready / Synced / Checking wallet…), the OperationPanel (operation selector + parameter form + invoke), an optional OneGate launch card with QR, a wallet/address status row, a network-safety badge, and a transaction-feedback notice (txid, shortened, View on explorer link, and a Confirming…/Confirmed/Failed/Submitted badge driven by polling the application log — a FAULT recolors the whole notice red). On mobile the aside collapses into a bottom action dock that opens a focus-action bottom sheet. The shell guards against double-submits across the embedded workspace and the host console, and blocks actions when the wallet is on the wrong network or the app isn't deployed on the target network.
ACCOUNT/DEVELOPER: /account shows a PageHero (wallet status + linked-socials stats) then cards for the connected wallet (address, copy, explorer) and OAuth/social bindings (link/unlink). /developer is a builder portal: a features strip (Template SDK, Schema Guard, Contract Templating, Template Market) and a drawer-style builder form (app_id/name/desc EN+ZH, icon, category, template type, contract template/hash, URLs, developer identity) that validates and submits a new miniapp definition, plus a template marketplace section.

**Interface description.** Neo Soft light system throughout on a #f6f8fb / #f7f8fb page wash, white cards (radius 12/16/20/24), hairline gray-200 borders, soft shadows (0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,.06)), and the green brand accent (Tailwind emerald-* aliased to brand green; --neo focus ring; emerald-700 #0a8050-ish filled CTAs, emerald-50/emerald-700 soft chips). GLOBAL NAV: a fixed translucent top bar (white/60 → white/80 + blur + shadow on scroll) with a rounded logo mark + "{Brand} MiniApps" wordmark (green "MiniApps"), a pill nav-link group (MiniApps / Docs / Developer, active link gets a white pill), a rounded search input (lg+ only), a notifications bell dropdown, an EN/中 globe language toggle, and the wallet Connect button; mobile collapses to a hamburger sheet. A Footer sits on most pages (detail page hides it). LAUNCHER HOME: max-width ~1500–1600px, a 2-up hero grid (hero card + 440px Featured aside), then the catalog band: white search header card, a 288px sticky category sidebar (Filter icon heading + count-bearing category buttons), and a 1/2/3-col card grid with skeletons and a friendly empty state (LayoutGrid icon + heading + hint). CATALOG/BROWSE: centered max-w-6xl hero with a pulsing live network pill, 3xl/4xl extrabold title, large 56px search, three 96–112px quick-filter tiles (circular pastel icon tokens: mint/blue/peach), a gradient emerald→white→violet lead-preview aside, status + category filter chip rows, and a vertical list of market rows. EMBEDDED MINIAPP: sticky ~48px app header (top-16) over a max-w-[1580px] grid `1fr / 360px`; left = play-area canvas card + a details/accordion summary card ("Reference and diagnostics" with chevron); right = the focus-action panel (white, radius-12, shadow-md) carrying the eyebrow/title, a colored status pill, the embedded OperationPanel, OneGate QR card, gray inset wallet-status row, amber availability warnings, and emerald/red tx-result notices; mobile shows a fixed emerald bottom action button + slide-up sheet (dark scrim). WALLET MODAL: portal, max-w-440, radius-24, two labeled sections with eyebrow captions (11px bold uppercase), provider cards with hover green glow. ACCOUNT/DEVELOPER reuse PageHero (radial green/blue tint, eyebrow in neo green, 3xl/4xl title, white/80 stat tiles with 2xl black tabular figures) plus glass cards and a framer-motion builder drawer. Status semantics use color tones consistently: live=emerald, pending=amber, other-network=sky, tool/neutral=gray, fault=red.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Launcher home** — Home (/) — fixed top nav + hero card (network/NEP-21/OneGate chips, mascot, title, Browse Catalog + Developer CTAs, 3-figure Platform Status panel), a Featured apps aside, then the catalog band: search header, sticky category sidebar with counts, Featured/Recent/Name sort pills + shown-count, and a 1/2/3-col grid of miniapp cards (logo, name, status pill, description) with skeleton-loading and a robot/LayoutGrid empty state. Operator Tools, Features grid, and CTA band below.
- **Catalog / browse (/miniapps)** — Discovery view: centered hero with pulsing live network pill, large search, three quick-filter tiles (Live / Tools / Coming soon with counts and pastel circular icons), a gradient lead-preview card spotlighting the top app, status + category filter chip rows with a 'showing N of M' readout, and a vertical list of rich market rows (gradient accent rail, logo, live dot, category chip, Open-app pill). Loading skeletons + dashed empty state.
- **Embedded running miniapp (/miniapps/[id])** — The framed miniapp surface: sticky compact app header (back, icon, name, category, status dot, Flagship/surface chips) under the global nav; left column = the embedded PlayArea canvas (live on-chain stats/activity) + a collapsed Reference & diagnostics accordion (status board + detail tabs/docs); right sticky aside = Focus action console (operation title, status pill, OperationPanel form, OneGate QR card, wallet-status row, network-safety badge, tx-confirmation notice with explorer link and Confirmed/Failed/Submitted state). Mobile: fixed emerald bottom action button opening a focus-action bottom sheet.
- **Wallet connect modal** — Centered portal modal (white, radius-24, blurred dark scrim): welcome heading, an 'Email & social' group of continue-with provider buttons, an OR divider, and a 'Neo ecosystem' group of wallet provider cards (icon, name, Recommended tag, description, NEP-21 chip) with green hover glow; optional dev-only amber WIF paste field. Connected state shows the nav wallet chip + dropdown (copy, explorer, account, disconnect) and an amber reconnect/resume chip variant.
- **Account & Developer portal** — /account: PageHero (wallet status + linked-socials stat tiles) over cards for the connected wallet (address, copy, view on explorer, status badge) and OAuth/social account bindings (link/unlink rows). /developer: features strip (Template SDK, Schema Guard, Contract Templating, Template Market) and a builder drawer form (bilingual name/description, icon, category, template type, contract template/hash, URLs, developer identity) with validation + submit result toast, plus a template marketplace section.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the green brand accent as the system color: emerald-700 filled pill/CTA buttons with white text, emerald-50/emerald-700 soft status chips, --neo (#16c784) focus rings and a glowing green "connected" live dot. Signature host motifs: the floating Neo robot/mascot mark in the home hero, pulsing animated live-status dots (emerald ping), per-category accent rails/dots on app rows (category-themed hues), pastel circular icon tokens on the catalog quick-filter tiles (mint/blue/peach), and the radial green→blue tinted PageHero band. Network identity is a recurring pill (mainnet/testnet). The shell stays neutral-green so each embedded miniapp's own accent (e.g., coral/gold red-envelope, mint time-capsule) reads through inside the framed workspace.

# Games

## Burn League  `burn-league`
*Category: game*

> A winner-takes-all seasonal contest where players burn GAS into a shared pool and only the season's top burner walks away with the entire prize.

**Purpose.** A designer must mock a vertically stacked single-column app (hero + season banner + burn card + leaderboard) rather than the platform's usual 2-column workspace. The emotional core is the prize pool + countdown + leaderboard rank.

**Functional description.** Primary flow: the user lands on the Burn tab → reviews the season banner (dormant / active-with-countdown / ended) → enters an Amount in GAS (free input, range 1–1000 GAS, validated; suffix "GAS"; hint "Burn range: 1-1000 GAS") OR taps a preset chip (1 GAS / 5 GAS / 10 GAS / 25 GAS) → an Impact strip live-recomputes four read-outs: ENTRY AMOUNT (e.g. "1 GAS"), PROJECTED TOTAL (their existing burn + new amount), PROJECTED RANK (e.g. "#1", computed by counting leaderboard entries above their projected total), and PRIZE POOL → user clicks the full-width green "Burn Now" CTA → confirms in wallet → on success the burn is recorded on-chain, leaderboard/pool refresh, and a "Burn confirmed — your season total is updated" notice + "Last submitted burn: {amount}" line appear. Secondary actions: an "i" review-tip button beside Burn Now (popover checklist: Confirm amount / Review rank impact / Sign wallet intent), a "Reset" button (resets amount to 1), and a "How burning wins" disclosure toggle that expands a 4-step ordered list (Pick an amount → Burn into the pool → Climb the leaderboard → Top burner takes it all, last step highlighted as the prize step). Season lifecycle: when dormant the first burn opens a fresh season; when active a countdown (HH:MM:SS), prize pool, current leader and season length are shown and burning is enabled; when ended, burning is BLOCKED ("Burning is paused until the ended season is settled") and a "Settle season" button pays the whole pool to the top burner — triggering a confetti celebration modal ("You won the pool!" / "Season settled"). Edge flows a designer must reflect: a degraded-data amber notice ("Burn league data unavailable" / "SEASON STATUS: DATA PENDING") when chain stats can't be read (form still usable); a leader badge ("You're #1 — defend the pool") replacing the rank line when the user leads an active season; and a prepaid-credit recovery card ("Prepaid credit · {amount}") with a "Withdraw credit" button when a deposit's burn didn't complete.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout (~720px content column, centered) — NOT the standard 2-column workspace. Top to bottom: (1) HERO CARD (white, rounded, soft shadow) with a left lead block — a soft-mint rounded square badge containing a green flame glyph, an eyebrow "LIVE LEAGUE" (11px uppercase tracked), title "Burn League" (~28px/800), subtitle "Burn tokens, earn rewards", and a contextual third line that is EITHER the leader badge, OR a "Your Rank #N of M players" line, OR the first-burn prompt "Be the first to burn this season and top the leaderboard." On the right is the FOCAL PRIZE POOL block: label "PRIZE POOL" with a big tabular figure, or when empty an eyebrow "START THE SEASON" + small flame + hint "Be the first burn and open a fresh season." Below it a compact muted sub-row: "TOTAL BURNED 0.00 GAS" and "YOUR BURNS 0.00 GAS". (2) SEASON BANNER (full-width status card, phase-tinted): a head row with eyebrow "SEASON --" (left) and status "Not started" / "Live now" / "Ended — awaiting settle" (right); dormant shows a note "No active season yet — the first burn starts a fresh season."; active shows a 3–4 fact row (Ends in + countdown, Prize pool, Current leader, Season length); ended shows the settle hint + a green "Settle season" button. (3) BURN TOKENS CARD (white): header with section title "BURN TOKENS" (left) and a pill-style "How burning wins ⌄" toggle (green text, right); optional amber service-notice box ("Burn league data unavailable" bold + body text + "SEASON STATUS: DATA PENDING"); the form: an "Amount" labeled number input with right-aligned "GAS" suffix and hint "Burn range: 1-1000 GAS", a row of 4 pill preset chips (1/5/10/25 GAS, active = soft-green fill + green text), then a 4-cell IMPACT STRIP (inset #f7f8fa rounded panel with vertical dividers): ENTRY AMOUNT / PROJECTED TOTAL / PROJECTED RANK / PRIZE POOL, each an uppercase label over an 800 tabular value; then the action row: a full-width filled deep-green "Burn Now" CTA, a small soft-green circular "i" review-tip button, and an outline "Reset" button. (4) LEADERBOARD CARD (white): section title "LEADERBOARD" then either ranked rows (#rank · truncated address 0x1234... abcd · burned GAS amount + "Burned" caption) or an empty state (centered soft-mint flame badge + "No leaderboard entries yet" + "Burns appear here with rank and burned GAS as soon as they confirm on chain for this season."). Optional (5) RECOVERY CARD for prepaid credit. Color identity: single green hue throughout — bright #16c784 for the flame/badges/soft chips, deep #0a8050/#0b5e3e for the filled CTA and green text, soft #e4f8f0 fills; amber only for the data-unavailable notice. Hairline borders, 12–20px radii, pill buttons/tabs.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Dormant / first-run (no active season)** — Hero shows empty prize-pool slot with 'START THE SEASON' eyebrow + flame + 'Be the first burn and open a fresh season'; sub-row TOTAL BURNED 0.00 / YOUR BURNS 0.00. Season banner: 'SEASON --' / 'Not started' + note 'No active season yet — the first burn starts a fresh season.' Burn card with amount=1 prefilled, presets, impact strip showing dashes/PRIZE POOL 'Start the season'. Leaderboard empty state with flame mascot. This matches the current screenshot.
- **Active season — ready to burn** — Hero shows live PRIZE POOL figure (e.g. 12.50 GAS) and either 'Your Rank #3 of 8 players' or the first-burn prompt. Season banner phase-tinted 'Live now' with the fact row: Ends in 02:14:07 countdown, Prize pool, Current leader, Season length. Burn form fully enabled; impact strip live-computes ENTRY / PROJECTED TOTAL / PROJECTED RANK / PRIZE POOL. Populated leaderboard rows below.
- **Leader state (you are #1)** — Same as active, but the hero's contextual line becomes the green pill badge 'You're #1 — defend the pool' with a status dot, replacing the rank line. Tension-forward variant emphasizing defense.
- **Submitting / burning** — 'Burn Now' CTA in loading state ('Burning...'), form inputs disabled; optional preparing notice 'Preparing a {amount} burn wallet confirmation. Keep this window open...'. Designer should show the busy/disabled treatment of the whole action row.
- **Season ended — settle required** — Season banner 'Ended — awaiting settle' with hint 'The season has ended. Settle to award the {amount} pool to the top burner.' + green 'Settle season' button. Burn card shows blocking notice 'Burning is paused until the ended season is settled.' and a disabled Burn Now.
- **Win / settle celebration** — Centered confetti modal over the page: flame icon, title 'You won the pool!' (or 'Season settled'), body 'The season settled and {amount} was paid to your wallet as the top burner.', and a 'Continue' primary button. Confetti pieces animate.
- **Degraded data (chain unavailable)** — Amber notice box inside the burn card: bold 'Burn league data unavailable' + 'Live burn stats could not be read from the chain right now. You can still prepare a burn...' + 'SEASON STATUS: DATA PENDING'. Hero pool and stats fall back to placeholders; form remains usable. Matches the current screenshot's amber banner.
- **Prepaid credit recovery** — Extra card at the bottom: 'Prepaid credit · {amount}' title + hint 'Unused GAS from a burn that didn't complete. Reused on your next burn, or withdraw it now.' with a 'Withdraw credit' secondary button.

**Visual identity.** Green brand accent (single hue, no violet) — the signature mark is a custom brand-tinted flame glyph (two-tone, currentColor with a faint outer flame at 0.16 opacity) used in the hero badge, the empty prize-pool slot, the leaderboard empty state, and the win celebration. Soft-mint #e4f8f0 rounded badges hold the flame; deep green #0a8050 for the "Burn Now" CTA. Distinctive moments: the HH:MM:SS season countdown, the live prize-pool figure as the hero's focal point, and a confetti burst celebration modal on settle/win ("You won the pool!"). Amber is the only off-accent color, reserved for the degraded-data notice.

---

## Daily Check-in  `daily-checkin`
*Category: game*

> Check in once every UTC day to build an on-chain streak and earn milestone GAS rewards at day 7 and day 14.

**Purpose.** Daily Check-in is a streak-habit game where a user connects their Neo wallet and performs one on-chain check-in per UTC day. Each check-in deposits a tiny GAS fee to a standalone contract and advances the user's streak; landing on day 7 pays 0.01 GAS and day 14 pays 0.02 GAS (cumulative 0.03), after which the cycle resets. Users open it to keep their streak alive against a global UTC-midnight countdown, watch their week-progress dots fill, and claim accrued GAS rewards. It rewards consistency with small, transparent, on-chain payouts.

**Functional description.** Core loop: connect Neo wallet -> the app reads on-chain status (current streak, can-check-in flag, next UTC midnight, fee, reward pool) -> if the UTC window is open, tap the primary "Check In Now" button. Check-in is a single deposit-then-act tx: a GAS transfer of the check-in fee (default 0.001 GAS, shown as "Check-in fee: 0.001 GAS") with memo "miniapp-dailycheckin:checkin"; the contract records the streak and emits the new streak + any reward. On success a confetti burst fires and, if the check-in lands on a milestone day (7 or 14), a transient reward-reveal banner announces "+0.01/0.02 GAS — Day {n} milestone reached". Secondary actions in the same action card: "Claim Rewards ({amount} GAS)" — a direct contract call that pays accrued GAS to the user (disabled/relabeled "Nothing to claim yet" when unclaimed is 0, "Pool topping up" when the reward pool is short, "Check-in paused" when paused); and "Refresh Status" — re-reads chain state. When the window is already used the check-in button reads "Wait for Next" (disabled) and the hero badge shows "Checked in today!". The week strip shows D1–D7 dots filling as "{n}/7 DAYS" with a "{days} days to reward" / "Milestone reached" chip. A live UTC clock (HH:MM:SS UTC) and a "Next Check-in" countdown (HH:MM:SS, color-shifting green→amber→red as the UTC reset nears) gate timing. Honesty banners surface a paused contract or an under-funded reward pool (check-in still records the streak; milestone GAS becomes claimable once the pool is topped up). Stats shown: personal strip (Total Check-ins / Unclaimed / Total Claimed), milestone ladder (Day 7 +0.01 / Day 14 +0.02 with locked/checked icons), recent check-ins history list, and Global Stats (Total Check-ins, Total Users, Total Rewarded, Reward pool). A collapsible "Request and result evidence" section shows raw latest request/result JSON.

**Interface description.** Single full-width screen on a white page, standard Neo Soft green identity (brand #16c784, soft fill #e4f8f0, deep-green text). TOP TWO-COLUMN GRID. Left column = hero card (soft green-to-white gradient): a large accent-ringed circular badge holding the big streak number ("0"), beside it an eyebrow "DAILY CHECK-IN" (11px uppercase tracked), title "{n} Day Streak" (24-30px/800), subtitle "Earn GAS by checking in daily", and two fact lines "Next reward: Day 7 · +0.01 GAS" and "Check-in fee: 0.001 GAS"; top-right of the hero a green pill badge ("Connect to start your streak" / "Ready" / "Checked in today!") and a "Best: 0 Days" line. Below the hero, the week-progress block: a label row "THIS WEEK · 0/7 DAYS" with a right-aligned green chip ("7 days to reward"), a row of seven pill day-slots D1–D7 (filled green with a check when completed, today highlighted, others muted #f7f8fa with a circle icon), a thin progress connector bar, and a timing row "09:31:40 UTC" + a green countdown pill "14:28:20 NEXT CHECK-IN". Right column = action card (white, rounded, soft shadow): an intro hint line ("Connect your Neo wallet, then check in once each UTC day to start your streak."), the primary full-width "Check In Now" button (filled green #0a8050 when ready / muted secondary when waiting), then a secondary stack "Claim Rewards (0 GAS)" (soft-green disabled empty state) and "Refresh Status" (outline). BELOW THE GRID, full width: "TODAY PLAN" card (green-tinted) with subtitle and a 3-up panel grid — "DAILY WINDOW / Start your streak", "MILESTONE IMPACT / No instant GAS", "YOUR REWARDS / Nothing to claim" each with eyebrow + bold title + copy, plus a footer route line "Check-in deposits the GAS fee to the on-chain contract; rewards claim directly from it." Then a compact 3-cell personal meta strip (Total Check-ins / Unclaimed / Total Claimed, big tabular-nums figures, muted when zero). Then a two-column row: "MILESTONES" card (two tiles Day 7 +0.01 GAS / Day 14 +0.02 GAS with lock icons and "(cumulative total)") and "RECENT CHECK-INS" card (history rows or a muted "No check-ins yet" empty pill). Then "GLOBAL STATS" card — a 4-cell grid (Total Check-ins, Total Users, Total Rewarded, Reward pool). At the very bottom a collapsible "REQUEST AND RESULT EVIDENCE" disclosure with a chevron, revealing two JSON code panels (Latest Request / Latest Result).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / disconnected (empty)** — Wallet not connected. Hero shows streak 0, badge 'Connect to start your streak', Best 0 Days; action card shows the connect hint and a green-filled 'Check In Now' (disabled) over disabled 'Claim Rewards (0 GAS)' + 'Refresh Status'. Today Plan leads with 'Start your streak / No instant GAS / Nothing to claim'. Week strip all-empty 0/7, all meta + global figures muted zeros, history shows 'No check-ins yet'.
- **Ready to check in (window open)** — Connected, UTC window open, streak e.g. 3. Hero badge 'Ready' (green), streak ring glows/pulses, countdown ticking green. Primary 'Check In Now' is solid-green and enabled; Today Plan reads 'Check-in available / day 4 will be recorded'. Week dots D1–D3 filled, D4 highlighted as today-ready.
- **Submitting / loading** — Check-in tx in flight: 'Check In Now' shows spinner/loading; a status pill surfaces the workflow message ('Submitting check-in'). Refresh/claim disabled. Used also for the initial status read ('Loading…' badge/label only while a chain read is actually in flight).
- **Success — milestone reward reveal** — Check-in succeeded on a milestone day: confetti pieces animate across the screen and a transient green reward-reveal banner (gift icon, 'MILESTONE REACHED', '+0.01 GAS', 'Day 7 milestone reached — claim it from your rewards.') appears for a few seconds. Hero badge flips to 'Checked in today!', week dot fills, Claim button becomes active with the accrued amount.
- **Checked in today (window closed)** — Already checked in this UTC cycle. Hero badge 'Checked in today!', primary button reads 'Wait for Next' (disabled), Today Plan 'Secured today / streak protected until next UTC reset'. Countdown counts down to the next window.
- **Reward pool low / paused (honesty state)** — Top-right notice banner: under-funded pool ('reward pool is running low… milestone GAS claimable once topped up') or paused ('Daily check-in is temporarily paused…'). Claim button relabels to 'Pool topping up' or 'Check-in paused'; Reward pool stat tile shows a warning style.
- **Rewards claimable / claim flow** — Unclaimed > 0: 'Claim Rewards (0.01 GAS)' active (green), Today Plan 'Rewards claimable / {amount} available through the claim wallet action.' Claiming shows the claim button loading and a 'Claiming rewards' status pill.

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green: brand #16c784 (decorative ring, badges, soft chips), filled CTA #0a8050 with white text, soft fill #e4f8f0, deep-green text on light surfaces; white cards, #f7f8fa insets, hairline borders, rounded 12-24px, soft shadows, pill buttons/tabs. Signature visuals: the large accent-ringed circular STREAK NUMBER badge (the focal element, pulses/glows when check-in is available); the D1–D7 week-progress dot strip that fills with green checkmarks; the color-shifting NEXT CHECK-IN countdown pill (green→amber #fbbf24→red #ef4444 as UTC midnight nears); a confetti burst + a gift-icon milestone reward-reveal banner (green with a subtle amber accent) on a successful milestone check-in. Note: the app's own theme SCSS defines a 'sunrise' amber/orange palette, but the platform's Neo Soft green tokens override it in the rendered app — keep the green identity.

## Dice Game  `dice-game`
*Category: game*

> Pick a die face 1-6, stake GAS, and roll — your bet commits on-chain in one transaction and the next block's randomness reveals the roll, paying 5.70x on a match.

**Purpose.** Dice Game is an on-chain, provably-fair single-face dice bet. A user picks one face (1-6), stakes GAS, and rolls; on Neo N3 the bet commits and reveals across two blocks (next-block native randomness, no oracle, instant payout), while on Neo X it settles via a VRF callback. People open it for a fast, transparent bet where the outcome is fixed by an unpredictable later block, with the 5% house edge, 5.70x payout, and refund/withdraw safety all stated up front.

**Functional description.** Core flow: connect wallet (OneGate; chain auto-detected and shown as a "Neo N3" / "Neo X" badge) -> pick a face on the 1-6 pill grid (default 6) -> set the GAS stake via the number input or the preset chips (0.10 / 0.50 / 1.00 / 5.00 GAS; min 0.05, max network/bankroll-dependent, e.g. 20 GAS on N3, 2 on Neo X) -> the panel live-previews Face / Stake / Win payout (stake x 5.70) / Net win (payout minus stake) -> press the green "Roll" button. Roll commits the GAS-backed bet on-chain ("Bet placed — your wager is locked. Revealing on the next block…"); the central die animates to a "?" rolling state, then the next block's randomness reveals the rolled face. Result resolves to Won (face matched -> 5.70x payout to wallet, win-burst/fireworks), Lost ("No win this time"), or Refunded ("The roll couldn't settle, so your stake was returned"). Secondary actions: if reveal hasn't landed, a "Reveal pending" state offers a "Reveal result" button to re-call settle (permissionless, safe to retry on N3); if stake was sent but the bet didn't commit, a roll-credit banner ("You have {amount} GAS roll credit…") offers a "Withdraw" button to send unused credit back to wallet (N3 only). Presets above the effective cap are greyed out (would strand GAS). On-chain behaviour a designer must reflect: a status bar shows last status + "Payout: {x} GAS" + truncated last transaction hash; a "Recent rolls" history lists face / result / payout / txid per row.

**Interface description.** Desktop is a two-card top row inside a light app shell, then two full-width stacked panels below. LEFT card "Dice stage": eyebrow "DICE GAME DESK" with a green pill chain badge "Neo N3" (dot + label) on its right; large hero title "Choose a face and roll" (24-30px/800); subtitle "Pick the face in the transaction panel, confirm the GAS stake, and your bet commits on-chain — the next block reveals the roll." A big mint-tinted rounded stage holds a white 3D die cube rendering pips for the previewed/rolled face (ghosted pips in preview, solid when settled, "?" while rolling), with caption "You picked 6" (bold face) / "Rolled X" after settle. Below: a metric grid of small inset tiles — Chance "1 / 6", Platform fee "5%", Stake range "0.05-20 GAS" (plus House liquidity when known); an amber/soft house-edge note "House edge 5% — a match pays 5.7x of the 6x fair payout."; and a three-item rule strip (soft green chips): "Wallet signs the GAS-backed bet — the wager locks now." / "A strictly later block's randomness decides the roll on reveal." / "Reveal is permissionless; unused stake credit is withdrawable anytime." RIGHT card "Bet panel": heading row "SELECTED FACE" + "Roll"; a 2x2 bet summary grid (Face / Stake / Win payout / Net win with bold tabular figures); a row of six face pill buttons 1-6 (active = filled green, e.g. 6); a "Stake" number field showing 0.10 with helper "Potential payout: 0.57 GAS"; a row of four preset chips (0.10 / 0.50 / 1.00 / 5.00 GAS); the full-width green primary "Roll" button (#0a8050, white text); a trust paragraph (N3) explaining commit-then-reveal; an optional roll-credit banner with Withdraw; and a bottom status bar showing "Ready" + "Payout: 0.57 GAS" (+ last tx code). BELOW: a collapsible "SETTLEMENT ROUTE / Safety model" details panel (numbered 1 Commit bet, 2 Wait one block, 3 Reveal & pay + a House-model 5% risk note), and a "RECENT ROLLS / Payout" history panel with an empty state (robot mascot + "No local roll history yet.") or rows of face/result/payout/txid. Identity: Neo Soft light theme, white cards on #f7f8fa, green brand accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 CTA), rounded 16-24px cards, soft shadows; the signature visual is the pip-rendered die cube on a mint stage.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / ready (empty)** — Wallet connected, default face 6 selected and 0.10 GAS stake prefilled. Die cube shows ghosted preview pips for face 6, caption 'You picked 6', hero title 'Choose a face and roll'. Right panel status bar reads 'Ready' / 'Payout: 0.57 GAS'. Recent rolls panel shows the robot-mascot empty state 'No local roll history yet.'
- **Filled / ready to roll** — User has changed face and/or stake (e.g. face 3, stake 1.00 GAS via preset chip). Bet summary updates: Face 3, Stake 1.00 GAS, Win payout 5.70 GAS, Net win 4.70 GAS; helper shows 'Potential payout: 5.70 GAS'. Active face pill and matching preset chip highlighted green; Roll button enabled.
- **Rolling / submitting** — After Roll: button shows 'Submitting roll…' then the die cube enters its '?' rolling animation, hero title 'Revealing the roll…' with body 'Your wager is locked. The roll reveals on the next block — this usually takes about 15 seconds.' Result strip shows a spinner + 'Rolling…'.
- **Reveal pending** — Bet committed but reveal not yet landed. Hero title 'Reveal pending — your wager is locked and the result is not confirmed yet.' with reassurance line 'The chain reveals your roll on the next block — your stake is safe and fully refundable.' Result strip offers a 'Reveal result' button to re-call settle.
- **Success / win result** — Die cube shows the settled face with solid pips and a win-burst (14 confetti shards) + fireworks. Hero title 'Won', body 'Your face matched the roll — payout sent to your wallet.' Result strip 'Rolled X' + 'Won'. Status bar shows payout and last tx; a new row appears in Recent rolls (win-styled).
- **Lost result** — Settled die shows the non-matching face, hero title 'Lost', body 'The roll didn't match your face. Try another roll.' Result strip 'Rolled X' + 'Lost'; Recent rolls gains a lost-styled row.
- **Roll-credit / refund banner** — Stake sent but bet didn't commit, or refunded: a status banner 'You have {amount} GAS roll credit — it funds your next roll automatically, or press Withdraw to send it back to your wallet.' with a 'Withdraw' button (N3). Refunded result body 'The roll couldn't settle, so your stake was returned.'
- **Error / invalid stake** — Invalid or out-of-range stake: field shows aria-invalid red state with helper 'Enter a GAS stake of at least 0.05, using up to 8 decimals. · Max on this network N GAS'; presets above the cap are greyed/disabled; status bar switches to error styling with the form error message; Roll button disabled.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand accent: #16c784 for decorative chips/badges/icons, #0a8050 for the filled "Roll" CTA (white text), soft #e4f8f0 green fills, and an amber/soft house-edge note tile. Signature visual is the white 3D die cube with rendered pips (ghosted in preview, solid when settled, "?" while rolling) sitting on a mint-tinted rounded stage, plus a green pill chain badge ("Neo N3" / "Neo X") and a win-burst confetti/fireworks animation on a win.

## FogPlay  `fogplay`
*Category: game*

> A provably-fair on-chain coin toss: pick Heads or Tails, wager GAS, flip, and win 2x with no house edge.

**Purpose.** FogPlay is a single-player on-chain coin-flip betting game. A user picks Heads or Tails, sets a GAS wager, and flips for a 50/50 chance to win 2x their stake with no house edge. It uses a commit/reveal mechanism (bet escrowed first, outcome revealed from a later block's randomness) so results are provably fair and cannot be peeked or aborted. Users open it for a quick, verifiable gambling-style flutter.

**Functional description.** Core loop (commit/reveal): 1) Pick a side — tap a Heads or Tails choice card (two-card selector, selected card gets a green border + filled green icon + checkmark badge). 2) Set wager — tap a preset chip (1 / 5 / 10 / 50 GAS) or type a custom amount in the "Custom Bet Amount" field (suffix "GAS", min 0.05, max 100, max 8 decimals). The live "House can currently pay up to {max} GAS" hint shows the bankroll cap; presets the house cannot pay 2x are greyed/disabled. 3) Flip — press the full-width green "Flip Coin" CTA. This first PLACES the bet ("Placing bet…"), the bet is escrowed + house exposure reserved, then the coin keeps spinning while waiting for the next block ("Revealing next block…"), then the outcome reveals and the winner is auto-paid 2x. A pending-reveal banner reassures "Bet placed — revealing on the next block…" / "Your bet is locked on-chain — the result reveals on the next block." If the reveal stalls, the banner switches to "Bet placed but the reveal hasn't landed yet — tap to reveal." with a "Reveal result" retry button. Result shows a coin face + win/loss banner ("You Won!" / "You Lost") and, on a win, a celebratory full-screen overlay ("You won the flip", +amount GAS, "Tap to continue") with fireworks. Secondary actions: a "Prepaid credit" chip with a "Withdraw" button appears if a wager was prepaid but the bet didn't commit/settle, letting the user recover or reuse stranded GAS. On-chain: direct GAS transfer commits the bet; permissionless settle reveals from block randomness; outcomes recorded on-chain for audit. Each completed round appends to "Recent Games" (Pick / Result / Bet / Payout columns). Tabs: Play, History, Stats. Wallet connection required.

**Interface description.** Single-column stack of rounded white cards on a light canvas (the app renders centered ~760px wide; redesign can use a hero + 2-column workspace at desktop widths). TOP HERO CARD: green circular coin icon badge, eyebrow "ON-CHAIN COIN TOSS" (11px uppercase muted), title "FogPlay" (~24px/800), subtitle "Provably-fair on-chain coin toss with a 2x payout". Below it, a soft mint inset panel = first-run prompt: small coin icon + "Flip to play your first round" (bold) + "Pick a side, set your wager, and flip — 50/50 odds, pays 2x." Once games exist this panel is replaced by a 4-cell stats row (Wins / Losses / Total Games / Total Earnings, divided by hairline rules, win count green, total earnings green). ARENA CARD: large centered animated coin (gradient mint sphere with the Neo "N" hexagon logo + "HEADS"/"TAILS" label), concentric decorative rings + ambient glow behind it; a status pill below the coin ("Place Your Bet" with a green dot, or "Placing bet…" / "Revealing next block…" while spinning). Beneath the coin a bet-preview strip in a white inset: "YOU PICKED / Heads" | "2X PAYOUT / 2.00 GAS" two-cell layout with a divider, and a small caption "50% chance · pays 2x · no house edge". WAGER CARD (NeoCard): step label "HEADS / TAILS" over a 2-column choice grid (Heads card selected = green outline, filled green icon circle, check badge top-right; Tails inactive = grey #f7f8fa fill, hairline border, ghost icon). Then "WAGER AMOUNT" label with a green soft-fill pill on the right reading "0.05 - 100 GAS"; a 4-up row of preset chips (1 / 5 / 10 / 50, each "amount" big + "GAS" small under it; selected = filled green with white text; disabled/unpayable = dimmed). "Custom Bet Amount" labeled number input with "GAS" suffix. Muted hint "House can currently pay up to 100.00 GAS". Full-width pill "Flip Coin" CTA in deep filled green (#0a8050) with white text. RECENT GAMES SECTION: eyebrow "RECENT GAMES" then an erobo card; empty = centered clock line-icon + "No games played yet. Place your first bet."; populated = a 4-col table (Pick / Result / Bet / Payout) with choice/outcome badges, win rows tinted, payouts "+x.xx" green on win / "0.00" on loss. Identity: Neo Soft light theme, bright green #16c784 decorative + #0a8050 filled CTA, mint soft fills #e4f8f0, white cards, hairline borders, soft shadows, pill chips.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — No games yet. Hero with mint first-run prompt panel ('Flip to play your first round' + hint), arena coin showing default 'HEADS' face with 'Place Your Bet' status pill and the YOU PICKED / 2X PAYOUT preview strip, wager card with Heads pre-selected + '1 GAS' preset, and a Recent Games card showing the empty clock-icon state 'No games played yet. Place your first bet.'
- **Filled / ready to flip** — User has picked a side and set a wager. Choice grid shows the selected card highlighted (green border + check), a wager preset chip active (e.g. '5 GAS') or custom amount entered, the bet-preview strip updated (e.g. 'YOU PICKED Tails' / '2X PAYOUT 10.00 GAS'), and the green 'Flip Coin' CTA enabled.
- **Placing bet (committing)** — After tapping Flip Coin: coin animates spinning, status pill reads 'Placing bet…' with active dot, CTA shows loading/disabled state. Bet is being escrowed on-chain.
- **Revealing (pending next block)** — Bet committed, awaiting reveal: coin keeps spinning, status pill 'Revealing next block…', and a reveal-pending banner 'Bet placed — revealing on the next block…' with reassurance 'Your bet is locked on-chain — the result reveals on the next block.'
- **Reveal stalled (manual retry)** — Reveal didn't land: pending banner switches to warning copy 'Bet placed but the reveal hasn't landed yet — tap to reveal.' with a 'Reveal result' retry button. Bet is safe on-chain.
- **Win result + celebration overlay** — Coin lands on the winning face, green 'You Won!' result banner with outcome tag, and a full-screen cinematic overlay: 'You won the flip', a glowing reward circle showing '+{amount} GAS', label 'You won the flip', and 'Tap to continue'. Fireworks active. Stats row + Recent Games updated with a green win row.
- **Loss result** — Coin lands on the losing face, neutral/red 'You Lost' banner with outcome tag; overlay variant shows 'Unlucky' / 'Bet was lost' / '0 GAS' / 'Tap to continue'. Recent Games adds a loss row with '0.00' payout.
- **Prepaid-credit recovery** — Wager prepaid but the bet didn't commit/settle: a 'Prepaid credit' chip appears above the CTA showing the held GAS amount with a 'Withdraw' button to recover or reuse it.
- **Bankroll-capped / validation** — House bankroll too low for some presets: oversized preset chips dimmed/disabled with tooltip, cap hint 'House can currently pay up to {max}', and inline input errors (e.g. 'Minimum bet is 0.05 GAS', 'Maximum bet is 100 GAS', 'Enter a valid number').
- **Recent Games table (populated)** — History card with a 4-column table — Pick / Result / Bet / Payout — choice and outcome badges, win rows tinted green, payouts shown as '+x.xx' (green) on wins and '0.00' on losses.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand identity. Bright green #16c784 for decorative elements (icon badges, soft chips, selected-card icon fill, win highlights), deep #0a8050 for the filled 'Flip Coin' CTA (white text), mint soft fills #e4f8f0 for inset panels and the 'Place Your Bet' / range pills. Signature visual = the animated 3D coin: a gradient mint sphere stamped with the Neo 'N' hexagon logo, surrounded by concentric decorative rings + ambient glow, that spins through the commit/reveal wait and lands on a Heads/Tails face. Heads = hexagon glyph, Tails = a coin/currency glyph. Win moment adds a glowing reward-circle overlay with fireworks.

## GasBox  `gasbox`
*Category: game*

> An on-chain gacha (blind-box) marketplace where players bet GAS to pull weighted prizes from escrowed capsule machines, and creators publish and fund their own machines.

**Purpose.** GasBox is a gachapon/blind-box marketplace on Neo. Players browse capsule machines, inspect transparent on-chain odds and prize inventory, then pay GAS to place a bet and reveal a weighted prize on the next block via commit-reveal RNG (provably unpredictable, un-cancellable). Creators open Studio to publish their own machines: name them, set price-per-play, define weighted prize items, fund the prize pool, and later top up, activate/deactivate, and withdraw accrued revenue. Users open it to gamble for escrowed on-chain prizes or to run a machine and earn play revenue.

**Functional description.** Primary player flow: wallet auto-connects (walletRequired) → browse the "Select a Machine" grid → click a machine card → a draw console opens showing the machine name/description/tags, a readiness banner (Ready for pull / Pull unavailable), and the big "Pull" lever button stamped with the cost (e.g. "Pull · 1.0 GAS"). The pull is a two-step commit-reveal: pressing Pull triggers "Placing bet…" → "Bet placed — revealing on the next block…" → "Revealing your prize…"; the lever has a pulled/spinning animation (~600ms). If settle times out, a "Draw in progress" pending panel appears with a spinner, the copy "Your bet is escrowed on-chain…", and a permissionless "Reveal result" retry button ("Safe to retry — the reveal is permissionless and pays exactly once."). On success a full-screen result overlay fires confetti and a rarity gem, showing "Congratulations!", the rarity word (LEGENDARY/EPIC/RARE/COMMON), the won item name, optional description, the prize amount, the note "The prize was drawn from a later block's randomness… This is a real on-chain prize, not a simulation.", and a "Dismiss" button. Below the lever the draw console also has: a "Prepaid credit" status chip (when the player has held credit reused on next pull), a "Pull decision" strip with four cells — Pull Cost (GAS), Inventory & Odds (e.g. "3/4 items" + ready/needs-funding note), Prize focus (top prize name), Readable odds (coverage %) — a three-row pull checklist (Machine active / Prize inventory / Odds readable, each Passed or Needs action), an optional unavailable-inventory note, and an expandable "Drop Rates" section with per-item rarity bars (gem icon + name + colored fill bar + %). Creator flow: from the market header or in-console "Open Studio" → Studio card with fields Machine Name (placeholder "e.g. Cyber Dragon Box"), Price per Play (GAS), Prize Asset selector (GAS/NEO) → a repeatable Inventory & Odds item list, each row: Item Name, Weight (positive integer), a live derived Tier preview (rarity is computed from weight share, not chosen — shows e.g. "RARE 22%"), and Prize per win ({asset}); Add Item / Remove item N controls; a running "Total Weight: N"; the warning "Pool deposits are non-recoverable…"; then a block primary "Create Machine" / "Publishing machine…" button. Creator-only controls appear in the console when the connected wallet owns the machine: a "Creator earnings" panel with accrued revenue + "Withdraw Revenue"; and "Machine controls" with "Prize pool / max prize" balance, a Top up amount ({asset}) input + "Top up pool", and an Activate/Deactivate machine toggle. Empty market state offers "Refresh Machines" and an "Open Studio" link. Secondary refresh + Open Studio actions sit under the lever.

**Interface description.** Light theme, single bright-green brand accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 filled CTAs). TOP HERO BAND (rounded ~24px card with a faint green wash on the right): left side has a small green line-icon badge (a gachapon-capsule "GachaMark"), an 11px uppercase eyebrow "ON-CHAIN BLIND BOXES WITH ESCROWED PRIZES", the title "GasBox" (~28px/800), a green status line with a dot reading "Live market status" (or "Ready to play" / "Publishing machine..."), and the subtitle "Prizes are escrowed before activation, odds remain inspectable, and pulls require the selected machine to be active." Right side: three boxed metric tiles MACHINES (figure, tabular, "—" when none) · YOUR PULLS (0) · EST. PLAYS (0, tooltip "Estimated from accrued revenue ÷ price"). MARKET SECTION below: a section header with eyebrow "MARKET" + bold "Select a Machine" on the left and a pill "Create Machine" primary button on the right, all inside a large inset (#f7f8fa) rounded panel. When empty it shows a centered teaser: a green capsule mark with a small lock badge, a row of four rarity gem chips (gold star / purple / blue / muted gem) each showing "?", the eyebrow "NO ACTIVE MACHINES LOADED", bold "Active capsule machines land here. Refresh to pull one and win an escrowed on-chain prize.", a filled green "Refresh Machines" button, and the line "Want to run your own machine? Open Studio" (green text link). When populated it becomes a responsive grid of machine cards: each card has a circular green icon badge (prize-capsule or gacha mark), machine name, optional description, cost "X GAS", and a meta row "N items · M plays"; the selected card gets a green ring, inactive cards dim. The draw console and Studio render as white rounded NeoCards (radius 16-20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,0.06)) stacked below. Components: pill buttons/tabs, hairline borders rgba(20,22,38,0.06), inset #f7f8fa form fields, status banners (green is-ready / amber is-blocked), checklist rows with Passed/Needs-action status, horizontal rarity bars, and a modal result overlay with confetti. Rarity colors: legendary gold/star, epic purple, rare blue, common muted — used on gems, bar fills, and the result card.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Market — empty / first-run** — Hero band (badge, eyebrow, GasBox title, green 'Live market status' dot, escrow subtitle, MACHINES '—'/YOUR PULLS 0/EST. PLAYS 0 tiles) above the MARKET panel showing the centered locked-capsule teaser: green capsule mark + lock, four '?' rarity gem chips, 'NO ACTIVE MACHINES LOADED', the 'Active capsule machines land here…' line, a green 'Refresh Machines' button, and the 'Want to run your own machine? Open Studio' link. 'Create Machine' pill top-right.
- **Market — machines loaded** — Same hero (with real MACHINES count) over a responsive grid of machine cards: circular green icon badge, name, description, 'X GAS' cost, 'N items · M plays' meta. Hover lift; one card selected with a green ring; inactive cards dimmed.
- **Draw console — ready** — Selected-machine NeoCard: icon + name + description + category/tag chips, a green 'Ready for pull' readiness banner, the large block 'Pull' lever button stamped with cost ('Pull · 1.0 GAS'), secondary 'Refresh Machines' + ghost 'Open Studio' row, the four-cell 'Pull decision' strip (Pull Cost / Inventory & Odds 3/4 items / Prize focus / Readable odds %), the three-row pull checklist (all Passed), and the expandable 'Drop Rates' rarity bars.
- **Draw console — blocked** — Same console but amber 'Pull unavailable' banner with copy explaining the machine is inactive or inventory isn't escrow-ready; Pull button disabled; checklist rows show Needs action; if the viewer is the creator, the inline 'Machine controls' (pool balance, Top up amount + button, Activate machine) and 'Creator earnings' panels are visible.
- **Pull in progress / reveal-retry** — Lever animating with phase label 'Placing bet…' → 'Bet placed — revealing on the next block…' → 'Revealing your prize…'; below it the 'Draw in progress' pending panel with spinner, the escrow explanation copy, and a 'Reveal result' retry button + 'Safe to retry…' hint.
- **Win result overlay** — Full-screen dim modal with confetti and a rarity gem burst, tinted to the won tier: 'Congratulations!', the rarity word (LEGENDARY/EPIC/RARE/COMMON), the won item name, optional description, prize amount, the 'real on-chain prize, not a simulation' note, and a block green 'Dismiss' button.
- **Studio — create machine** — Studio NeoCard: header 'Studio / Design your machine' + 'Back to Market'; fields Machine Name (placeholder 'e.g. Cyber Dragon Box'), Price per Play (GAS), Prize Asset (GAS/NEO) selector; an Inventory & Odds repeater with 'Total Weight: N', each item row = Item Name + Weight + live derived Tier preview ('RARE 22%') + Prize per win ({asset}), plus Add Item / Remove item N; the non-recoverable-pool warning; an inline error line on validation; and a block 'Create Machine' / 'Publishing machine…' button.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default single bright-green brand accent (#16c784 decorative badges/chips/icon fills, #0a8050 filled CTAs, soft #e4f8f0 fills). Signature visual = the gachapon/blind-box motif: a green line-icon capsule machine ("GachaMark") and dispensed prize capsule ("PrizeMark") used as hero badge, card icons, and empty-state teaser; plus rarity gems/stars in tier colors (gold/star legendary, purple epic, blue rare, muted common) appearing on chips, drop-rate bars, and the confetti-celebrated result card. The capsule-machine lever (big Pull button) and the locked '?' capsule teaser are the defining identity elements.

## Last Survivor  `last-survivor`
*Category: game*

> A winner-takes-all GAS pot where every key you buy resets a 24-hour countdown — if it ever hits zero, the last buyer wins the entire pool.

**Purpose.** Last Survivor is an on-chain game of nerves inspired by Reddit's "The Button": a single growing GAS prize pool sits behind a live countdown that any player can reset by buying keys. Each key adds 0.10+ GAS to the pot and extends the clock by 30 seconds (capped at 24h); if the timer ever reaches zero with no new buy, whoever bought last wins the whole pot on-chain with no platform fee. A user opens it to watch the tension meter climb, gamble on being the final buyer, and chase the entire pool. Key purchases are non-refundable and key share is NOT a claim on the pot — only the last buyer wins everything.

**Functional description.** Core loop: (1) Connect Neo wallet (walletRequired). (2) If round state is unavailable, a top "Round state unavailable" banner with a "Refresh Round" button must be cleared first — Buy Keys stays disabled until refreshed. (3) Pick a key quantity in the Buy Keys card: a −/+ stepper showing "1 KEYS", plus preset chips 1 / 3 / 5 / 10. Changing the count live-updates the "ESTIMATED COST" row (e.g. "⛽ 0.10 GAS"; base price 0.1 GAS/key, increases with demand via a bonding curve). (4) Press the full-width green "Buy Keys" CTA → deposits GAS, becomes the current leader, and resets/extends the countdown; button shows "Buying…" while submitting. Each buy adds GAS to the pot and adds 30s to the clock. (5) The hero countdown ring ticks down and the danger meter rises SAFE → ELEVATED → HIGH ALERT → CRITICAL as time nears zero. A "Current leader" badge shows the leading address with "Wins if the timer hits zero". (6) When the clock expires on-chain the round is "Rollover ready" and needs a permissionless settle: a payout card reveals the winner + exact pot, and ANY user can press "Settle Round" (shows "Settling…") to pay the last buyer the entire pot and open a fresh round. If the connected wallet IS the winner, this card becomes a personal "You won the pot! / You take the entire pot" celebration with fireworks. (7) Recovery: if a deposit landed but its buy didn't complete, a "Prepaid credit" card offers "Withdraw credit" to reclaim unused GAS. Secondary: a "Your keys / Total keys / Key share" participation strip, a collapsible "How It Works" 3-step explainer, and a "Recent Rounds" history list of past winners.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout (not a 2-col workspace), white cards on light ground, green Neo Soft accent. Top: optional amber-bordered "Round state unavailable" notice banner — bold title + body copy on the left, outlined "Refresh Round" pill button on the right. HERO card: top-left cluster = circular green icon badge (stopwatch glyph) + eyebrow "ROUND STATUS" + a facts line "#0 · ○ Rollover ready" (round number, dot separator, status dot + status text). Centered below: a large circular COUNTDOWN RING (soft green concentric ring with a small green dot at 12 o'clock) containing big tabular-nums monospace digits "00:00:00" and a 2-line uppercase caption ("BE THE FIRST TO BUY A KEY" when idle, else "TIME UNTIL EVENT"). Under the ring: a small inset "TOTAL POT" tile with figure "0.00" + "GAS". Bottom of hero: the DANGER METER — a horizontal gradient track (green→amber→red) with left label "SAFE", right label "CRITICAL", a centered status pill ("ROUND NOT STARTED" idle, or SAFE/ELEVATED/HIGH ALERT/CRITICAL), and a draggable-looking glow knob at the fill position. BUY KEYS card: eyebrow "BUY KEYS"; centered −/+ stepper with a boxed count "1 / KEYS"; preset chips 1·3·5·10 (active chip = green soft fill + green text); a full-width inset "ESTIMATED COST" row with right-aligned "⛽ 0.10 GAS"; helper line ("Key purchase opens once the round state above is refreshed." / "Base price: 0.1 GAS per key…"); full-width primary CTA "Buy Keys" (disabled = dashed outline ghost; enabled = solid green #0a8050 white text); risk note "Key purchases are non-refundable and added to the pot. If another player buys after you, you forfeit your spend — only the final last buyer wins." PARTICIPATION strip: 3 items separated by thin dividers, each = small green circle icon + label + value: "YOUR KEYS 0", "TOTAL KEYS 0", "KEY SHARE —", followed by the share-hint line "Participation only — your key share does not entitle you to the pot. Only the last buyer wins everything." Collapsible "HOW IT WORKS" card (chevron) revealing 3 numbered rules (Buy keys / Each key extends the clock / Last buyer wins). "RECENT ROUNDS" card with a refresh-arrow icon header; empty = friendly robot mascot + "No events yet".

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Round unavailable / first paint** — Idle/degraded state from the screenshot: amber 'Round state unavailable' banner + 'Refresh Round' button on top; hero ring shows calm green breathing 00:00:00 with 'BE THE FIRST TO BUY A KEY', TOTAL POT 0.00 GAS, danger meter pill 'ROUND NOT STARTED' at zero fill; Buy Keys card with dashed-outline disabled 'Buy Keys' and helper 'Key purchase opens once the round state above is refreshed.'; participation all 0/—; Recent Rounds empty with robot mascot.
- **Live round — ready to buy** — Refreshed active round: status '#N · ● Active' (green dot), countdown ring ticking with 'TIME UNTIL EVENT', real TOTAL POT figure, danger meter at SAFE/ELEVATED with colored fill + knob, a 'Current leader' star badge (address + 'Wins if the timer hits zero'), enabled solid-green 'Buy Keys' CTA, populated key share %.
- **Selecting keys / submitting** — Buy Keys interaction: preset chip (e.g. 3) active green, stepper showing '3 KEYS', ESTIMATED COST updated to the multi-key bonding-curve price; CTA in loading 'Buying…' state, inputs/chips disabled.
- **Critical countdown** — High-tension state: ring re-tinted amber/red and pulsing, countdown low (e.g. 00:00:12), danger meter pill 'CRITICAL'/'HIGH ALERT' with near-full red fill and glow knob at the right end — peak urgency.
- **Rollover ready — settle / payout** — Round ended on-chain: 'Rollover ready' status; trophy payout card showing eyebrow 'Winner declared', large pot amount + 'GAS', winner address, hint 'Anyone can settle: the last buyer is paid the entire pot on-chain and a fresh round begins.', full-width primary 'Settle Round' (→ 'Settling…').
- **You won (winner celebration)** — Connected wallet is the last buyer: trophy card switches to 'You won the pot!' eyebrow, big pot figure, 'You take the entire pot', hint 'Settle the round to claim your winnings on-chain', fireworks; 'Settle Round' CTA to claim.
- **Prepaid credit recovery** — A deposit landed but buy didn't finish: recovery card 'Prepaid credit · X GAS' with hint 'Unused GAS from a buy that didn't complete. Reused on your next buy, or withdraw it now.' and a 'Withdraw credit' button.
- **History populated** — Recent Rounds list of past winners: rows of 'Winner declared' with detail line '#round • 0xabc…1234 • X.XX GAS' instead of the empty mascot state.

**Visual identity.** Green Neo Soft default accent (#16c784 decorative ring/badges/chips, #0a8050 filled CTA, soft #e4f8f0 fills). Signature visuals: a circular COUNTDOWN RING with monospace tabular countdown digits and a 12-o'clock progress dot; a horizontal SAFE→CRITICAL DANGER METER with a glowing knob and green→amber→red gradient; a stopwatch hero icon badge. Tension escalation re-tints ring + meter through green/amber/red as the clock nears zero (red/amber strictly reserved for a genuinely live round — idle stays calm green with a slow breathing glow). Win moment = fireworks + trophy payout card. Despite the "skull" manifest icon, the live UI is calm-green, not morbid.

## On-Chain Tarot  `on-chain-tarot`
*Category: game*

> Ask a question, pay 0.1 GAS, and reveal a Past / Present / Future tarot spread drawn on-chain by Neo N3's Runtime.GetRandom — verifiable and auditable.

**Purpose.** On-Chain Tarot is a blockchain-powered divination game that turns a three-card tarot reading into a provably-fair on-chain event. The user opens it to ask a focused question and get a Past / Present / Future spread where the three cards are picked by the smart contract itself (not the app), so the result can be audited from the ReadingDrawn event. It exists to make a normally "trust-me" mystical experience transparent: every draw is a wallet-reviewed 0.1 GAS transfer plus a draw() call, and the 78-card outcome is authoritative and replayable.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (single screen, no tab switching needed for play; the manifest defines a "Draw Your Cards" game tab and a "Stats" tab): 1) Connect wallet (walletRequired). 2) Type a focused question into the "Question prompt" textarea (capped at 200 chars, placeholder "Ask a question..."; the question is kept on-device, never stored on-chain). 3) Press the single primary CTA "Draw 3 Cards" (subhint: "Pay 0.1 GAS to draw your Past · Present · Future cards on-chain."). On click it becomes a loading "Drawing cards..." state. Under the hood: if the wallet has no prepaid credit, it first sends a 0.1 GAS transfer (memo miniapp-tarot:draw) which the wallet shows for approval, then calls draw() — the contract picks 3 distinct cards via Runtime.GetRandom in the same tx. 4) The three card slots (Past, Present, Future) populate face-down; each is a tappable button — "Tap to reveal" flips it (520ms 3D rotateY flip) to show the card art, name, and keywords. The CTA swaps to a secondary "Draw Again". 5) When all three are revealed, a "Your Reading" summary card eases in (fade + scale) listing each position with its card thumbnail, name, and keywords, plus a "Copy reading" secondary button (copies "Past: X · Present: Y · Future: Z"). Secondary/edge actions a designer must reflect: an "On-chain verified" banner appears after a successful draw; a "Prepaid credit" recovery card appears in the sidebar ONLY when unused credit exists (a deposit landed but draw didn't finish) — it shows the GAS amount and a "Withdraw credit" button to reclaim it. On-chain results: readings counter and cards-drawn counter (readings × 3) update from authoritative contract state after each draw.

**Interface description.** Light "Neo Soft" layout on a #f7f8fb page, max width ~1420px, as a 2-column grid: a wide main column (~1fr) and a narrow right sidebar (~0.34fr, min 280px). TOP: a full-width hero band (rounded 20px, green radial-gradient wash over white, hairline green border, soft shadow, faint twinkling-constellation sparkle). Hero contains a floating 48px white circular badge with a green outline 5-point STAR icon, an eyebrow "NEO N3 SHARE-READY READING" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em, green), the title "On-Chain Tarot Reading Desk" (28px/800, -0.02em ink #1e1e2e), a body subtitle, and a row of pill chips: "Draw fee · 0.1 GAS on-chain", "Contract route · transfer -> draw()", and a state chip ("Ready" idle, or a filled soft-green "0/3 revealed" progress chip after drawing). BELOW the hero, the main column holds a 2-column workspace (left ~0.42fr / right ~0.58fr, collapses to 1 column under 1080px): LEFT = "Your question" card (NeoCard, radius 20px) with section heading (uppercase muted label + right-aligned status "Ready" / "On-chain verified"), an uppercase "QUESTION PROMPT" field label, a large inset #f7f8fa textarea (min 132px, green focus ring), then the green primary CTA "Draw 3 Cards" with centered muted subhint (after drawing: a secondary "Draw Again"). RIGHT = "Three-card spread" card with heading status ("Awaiting draw" / "Tap to reveal" / "All Revealed") and a 3-up grid of card slots — each slot has a soft-green pill position label (PAST / PRESENT / FUTURE), a 744:1040 portrait card frame (dark mystical face #1a0b2e, rounded 14px, deep shadow) showing the card-back art with a "Tap to reveal" pill until flipped, and a caption (card name + keywords, or "Sealed card / Sealed" when face-down); empty slots show a "—" placeholder and a centered "Submit a question first" hint. SIDEBAR (right): a 2-up metric grid (white tiles, green 28px/800 tabular figures) for "Readings" and "Cards Drawn"; a "Transaction safety" NeoCard with a paragraph and a 3-item green-check bullet list ("0.1 GAS draw fee is shown in the wallet before you approve." / "Cards are picked on-chain via Runtime.GetRandom — not by this app." / "The result is recorded in the ReadingDrawn event for auditing."); and the conditional "Prepaid credit" recovery card. Below the workspace: an "On-chain verified" soft-green banner and the "Your Reading" summary card with per-position thumbnail rows. Identity is a single green accent hue (brand #0fb174 / deep #0b5e3e text / #0a8050 CTA fill) against dark, mystical card faces that supply the divination mood.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (idle)** — Wallet connected, no question yet. Hero shows star badge + 'On-Chain Tarot Reading Desk' + chips ending in a 'Ready' state chip. Left 'Your question' card has empty textarea (placeholder 'Ask a question...') and green 'Draw 3 Cards' CTA with subhint about paying 0.1 GAS. Right 'Three-card spread' shows three empty PAST/PRESENT/FUTURE slots with '—' placeholders and the 'Submit a question first' hint. Sidebar metrics read 0 Readings / 0 Cards Drawn; Transaction safety bullets visible.
- **Ready (question typed)** — Same layout with the textarea filled with the user's question; section status still 'Ready'; CTA enabled and emphasized. Spread still face-down/empty awaiting the draw.
- **Loading / submitting** — After pressing draw: CTA shows spinner + 'Drawing cards...' and is disabled; wallet-approval moment for the 0.1 GAS transfer + draw() is in flight. Spread still awaiting cards.
- **Drawn, face-down (reveal in progress)** — Three card slots populated face-down with card-back art + 'Tap to reveal' pills; hero state chip becomes a filled soft-green progress chip e.g. '0/3 revealed' then '1/3', '2/3'; spread heading reads 'Tap to reveal'; CTA swapped to secondary 'Draw Again'. Show the 3D mid-flip state on one card.
- **Success / full reading** — All three cards flipped showing Neo-styled art, names, and keywords; spread heading 'All Revealed'; an 'On-chain verified' soft-green banner; the 'Your Reading' summary card eased in with three thumbnail rows (Past/Present/Future + card name + keywords) and a 'Copy reading' button; sidebar counters incremented.
- **Prepaid-credit recovery** — Sidebar shows the conditional 'Prepaid credit' card (a deposit landed but draw didn't complete): heading 'Prepaid credit' with the GAS amount on the right, hint about reuse-or-withdraw, and a secondary 'Withdraw credit' button.
- **Error / wrong chain** — chainWarning state: a 'Wrong Chain' message ('This app requires Neo N3. Please switch networks.') or a draw-failure notice ('Draw fee prepaid, but the reading did not complete. The credit is held on the contract and will be reused on your next draw.'). Designer should mock an inline error/notice treatment consistent with the safety panel.

**Visual identity.** Single coherent green accent (Neo Soft default) — brand #0fb174 for badges/pills/icon fills, soft-green #e4f8f0 chip/banner fills, deep #0b5e3e for green text on light, #0a8050 (hover #086a45) for the filled CTA. The mystical mood comes from contrast: dark indigo-black tarot card faces (#1a0b2e) with Neo shield/oracle-circuit motifs against the clean light UI. Signature visuals: the floating circular green STAR badge in the hero, a drifting twinkling-constellation sparkle over the hero, the 3D card-flip (rotateY 520ms) reveal of the Past/Present/Future spread, and the soft-green "0/3 revealed" progress chip. Portrait cards are 744:1040; 78-card Neo-styled deck.

## Unbreakable Vault  `unbreakable-vault`
*Category: game*

> Lock a GAS bounty behind a secret hash and dare anyone to crack it — every failed attempt grows the prize, the right secret wins the pot.

**Purpose.** .

**Functional description.** Two parallel flows, exposed as the "Create" and "Break" tabs and laid out side-by-side. CREATE: enter a Vault Title (required), an optional Description ("Optional hints or lore"), a Bounty in GAS (minimum 1, "Minimum 1" placeholder), pick a Difficulty from a dropdown that bundles the per-attempt fee challengers will pay — "Easy · 0.1 GAS", "Medium · 0.5 GAS", "Hard · 1 GAS" — then type a Vault Secret and Confirm Secret (two side-by-side password fields; a mismatch shows the inline error "Secrets do not match" and blocks submit). A collapsible "Fees & secret details" disclosure holds three notes (secret is hashed locally / difficulty sets the attempt fee / 2% fee on a successful break). A live inline hint above the CTA names the next missing step: "Add a vault title to continue." → "Enter a bounty of at least 1 GAS." → "Enter and confirm a secret phrase." → ready state "Ready — secret hashed locally before submit." The primary button reads "Create Vault (bounty + hash)" (→ "Creating vault..." while submitting); on success a green confirmation banner shows "VAULT CREATED" with the new "#<id>". BREAK: type a Vault ID and press "Load Vault" (secondary button). Loaded details render as a stack of label/value rows BEFORE any payment: the vault title + public hint, a Status pill (Active/Broken/Expired/Claimable), Bounty (accent green GAS), a growth note ("Each failed attempt adds its fee to this bounty..."), "You win (after 2% fee)" net payout (accent green), Difficulty, Attempt Fee, Attempts count, and Days Left (when active/claimable). For an active vault: a "Break Secret" password field, a red/danger "Attempt Break" button, the warning note "The attempt fee is charged on every try and is non-refundable...", and a bordered top-up sub-row ("Increase Bounty (GAS)" input + secondary "Add Bounty" button — anyone can grow an active bounty). When a vault is expired/reclaimable the creator sees a green "Reclaim Vault" button; a broken vault shows "This vault is broken — the bounty was paid to the winner." On mainnet a green note explains the deposit + contract call are batched by the host operation panel. On-chain behaviour a designer should reflect: bounties are real GAS, the secret never leaves the device (only its hash), failed attempts visibly increase the bounty, and the winner gets bounty × 0.98.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling column, ~1365px desktop, on the platform's light "Neo Soft" canvas. TOP: a hero band — white-to-soft-mint gradient card (radius 20px, soft shadow) with a 52px mint-soft rounded-square badge holding a green padlock icon, then stacked text: eyebrow "HASH-LOCKED SECURITY" (11px/700 uppercase, green, .12em tracking), title "Unbreakable Vault" (24px/800, -0.02em, ink #1e1e2e), subtitle "Hacker bounty vaults secured by on-chain hashes" (13px, #5b6478). BELOW HERO: a 2-column stat-tile row — each tile is a subtle #f7f8fa rounded card (radius 16px) with a mint-soft square badge holding a big tabular green number (22px/800) + uppercase muted label: "0 MY VAULTS" and "0 OPEN VAULTS". WORKSPACE: a 2-column grid of white NeoCards. LEFT card titled "Create Vault" holds the tall create form (Vault Title input; Description textarea; a paired row of Bounty number input [hint "Minimum bounty: 1 GAS"] + a custom Difficulty select with single chevron showing "Easy · 0.1 GAS"; a paired row of Vault Secret + Confirm Secret password inputs; the "Fees & secret details" collapsible with a rotating chevron; the dot-prefixed inline create hint; and the full-width primary CTA — disabled CTAs render as a neutral #f1f3f6 fill with a dashed border, not pale-green). RIGHT card titled "Break a Vault" holds the Vault ID input + full-width "Load Vault" secondary button, and (once loaded) the detail rows, attempt field, and danger CTA stacked beneath. FULL-WIDTH BELOW: a "Recent Vaults" NeoCard whose body is an inset #f7f8fa panel containing an auto-fill grid of clickable vault chips (each a white pill row: "#<id>" left, status pill right; hover lifts + turns mint); empty state shows the friendly robot mascot illustration + "No vaults found". A conditional "My Vaults" card appears only when the user owns vaults. Status pills are color-coded: Active/Claimable = green on mint, Broken = muted grey, Expired = amber. Accent values use deep green; addresses use ellipsized monospace. Identity hue is the standard Neo green (brand #16c784, strong #0fb174, CTA #0a8050).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Both stat tiles read 0 (MY VAULTS, OPEN VAULTS). Create Vault form is empty with the inline hint 'Add a vault title to continue.' and a disabled, dashed-border 'Create Vault (bounty + hash)' button. Break panel shows just the Vault ID field + disabled 'Load Vault'. Recent Vaults shows the robot mascot + 'No vaults found'. This is the rendered screenshot state.
- **Create form filled / ready** — Title, description, bounty (e.g. 5), Difficulty 'Medium · 0.5 GAS', and matching Vault Secret + Confirm Secret all filled. Inline hint flips to green 'Ready — secret hashed locally before submit.' and the CTA is the solid green 'Create Vault (bounty + hash)'. Optionally show the 'Fees & secret details' disclosure expanded with its three notes.
- **Create validation error** — Vault Secret and Confirm Secret differ — the red 'Secrets do not match' field error shows below the secret row and the CTA stays disabled (dashed/neutral).
- **Submitting** — CTA in loading state reading 'Creating vault...' with spinner; form inputs held.
- **Vault created confirmation** — Green mint-soft confirmation banner at the bottom: eyebrow 'VAULT CREATED' on the left, '#<id>' (18px/800) on the right — the shareable vault ID.
- **Break — active vault loaded** — Loaded vault shows title + hint, a green 'ACTIVE' status pill, Bounty (e.g. 5.5 GAS accent), bounty-growth note, 'You win (after 2% fee)' net payout, Difficulty, Attempt Fee, Attempts, Days Left rows; then the 'Break Secret' password field, the red 'Attempt Break' button, the non-refundable-fee warning note, and the 'Increase Bounty (GAS)' top-up row with 'Add Bounty'.
- **Break — broken / reclaimable states** — Broken: muted 'BROKEN' pill, Winner row (monospace address), note 'This vault is broken — the bounty was paid to the winner.' Expired/claimable (creator view): amber/green pill plus a full-width green 'Reclaim Vault' button (loading 'reclaiming' state).
- **Populated lists** — Recent Vaults grid filled with vault chips ('#id' + colored status pill, hover lifts to mint) and the conditional 'My Vaults' card visible below it.

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green identity (no secondary theme). Brand accent #16c784 bright for badges/soft chips/hover, strong #0fb174 / deep green for accent values, #0a8050 for the filled primary CTA. Signature visual = a green padlock motif: a mint-soft rounded-square hero badge holding a stroked padlock-with-keyhole icon, echoing the 'hash-locked vault' concept. Difficulty is communicated as fee-tiered chips (Easy/Medium/Hard · 0.1/0.5/1 GAS). Status is a color-coded pill system: green (Active/Claimable), grey (Broken), amber #b4690e (Expired). The 'Break' side intentionally uses a red/danger CTA to signal the risky, non-refundable attempt; disabled CTAs deliberately render as neutral grey with a dashed border so pale-green never reads as already-clickable. Empty state uses the platform's friendly robot mascot.
