# DeFi & Finance

## AA Market Hub  `aa-market-hub`
*Category: defi/finance*

> A trustless escrow marketplace for buying, selling, and settling deterministic Neo Abstract Account (AA) address shells, paid in GAS.

**Purpose.** AA Market Hub lets operators trade Neo Abstract Account addresses (the "shell" of a smart-contract wallet) through a trustless, GAS-escrowed market — no platform fee, only network GAS. A seller lists a registered AA Account ID at a price; a buyer pays GAS into escrow that releases to the seller only when the AA shell transfers successfully, and is refundable by the buyer if settlement fails. Sellers can also reprice or cancel their own listings. A user opens it to put an AA address up for sale, browse the live board, or settle a purchase safely.

**Functional description.** Three roles flow through one screen. SETUP: the user pastes a Market Hash (the AA market contract, validated as a Neo address or 20-byte 0x hash) and taps "Connect Wallet" (wallet is optional to view but required to read the board and act); once connected, "Load Listings" reads the contract — the wallet status flips from "not connected" to the address. SELL: in the Create Listing card the seller fills Price (GAS) (0.01–1000, up to 8 decimals, suffix "GAS"), Account ID Hash (20-byte hash of the deterministic AA address), and an optional Listing Title; an "Advanced (AA core, metadata)" disclosure reveals AA Contract Hash (defaults to the network's canonical AA core) and a Metadata URI textarea. A live Listing Preview card mirrors the buyer-facing result (AA avatar, title or "Untitled listing", short Account ID, price); a "no platform fee" note sits above the "Create Listing" CTA (disabled until market+wallet+valid price+valid hashes). BROWSE: the Live Board / Listings column shows ListingCards — each with an AA avatar, "#id", status chip (active/sold/cancelled), optional "mine"/"pending refund" chips, title, "Listed/Updated {time} ago", a large tabular price + "GAS", a detail inset (AA Contract, Account ID, Seller, Buyer, optional Metadata link, My Pending amount), and a Select button. MANAGE/SETTLE: selecting a listing opens the Manage Selected Listing card (right of Create Listing). If the user is the seller (canManage): New Price (GAS) input + "Update Price", "Cancel Listing", and a settlement note. If a buyer (canBuy): a shell-only caveat, a "New Backup Owner" input (defaults to connected wallet, accepts Neo address or 0x hash), an escrow explainer ("Pays {price} GAS into escrow; released to seller only when the address transfers to you"), and "Buy Listing". If the buyer has escrowed funds on a failed settlement (hasPendingRefund): a "Refund Pending Payment" button. A collapsed "Shell-Only Transfer" caveat note sits under Create Listing explaining verifier/hook/backup-owner are cleared on sale.

**Interface description.** Console-shell layout, light Neo Soft theme, single green accent. HERO BAND (white, hairline border, faint green tint at right): a rounded green-soft storefront icon badge, eyebrow "AA MARKET HUB" (11px/700 uppercase tracked), title "AA address market" (~24px/800, -0.02em), subtitle "Create, manage, and settle AA address listings"; far right two bordered metric pills "0 / LISTINGS" and "0 / ACTIVE" (tabular figures over uppercase muted labels). WALLET BAR (full-width card titled "Wallet & Market"): an inset status row "WALLET … not connected" (turns green when connected), a "Market Hash" input (placeholder 0x…, hint "Required before loading listings or creating a market order."), then two pills — filled-green "Connect Wallet" + secondary "Connect wallet to load" (becomes "Load Listings"/"Loading…"). WORKSPACE = 2 columns. LEFT RAIL: "Create Listing" card with a green pre-condition hint ("Connect a wallet before creating a listing." + small Connect Wallet shortcut), a "LISTING PREVIEW" eyebrow over a preview card (AA tile, "Untitled listing" / "Account ID pending", price "1.5 GAS"), then Price (GAS), Account ID Hash, Listing Title inputs, a collapsed "Advanced (AA core, metadata)" disclosure, a muted no-fee note, and the "Create Listing" primary; below it a collapsed "Shell-Only Transfer" details note; a second "Manage Selected Listing" card appears here only after a listing is selected. RIGHT COLUMN "LIVE BOARD / Listings": eyebrow + h3 heading, then either an empty-state card (centered green-soft "AA" badge, bold heading, hint) or a stack of ListingCards. Cards: 20px radius, white, soft shadow (0 8px 24px), hover lift; selected = green ring (0 0 0 3px rgba(22,199,132,.14)) + brighter border. Chips are pill-shaped, color-coded (active=green-soft, sold=blue, cancelled=red, mine=amber, pending=green). Inputs/insets use #f7f8fa; uppercase tracked micro-labels throughout; AA avatars use green-soft fill #e4f8f0 with #0fb174 text.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First run — no market hash** — Hero shows 0 LISTINGS / 0 ACTIVE. Wallet bar shows 'not connected', empty Market Hash field. Create Listing card shows the 'Connect a wallet…' green hint and a preview reading 'Untitled listing / Account ID pending / 1.5 GAS'. Right board shows the 'Market contract required' empty state (AA badge + 'Enter an AA market contract hash in the operation panel to load listings.').
- **Market entered, wallet not connected** — Market Hash filled and valid; board shows the 'Connect a wallet to load the board' empty state (AA badge + 'Reading the market contract needs a connected wallet. Connect to load live listings…' + a Connect Wallet button). Load button reads 'Connect wallet to load' (disabled).
- **Loading listings** — Wallet connected, read in flight: board shows a spinner empty-state with 'Loading listings…' heading and 'Reading the market contract for the current hash.' Load pill reads 'Loading…'.
- **Board populated — nothing selected** — Hero metrics reflect counts; Left rail is single-column (Create Listing full width, Manage card hidden). Right column lists ListingCards with status/mine/pending chips, prices, detail insets, Select buttons; a hint below: 'Pick a listing to update, cancel, buy, or refund a pending payment.' Possible truncation notice 'Showing {shown} of {total} listings (most recent first).'
- **Listing selected — seller (manage)** — Selected card has the green ring; Manage Selected Listing card appears beside Create Listing with selection summary (#id, price GAS, status), New Price (GAS) input, 'Update Price' primary, 'Cancel Listing' secondary, and the seller settlement note.
- **Listing selected — buyer (settle)** — Manage card shows the shell-only caveat note, 'New Backup Owner' input (prefilled with wallet), the escrow explainer with the live price, and 'Buy Listing' primary; if a prior settlement failed, an extra 'Refund Pending Payment' secondary.
- **Empty board** — Wallet connected, valid market, zero results: board shows '0' badge empty state with 'No listings yet' / 'No listings found for the current market hash.'
- **Submitting / error** — Action buttons in loading state during create/update/cancel/buy/refund; inline field errors ('Price must be between 0.01 and 1000 GAS…', 'Enter a valid Neo address or 20-byte 0x hash.') and toast-style failures (Connect failed, Load listings failed, Action failed).

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default single green accent (#16c784 bright for badges/chips/avatars and the green-soft #e4f8f0 fills with #0fb174 text; #0a8050 filled CTAs). No secondary theme. Signature visual = the AA storefront identity: a rounded green storefront/awning icon badge in the hero plus repeated square "AA" monogram avatar tiles (green-soft fill) on every preview, listing card, and empty state — the AA monogram is the recurring motif. Status is communicated through color-coded pill chips (green active / blue sold / red cancelled / amber mine / green pending). Tabular-nums price figures and uppercase tracked micro-labels reinforce a clean operator-console feel.

## Confidential Transfer  `private-transfer`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Seal a private Neo N3 transfer intent — recipient, amount and memo are encrypted in your browser before they ever leave the page, ready for Morpheus confidential compute to verify in a TEE.

**Purpose.** Confidential Transfer is a privacy-payments "sealing desk." A user fills out a transfer (recipient, asset, amount, optional private memo) and the app encrypts those fields locally with X25519-HKDF-SHA256-AES-256-GCM, storing only ciphertext through Morpheus. The output is an encrypted intent reference (secret ref + public commitment + nullifier) that a TEE can later decrypt and settle. Critically, this app moves NO funds and has NO contract — it only produces an attestable sealed intent. Users open it to privately prepare a transfer without exposing the recipient/amount on-chain, and without requiring an unsupported zk curve on Neo N3.

**Functional description.** Wallet is NOT required (walletRequired:false); the app auto-detects the connected chain to default the Network. Core flow: (1) Pick Network (Testnet / Mainnet) and Asset (GAS / NEO) from two dropdowns — each network option shows a live/degraded/checking suffix (e.g. "Mainnet · Degraded") from a Morpheus public-key health ping. If the chosen lane is degraded, an inline amber alert appears ("Oracle key unavailable on this network — sealing is paused.") plus a reassuring note that the form still works, and if the OTHER lane is live a one-tap "Switch to {network} (live)" pill. (2) Enter Recipient (Neo N3 address, placeholder "N…", Base58Check-validated; invalid shows "Enter a valid Neo N3 address."). (3) Enter Amount — a number field with preset pills (GAS: 0.1 / 1 / 5; NEO: 1 / 5 / 10), a persistent precision hint ("GAS settles at up to 8 decimal places." / "NEO is indivisible — whole numbers only."), and asset-aware validation. Switching GAS→NEO auto-floors a fractional amount. (4) Optional Private memo with a live N/160 character counter. Once recipient + amount are valid, a "You are about to seal" confirm summary replaces the validation hint, echoing Recipient (shortened head…tail), Amount + Asset, Network, and Encryption algorithm. Below it a persistent "No funds were moved…" note sits above the primary CTA "Seal private transfer intent". On click it shows "Sealing…", fetches the Morpheus key, encrypts locally, and stores ciphertext; on success the right rail flips to a result showing the message and three copyable rows — Secret ref, Commitment, Nullifier — each with a one-line purpose caption and a Copy/Copied (⧉/✓) button. Secondary surfaces: a "Sealed intents" history (locally stored per-device list with Network/Asset meta + copyable Secret ref & Commitment, plus a "Clear history" action) and an always-open "Where a confidential transfer fits" 4-step explainer badging steps 1 & 4 "Not in this app" and 2 & 3 "In this app". On-chain behaviour: none — no transaction is signed or sent; failures keep details local and surface a "Nothing was sent on-chain" safe-copy plus an optional service detail.

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft layout, centered max-width ~1040px, 16px vertical rhythm. TOP: a hero card (radius 20px, white→mint gradient #ffffff→#eefdfa, soft shadow) with a 48px rounded square lock-icon badge (soft green #e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 glyph) on the left; body has eyebrow "NEO N3 PRIVATE PAYMENTS" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em), title "Seal a private transfer intent" (26px/800, -0.02em ink #1e1e2e), a descriptive paragraph (~14px, max 620px), then a facts row showing "{Network} · {Asset}" and a green badge pill "No on-chain zk curve dependency". MIDDLE: a 2-column workspace. LEFT panel (white card) holds a form-grid — top row Network select + Asset select side-by-side (with the degraded inline alert/switch under Network), a full-width Recipient input, an Amount input with three preset pills + precision hint, and a full-width Private memo input with "Optional" tag and N/160 counter. Below the fields: either the amber "You are about to seal" summary card (a 4-cell dt/dd grid: Recipient, Amount, Network, Encryption) when valid, or a muted validation hint when not; then the "No funds were moved…" note; then the full-width filled-green CTA "Seal private transfer intent". RIGHT rail is contextual: before first seal it's an intro card ("Encrypted before it leaves your browser") with a lock icon and a 3-item green-tick checklist (Sealed locally / TEE verified / No funds moved, each title + one-line desc); after sealing it becomes a status card (shield-check icon, "MORPHEUS CONFIDENTIAL COMPUTE" eyebrow, bold status line) with either the 3 copyable result rows (success) or safe-copy + service detail (error). BELOW: a "SEALED INTENTS" section (eyebrow + subtitle + Clear history button) showing an empty state (StateView line-icon + "Sealed transfer intents will appear here after you seal one.") or a list of intent cards. BOTTOM: an open <details> "Where a confidential transfer fits" with a 4-card numbered grid, each card tagged "In this app" (green, steps 2/3) or "Not in this app" (muted, steps 1/4). Identity: single green accent (#16c784 decorative, #0fb174 strong, #0a7d52 text, #e4f8f0 soft), hairline borders rgba(20,22,38,0.06), the padlock/shield as the signature security motif.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / idle (degraded lane)** — Empty form with Network defaulting to detected chain shown as 'Mainnet · Degraded', the amber inline alert + reassurance note + 'Switch to Testnet (live)' pill under the Network select. Amount/recipient blank so the LEFT panel shows the muted validation hint ('Add a valid recipient and positive amount to enable local sealing'); CTA disabled. RIGHT rail shows the intro card 'Encrypted before it leaves your browser' with the 3 green-tick points. History section shows its empty state with line-icon + hint.
- **Filled / ready to seal** — Valid Neo N3 recipient + amount entered. LEFT panel now shows the green-accented 'You are about to seal' summary (Recipient head…tail, Amount + Asset, Network, Encryption algorithm X25519-HKDF-SHA256-AES-256-GCM), the 'No funds were moved…' note, and an enabled filled-green 'Seal private transfer intent' CTA. Preset pill highlighted if matched; memo counter active. RIGHT rail still the intro card.
- **Sealing / submitting** — CTA shows 'Sealing…' and is disabled; a header-level info status reads 'Sealing private transfer'. Right rail can carry an in-progress treatment of 'Fetching Morpheus key, encrypting locally, and storing ciphertext.' Use this to mock the loading/busy treatment of the button and any spinner.
- **Success / sealed result** — RIGHT rail becomes the status card (shield-check icon, 'MORPHEUS CONFIDENTIAL COMPUTE' eyebrow) with the success message about ciphertext stored, then three copyable rows — Secret ref, Commitment, Nullifier — each with its purpose caption and a ⧉ Copy → ✓ Copied button. The 'Sealed intents' history below now lists the new intent card (Network/Asset meta + copyable Secret ref & Commitment) with a 'Clear history' action visible.
- **Error / sealing unavailable** — RIGHT rail status card in error tone showing a user-facing message (e.g. 'Morpheus sealing is unavailable for this network. Your transfer details remain local.'), the reassuring safe-copy 'Nothing was sent on-chain. Fix the inputs or try again when the Morpheus service is available.', and an optional muted 'Service detail: …' line. Form values persist.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft green accent used as the security/privacy theme: bright #16c784 for the badge pill and decorative chips, #0fb174 strong for icon glyphs, #0a7d52 for green text, soft #e4f8f0 fills, and the filled-green primary CTA. Signature visual is the padlock icon (closed lock in the hero badge + intro card) and a shield-with-check mark for the sealed/status card — reinforcing "sealed, verified, no funds moved." Hero uses a subtle white→mint gradient (#ffffff→#eefdfa). A distinct amber/warning tone appears only for the degraded-network alert and the pre-seal confirm summary, signalling caution without breaking the green identity.

## Flash Loan  `flashloan`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Atomic flash loans on Neo N3 — borrow GAS, run your callback logic, and repay principal + a 0.09% fee in a single transaction, or provide liquidity to earn a share of the fees.

**Purpose.** Flash Loan is a power-user DeFi tool that lets developers borrow uncollateralized GAS from an on-chain pool and repay it (plus a 0.09% fee) within the same transaction via a deployed onFlashLoan callback contract — if the callback fails to repay exactly, the whole transaction reverts. It doubles as a passive yield surface: anyone (no callback contract needed) can deposit GAS into the pool and earn a share (default 80%) of every loan fee. Users open it to inspect live pool stats, calculate fees/repayment before borrowing, submit a callback-based loan, look up any loan's on-chain status, or supply/withdraw liquidity.

**Functional description.** The app spans a borrow flow and a liquidity flow, both wallet-gated.

BORROW (developer flow): The hero shows wallet status ("Wallet required / Not connected" until connected). An "INSTRUCTIONAL MODE" note up front warns that borrowing requires a deployed contract implementing onFlashLoan and points non-developers to the liquidity card. In the "Request Flash Loan" card (eyebrow "ATOMIC LIQUIDITY", method pill "requestLoan"): user enters Loan Amount in GAS (number input, placeholder "Enter amount in GAS") or taps a preset chip (1 GAS / 10 GAS / 100 GAS — active chip turns green-filled); enters the Callback Contract address (validated as 0x-hex or N-address); the Callback Method is locked/displayed as a read-only row "Callback Method → onFlashLoan" (fixed for safety). A helper note explains the onFlashLoan(borrower, amount, fee, loanId) prerequisite with a "View onFlashLoan example" link. The primary CTA reads "Connect and Sign" when disconnected (a secondary "Connect Wallet" button appears) and "Sign requestFlashLoan" when connected; it's disabled (soft-green) until both amount + callback are filled. On submit it dispatches requestLoan; a session-only "Latest Tx / Borrower / Amount" summary appears in the calculator card (else "No flash-loan transaction has been submitted in this session.").

CALCULATOR (right column, eyebrow "CALCULATOR", rate pill shows "0.09%"): live read-only preview grid of 6 tiles — Min Loan (1 GAS), Max Loan (100,000 GAS), Cooldown ("5 min between loans"), Daily Limit ("10 loans / day"), Estimated Fee (computed = amount × 0.09%), and an accent-highlighted Total Repayment tile (principal + fee). Below it a pill flow: Borrow → Execute → Repay.

PROVIDE LIQUIDITY (full-width card, method pill "deposit / withdraw"): explains depositing GAS backs loans and earns a share of the 0.09% fee, plus the share split note (LPs earn 80%, 20% protocol; fees credited on distributeFees so "Fees Earned" can lag). Two stat tiles: Your Liquidity, Fees Earned (both GAS, default 0.0000). Amount input + Deposit (primary) and Withdraw (secondary) buttons. On mainnet an extra "Payment Receipt ID" input appears (deposit requires it).

LOOKUP + HISTORY (bottom two-column): "Loan Status Lookup" (eyebrow "ON-CHAIN LOOKUP", method pill "getLoanDetails") — Loan ID input + "Check Status" button; result renders a row (#id, amount GAS, +fee GAS, status pill) plus a mono callback line. "Recent Executions" (eyebrow "HISTORY") — a 4-column table (Loan ID / Amount / Fee / Status) of past loans, or empty state "No executions yet". Status pills: Executed (green), Failed (red), Pending (amber). A read-failure banner ("Stats unavailable" + "Live pool stats are temporarily unavailable. Showing the last known values.") can appear in amber in the hero.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling page, ~1200px, vertical stack of Neo Soft white rounded cards (radius 16px, hairline border rgba(20,22,38,0.06), soft shadow) on a light canvas; green accent (#16c784 bright, #0fb174 strong, #0b5e3e deep text, #e4f8f0 soft fill).

HERO band (white card): left a 52px rounded soft-green tile holding a lightning-bolt icon; center eyebrow "ATOMIC LIQUIDITY" (11px/700 uppercase, green), title "Flash Loan" (30px/800, -0.02em ink), subtitle "The callback contract receives the principal, executes your logic, and must return principal + fee before the transaction finishes."; right a wallet chip (subtle inset, or green-tinted when connected) showing label "Wallet required"/"Wallet connected" over the address/"Not connected". Below: a 4-up stat row (subtle #f7f8fa inset tiles) — Pool Balance, Loans Executed, Total Volume, Total Fees (figures 22px/800 tabular-nums over 10px uppercase muted labels; screenshot shows 0.0000 / 0 / 0.00 / 0.0000 first-run).

INSTRUCTION note: full-width subtle inset with deep-green "INSTRUCTIONAL MODE" badge + body about the onFlashLoan prerequisite and LP fallback.

WORKSPACE row 1 (2 cols ~1.08:0.92): left "Request Flash Loan" form card (eyebrow + title + neutral mono method pill "requestLoan"); amount input, 3 preset chips grid, callback input, a 2-col "Callback Method / onFlashLoan" fixed-row, helper note + link, full-width primary CTA. Right "Loan Calculator" card with green "0.09%" rate pill, 2×3 preview-tile grid (last tile Total Repayment accent-tinted green), Borrow→Execute→Repay pill flow with faint arrows, and a request-summary / "No flash-loan transaction..." dashed empty box.

FULL-WIDTH "Provide Liquidity" card: info paragraphs, 2 stat tiles (Your Liquidity / Fees Earned), amount input, Deposit + Withdraw 2-button grid.

WORKSPACE row 2 (2 cols ~0.9:1.1): left "Loan Status Lookup" (input + Check Status button inline, optional result row); right "Recent Executions" table or "No executions yet" dashed empty state. Method pills are neutral grey mono chips except the green data pills (rate, total). Status pills are small uppercase rounded chips (green/red/amber).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / disconnected** — Wallet chip shows 'Wallet required / Not connected'; hero stats all zero (0.0000 / 0 / 0.00 / 0.0000); INSTRUCTIONAL MODE note visible; request form empty with disabled soft-green 'Connect and Sign' CTA + secondary 'Connect Wallet'; calculator shows defaults (Min 1, Max 100,000, 5 min, 10 loans/day, 0 fee, 0 repayment) and 'No flash-loan transaction...' empty box; liquidity stats 0.0000; Recent Executions shows 'No executions yet'.
- **Connected / ready to borrow** — Wallet chip green-tinted 'Wallet connected' + truncated address; amount filled (e.g. 100 GAS preset active/green), callback address entered, fixed onFlashLoan row shown; calculator live-updates Estimated Fee (0.09 GAS) and accent Total Repayment (100.09 GAS); primary CTA active green 'Sign requestFlashLoan'.
- **Submitting** — CTA in loading state (spinner), inputs disabled; used for both requestLoan and deposit/withdraw/lookup actions.
- **Loan submitted (success summary)** — Calculator request-summary populated with Latest Tx (compacted txid), Borrower, Amount (e.g. 100 GAS); Recent Executions table now lists rows with #id, amount, fee, and green 'Executed' status pill.
- **Loan lookup result** — Lookup card shows a result row (#id, amount GAS, +fee GAS, status pill Executed/Failed/Pending) plus a mono 'Callback Contract: 0x… → onFlashLoan' line.
- **Liquidity provider view** — Provide Liquidity card with non-zero Your Liquidity / Fees Earned tiles, amount input filled, Deposit (green primary) + Withdraw (secondary) active; on mainnet an extra 'Payment Receipt ID' input appears above the buttons.
- **Stats-unavailable / error** — Amber 'Stats unavailable' banner in hero ('Live pool stats are temporarily unavailable. Showing the last known values.'); inline red validation-error box under the form for invalid amount/callback; Failed loan rows show red status pill.

**Visual identity.** Neo green default accent — #16c784 bright (badges, soft chips, preset-active fill), #0fb174 strong (icon/eyebrow), #0b5e3e deep green for green text on light surfaces, #e4f8f0 soft fill; light theme on white cards over #f7f8fa insets. Signature visual: the lightning-bolt icon in a 52px rounded soft-green tile (the 'zap' / atomic-instant motif), reinforced by the green '0.09%' rate pill and the Borrow → Execute → Repay pill flow conveying single-transaction atomicity. Neutral grey monospace method pills (requestLoan, getLoanDetails, deposit / withdraw) give it a developer-console feel. Status semantics: green Executed, red Failed, amber Pending; amber for service-degraded notices, red for validation errors.

## Milestone Escrow  `milestone-escrow`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Lock NEO or GAS in one escrow, split it into up to 12 milestones, and release each tranche only after the creator approves the work.

**Purpose.** Milestone Escrow lets a creator (payer) lock funds with a beneficiary and pay them out in stages tied to deliverables instead of a single lump sum. Each milestone is approved on-chain by the creator, then claimed on-chain by the beneficiary; any unapproved funds stay locked and refundable if the escrow is cancelled. Users open it to set up trustless staged payments for freelance/contract work ("Website delivery escrow") with no platform fee — beneficiaries receive the full milestone amount and only network gas is paid.

**Functional description.** Two roles share one screen. FIRST RUN (no wallet): the explainer panel pitches "Pay in stages, release on approval" with a 3-step how-it-works list (Step 1: Define milestones and amounts; the total equals the deposit. Step 2: Creator approves milestones after deliverables are accepted. Step 3: Beneficiary claims each approved tranche on-chain.), a no-fee note, a full-width green "Connect Wallet" CTA, and a read-only "Sample escrow" card on the right. CREATE FLOW (wallet connected, contract ready): a secondary "Create Escrow" button toggles open a form card with fields — Escrow name (placeholder "Website delivery escrow"), Beneficiary address (placeholder "Enter Neo N3 address"), an Asset pill toggle GAS/NEO (radiogroup), a Milestones repeater (1-12 rows, each a labeled "Milestone {index}" amount input with a "Remove" link; a "+ Add milestone" button plus a live "Total amount → {sum} {asset}" readout that must equal the deposit), and a Description textarea. Two notes sit above submit: "No platform fee…" and "Creating an escrow needs two wallet signatures: first the {asset} deposit, then the escrow setup." Submit is gated until a beneficiary + all positive amounts exist; creating fires two wallet prompts (deposit, then createEscrow) and on success closes/resets the form. MANAGE FLOW: an "Escrows" header with a "Refresh" button, then the ledger grouped into "Created by you {count}" and "For you {count}" (the latter carries the note "Funds release only after the creator approves each milestone."). Each escrow card shows title (or "#{id}"), the counterparty address, a status pill (Active/Completed/Cancelled), an amount block (total, "{released} / {total} released", "{done} / {count} milestones"), a per-milestone breakdown list (M1/M2/M3 with amount + Pending/Approved/Claimed pill, shown only when >1 milestone), and role-specific actions. Creator cards: "Approve M{n} — {amount}" (acts on the next unapproved milestone; disabled with tooltip "All milestones approved" when none) and a two-click "Cancel" → "Confirm cancel — refunds {amount}". Beneficiary cards: "Claim M{n} — {amount}" (next approved-but-unclaimed; disabled with "No approved milestone to claim"). Actions show "Approving…/Claiming…/Cancelling…" busy text and toast on success (e.g. "Milestone claimed — funds released").

**Interface description.** Centered single-column stack (~680px content width) on a light page, made of two stacked white rounded cards (radius 20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,0.06)). TOP CARD = hero: a 48px circular soft-green badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 green checkmark icon) left of the headings — eyebrow "ESCROWS" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em tracking, muted), title "Milestone Escrow" (24-28px/800, ink #1e1e2e, -0.02em), subtitle "Approve milestones and release funds in stages". Below sits an inset (#f7f8fa, radius 16px) 3-up stat strip divided by hairline rules: ESCROWS / "Total you are party to", ACTIVE / "Funds still locked", COMPLETED / "Fully released" — figures are tabular-num and render "—" before any escrow exists. Once escrows exist a green progress band appears ("Escrows completed" label + percent, a gradient fill #16c784→#0fb174 track with numbered checkpoint dots). SECOND CARD = workspace, a 2-column split: LEFT pitch column has h3 "Pay in stages, release on approval", a lede paragraph, a numbered 3-step list (small green-soft circular numerals with STEP kicker + copy), a muted no-fee note, then the green primary CTA + hint; RIGHT preview column has a "SAMPLE ESCROW" tag above a bordered sub-card: title "Website delivery escrow" + green "Active" pill, meta "Neo N3 · Milestone Escrow", a big "10 GAS" amount with "5 GAS / 10 GAS released", and three milestone rows (M1 5 GAS · CLAIMED, M2 3 GAS · APPROVED, M3 2 GAS · PENDING) where status pills use green-soft for claimed/approved and neutral grey for pending. Connected state replaces the workspace with the create form card and the grouped escrow ledger. Buttons are pill-shaped (radius 9999px); primary = filled deep green (#0a8050) white text; asset toggle + remove/add are soft neutral pills. Color identity is the standard Neo Soft green single-accent palette.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First run — disconnected explainer** — Hero card (stats show '—', no progress band) + workspace card split into the left how-it-works pitch (headline, lede, 3 numbered steps, no-fee note, full-width green 'Connect Wallet' CTA with 'Connect a wallet to create your first escrow' hint) and the right read-only 'SAMPLE ESCROW' card (Website delivery escrow, Active, 10 GAS, 5/10 released, M1 Claimed / M2 Approved / M3 Pending). This is the current rendered state.
- **Connected — empty ledger** — Hero with real wallet but zero escrows ('—' or 0 stats). A secondary 'Create Escrow' button, an 'Escrows' header with 'Refresh', and a friendly empty state (robot/line-icon mascot + 'No escrows yet').
- **Create escrow form (filled/ready)** — Form card open: Escrow name, Beneficiary address, GAS/NEO asset pill toggle, a multi-row Milestones repeater (e.g. M1 5, M2 3, M3 2 with Remove links + '+ Add milestone') with live 'Total amount → 10 GAS', Description textarea, the no-fee + two-signature notes, and an enabled green 'Submit'. Show the disabled/gated variant too (no beneficiary yet).
- **Active escrow card — creator & beneficiary** — Ledger grouped 'Created by you 1' / 'For you 1'. Creator card: title, beneficiary address, Active pill, total + released/total + milestone-count, M1/M2/M3 breakdown pills, 'Approve M2 — 3 GAS' + 'Cancel' actions. Beneficiary card with approval note + 'Claim M1 — 5 GAS'.
- **Submitting / busy** — Submit shows spinner; or an action button shows 'Approving…' / 'Claiming…' / 'Cancelling…'; two-signature flow implies sequential wallet prompts (deposit then setup). Cancel shows its armed 'Confirm cancel — refunds 5 GAS' confirm state.
- **Success / completed** — Success toast (e.g. 'Milestone claimed — funds released'); escrow card progresses (released/total increases, milestone pill flips to Claimed); a fully-released escrow shows 'Completed' status and bumps the hero Completed stat + progress band percentage.
- **Unsupported network / error** — Wallet connected but contract not configured: explainer keeps the pitch but swaps the CTA for a status notice 'Not available on this network' + 'Milestone escrow isn't live on this network yet…'. Also covers inline validation errors (invalid address, milestone sum ≠ total, min 1 NEO / 0.1 GAS).

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft light theme with a single green accent (bright #16c784 for badges/chips/progress gradient, #0fb174 for green icons/text, deep #0a8050 for the filled primary CTA, soft #e4f8f0 fills). Signature visual = the circular soft-green badge with a green checkmark (approval/release motif) and the staged-release milestone ledger: numbered M1/M2/M3 rows with Pending→Approved→Claimed status pills and a green gradient progress band with numbered checkpoint dots tracking escrows-completed. No secondary theme — green-only, finance/escrow tone.

## Neo Multisig  `neo-multisig`
*Category: defi/finance*

> An on-chain custody vault where a group of signers pool GAS or NEO and the contract only releases a spend once the approval threshold is met.

**Purpose.** Neo Multisig is an on-chain custody vault on Neo N3. A group creates a shared vault from 2-16 signer addresses with an M-of-N approval threshold, deposits GAS or NEO, proposes a spend, and the contract releases the funds only after enough listed signers approve. Users open it to run a shared treasury without single-key trust, or to load a vault/request ID shared by a co-signer and cast their approval.

**Functional description.** The primary flow is a numbered create-then-operate sequence inside the left "Custody vault" card. Step 1 Create vault: the user fills a dynamic list of "Signer Address N" inputs (starts at 3 rows, "Neo N3 address (N...)" placeholder, "+ Add signer" up to 16, each populated/extra row gets a × remove button down to a 2-signer minimum), picks "Approval Threshold" from a dropdown (1..signer-count), and presses the filled-green "Create Vault" button. A live hint below the form is either a blocking reason ("Add at least two signer addresses before creating a vault.", "A vault supports at most 16 signer addresses.", "Each signer must be a valid Neo N3 address.", "Signer addresses must be distinct.", "Threshold cannot be greater than the number of signers.") or the green ready state "Ready to deploy an on-chain custody vault." Create is disabled until valid; wallet connection is required. On success a receipt strip appears ("Vault ID #N" + "Share this vault ID so signers can deposit and approve spend requests."), and Steps 2 and 3 unfold in the same card. Step 2 Deposit funds: an Amount field with the asset as a suffix + an Asset selector (GAS/NEO), and a "Deposit" button — the transfer credits the vault balance directly. Step 3 Propose a spend: Recipient Address (N3) + Asset selector, Amount (suffix), and a "Memo (optional)" textarea ("Short note for signers"), with a pre-flight insufficient-balance guard ("Amount exceeds the vault balance ({balance} {asset} available).") and a persistent info note that the vault balance is pooled/shared across pending requests (funds aren't reserved until threshold). Step 4 Approve/cancel a loaded request: shows the Request ID (#N) with a Copy button (toggles to "Copied"), a share hint, a 4-cell detail grid (Status / Amount / Signers count-of-threshold / To recipient), an approval-progress line ("{count} / {total} approvals collected"), and a two-button row — filled-green "Approve" (membership-gated: only listed signers who haven't already approved; otherwise a hint "Only the vault's signer addresses can approve or cancel this request." or "You have already approved this request.") and a secondary "Cancel". If a threshold approval finds the vault underfunded the request auto-cancels with an amber notice explaining required vs held amounts. The right aside has: Vault overview (Signers count, Approval Threshold, a signer roster of shortened 0x hashes with a "You" badge on the connected wallet, and GAS/NEO balance rows once a vault is loaded); Recent Activity (clickable history cards labeled "Vault #id · t/n" or "#id · amount asset" that re-load on tap, else "No vaults or requests yet."); and "Load vault or request" with two ID inputs each paired with a "Load" button, plus a collapsed "Execution route" disclosure mapping Create vault / Propose spend / Approve & release. Toasts confirm each on-chain step (vault created, deposited, request created, approval submitted, "Threshold met — funds released.").

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft layout on a #f5f7fa page, centered ~1200px shell. Top hero band is a white-to-mint card (linear-gradient white→#e4f8f0, radius 20px, soft shadow): a green shield-with-check badge chip (soft mint fill #e4f8f0, brand-strong icon), an "ON-CHAIN CUSTODY" eyebrow (11px/700 uppercase, .12em tracking, muted), the title "Multisig Custody Vault" (~26px/800, -0.02em), and subtitle "Deposit GAS or NEO into a shared vault, propose a spend, and let the contract release funds once the approval threshold is met." Below the subtitle sit three inset stat tiles (white, radius 14px, hairline) labeled THRESHOLD ("2 / 2"), VAULT BALANCE ("—" until funded) and REQUEST ("—" until a request loads), followed by a connection line "Connect a wallet to create or sign" (or "Connected as: <short addr>"). Under the hero is a 2-column workspace. Left column is one tall white "erobo" card "Custody vault" with a section-heading row (label left, "Step 1" pill right) and progressively revealed Step 2/Step 3/Step 4 sub-sections separated by hairline dividers; signer rows are stacked inputs with a small × remove glyph and a green-text "+ Add signer" affordance; the ready hint renders as a soft mint band, blocking hints as a neutral guide band, the pooled-balance note as an info band, and the unfunded notice as an amber band (#fdf6ec bg, #b45309 text). Right aside stacks three smaller white cards: "Vault overview / Signers", "Recent Activity", and "Load vault or request / Neo N3" — each with the same eyebrow-style section heading, key/value signal rows, pill-shaped secondary "Load" buttons, and a roster where the connected signer gets a soft-mint "You" pill. Primary CTAs are filled green (#0a8050-family, white text), secondary buttons are outline/soft; inputs are #f7f8fa insets with rounded corners and a green focus ring (rgba(22,199,132,0.18)). Tabular-nums on all figures.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / not connected (empty)** — Hero with shield badge, 'Multisig Custody Vault' title, THRESHOLD 2/2, VAULT BALANCE —, REQUEST —, and 'Connect a wallet to create or sign'. Left card shows Step 1 only: three empty 'Signer Address' inputs, '+ Add signer', Threshold=2 dropdown, neutral hint 'Add at least two signer addresses before creating a vault.', disabled green Create Vault. Aside: Signers 0, Threshold 2/2, 'No vaults or requests yet.', and the two empty Load ID boxes.
- **Vault form ready (valid, connected)** — Connected line shows shortened address. Signer inputs filled with valid N-addresses, threshold selected, green ready band 'Ready to deploy an on-chain custody vault.' and an enabled filled-green Create Vault button. Hero THRESHOLD tile reflects the draft e.g. 2 / 3.
- **Vault created — deposit & propose unfolded** — Receipt strip 'Vault ID #N' + share copy. Step 2 deposit (Amount+GAS/NEO selector, Deposit) and Step 3 propose (Recipient, Asset, Amount, Memo textarea) now visible with the pooled-balance info note. Aside Vault overview now lists the signer roster (shortened 0x hashes, a 'You' pill on the connected signer) and GAS/NEO balance rows; hero VAULT BALANCE shows e.g. '12.5 GAS · 0 NEO'.
- **Submitting / on-chain pending** — A primary action in loading state (e.g. Create Vault spinner, or Approve showing 'Approving...', Cancel showing 'Cancelling...'); buttons disabled while the wallet signs and the tx broadcasts.
- **Request loaded — approval in progress** — Step 4 block: Request ID #N with Copy/Copied button + share hint, detail grid (Status Pending / Amount / Signers '1 / 2' / To recipient), progress line '1 / 2 approvals collected', filled-green Approve + secondary Cancel. Hero REQUEST tile reads 'Pending'.
- **Released / executed success** — Request status flips to 'Executed' (success toast 'Threshold met — funds released.'), progress shows full count, hero REQUEST tile 'Executed', and the item appears as an executed card in Recent Activity.
- **Blocked / error states** — Create blocked hints (too many signers, invalid address, duplicates, threshold > signers); propose insufficient-balance hint; approve gating hint ('Only the vault's signer addresses can approve…' / 'You have already approved this request.'); and the amber unfunded auto-cancel notice with required vs held amounts.

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green identity, single accent hue: brand #16c784 (decorative badge/chips), brand-strong #0fb174 (icon fills, soft-mint text), filled CTAs in deep green with white text, soft fill #e4f8f0 for the hero gradient/ready band/'You' pill and green focus ring rgba(22,199,132,0.18). Signature visual is the green shield-with-checkmark glyph (custody/security motif) in a soft-mint rounded badge atop the hero, paired with a white→mint hero gradient. Amber (#fdf6ec/#b45309) is the only non-green accent, reserved for the underfunded auto-cancel warning; red (#ef4444) only for signer-row remove hover. Rounded cards (14/20px), soft shadows, pill buttons, tabular-nums figures.

## Neo Pay  `neo-pay`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Lock GAS or NEO and release it on a schedule — streaming payment vaults for payrolls, subscriptions, and allowances on Neo N3.

**Purpose.** Neo Pay (on-screen title "NeoPay") lets a user create funded, on-chain payment streams: a sender locks a total amount of GAS or NEO and the contract releases it linearly per day over a chosen duration, so the recipient can claim vested funds over time. A user opens it to set up a recurring/scheduled payout (payroll, subscription, allowance) without trusting an off-chain scheduler, and to manage existing streams — track release progress, claim what has vested, or cancel an outgoing stream to reclaim unreleased funds. It is wallet-gated and shows a chain warning if on the wrong network.

**Functional description.** PRIMARY FLOW — Create a stream: connect wallet -> fill the "Create Stream" form: Recipient Address (N3 address... placeholder), Amount (numeric, suffixed with the selected token "GAS"/"NEO", placeholder 0.00), Duration (numeric, suffixed "days", placeholder "Number of days", 1–365), Notes (optional, "Add context for the recipient") -> pick TOKEN via a two-pill segmented selector [GAS | NEO], GAS default/active. As Amount+Duration are filled, a live SCHEDULE DISCLOSURE note appears below the selector: for the linear case "Releases {amount} {token}/day for {days} days."; for a sub-1-NEO/day NEO total a WARNING-styled note "Less than 1 NEO/day at this duration — all {amount} NEO releases in a single cliff at day {days}, not gradually." A second always-on disclosure reads "Creating a stream needs two wallet signatures: first the {token} deposit, then the stream setup." -> press the full-width "Create Stream" button (disabled/quiet until recipient+amount+duration are all present; shows a loading spinner while submitting). On submit the wallet prompts TWICE (deposit, then createStream); on success the form clears, a "Stream created" toast fires, and the new stream appears in "Your Created Streams". SECONDARY ACTIONS: Claim — on a card in "Streams You Receive" press the green "Claim" button (enabled only when claimable balance > 0 and stream not finalized; otherwise the card shows "Nothing to claim yet — funds vest over time."), wallet prompt -> "Funds claimed" toast. Cancel — on a card in "Your Created Streams" press the red "Cancel" button (disabled once completed/cancelled), wallet prompt -> "Stream cancelled" toast, unreleased funds returned to creator. On-chain behavior a designer must reflect: funds lock on creation; each card shows a release progress bar (green while active, blue at 100%) with a "{streamed} / {total} {token} ({pct}%)" label; created cards also show the per-day rate chip "{amount} {token} / day"; status badges are Active (green) / Completed (blue) / Cancelled (red). A degraded/service state ("Stream index unavailable") can appear above the form when the stream index can't load — the create form still works.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout of rounded white cards on a light page (the rendered screenshot is the narrow launcher column ~1320px). TOP — HERO CARD: left a 46px circular soft-green badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #16c784 line-chart/pulse glyph) with a 4px green halo; right an eyebrow "STREAMS" (11px/700 uppercase, green, .12em tracking), title "NeoPay" (22px/800, ink, -0.02em), subtitle "Scheduled releases for payrolls, subscriptions, and allowances". Below, a full-width inset stat strip (#f7f8fa, radius 16px) split 1fr|divider|1fr into two centered stat tiles: TOTAL STREAMS and ACTIVE, each a big tabular figure (22px/800) over a 10px uppercase muted label; at first run both render an em-dash/muted "0" and a centered uppercase note "AWAITING ACTIVITY". SECOND CARD — "Create Stream" (bold card title, hairline rule): stacked fields Recipient Address, Amount (right-aligned "GAS" suffix), Duration (right-aligned "days" suffix), Notes (optional); then a TOKEN row = small uppercase muted "TOKEN" label + two pill/8px-radius buttons GAS|NEO (active = green-soft fill #e4f8f0, deep-green text #0b5e3e, green border); then the disclosure note(s) in a #f7f8fa rounded box (warn variant = amber #fff4e5/#b25e09); then a full-width primary button "Create Stream" (filled #0a8050 white text when ready, quiet gray #f7f8fa muted text when disabled — note the screenshot shows a pale-green outline ready/idle state). THIRD CARD — "Your Created Streams (N)": list of stream rows; each row = absolute status pill top-right, "#id" + optional title heading, a wrapped detail line (To: N3 truncated, total amount+token, "{n}d" duration, green per-day rate chip), optional note, a 6px green progress bar with a "{streamed}/{total} {token} ({pct}%)" caption, and a small red "Cancel" action. FOURTH CARD — "Streams You Receive (N)": same row anatomy but with "From:" address, a "Claimable: {amt} {token}" line (green strong) or the italic "Nothing to claim yet" hint, and a small green "Claim" action. EMPTY states inside each list card = centered 44px circular soft-green icon (pulse glyph for created / down-arrow glyph for incoming) + muted line ("You haven't created any streams yet" / "No incoming streams"). Mobile (≤640px): stream rows stack vertically, actions become full-width. Identity is the default Neo Soft green system with tabular-nums figures and soft 0 8px 24px shadows.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First run / empty (wallet connected, no streams)** — Hero with muted 0 / em-dash stats and 'AWAITING ACTIVITY' note; Create Stream form blank with GAS pill active and the two-signature disclosure visible; both list cards (Your Created Streams (0), Streams You Receive (0)) show their centered soft-green icon + 'You haven't created any streams yet' / 'No incoming streams'. This is the rendered screenshot state.
- **Create form filled / ready** — Recipient, Amount, Duration filled; the live linear schedule note 'Releases X GAS/day for N days.' shown plus the two-signature disclosure; Create Stream button in its solid filled-green ready state.
- **NEO cliff warning variant** — Token = NEO with a sub-1-NEO/day total: amber warning disclosure 'Less than 1 NEO/day at this duration — all X NEO releases in a single cliff at day N, not gradually.' replacing the linear note.
- **Submitting** — Create Stream button shows spinner/loading; (separately) list cards can show the centered spinner + 'Loading…' during fetch/refresh.
- **Created streams populated** — Your Created Streams list with one+ rows: Active green badge, #id, To: address, total+token, duration, green per-day rate chip, partial green progress bar with '% streamed' caption, red Cancel button.
- **Incoming / claim-ready** — Streams You Receive row with From: address, a green 'Claimable: X GAS' line, enabled green Claim button; sibling row in the not-yet-vested state shows italic 'Nothing to claim yet — funds vest over time.' with Claim disabled.
- **Finalized streams** — Cards showing Completed (blue badge, 100% blue progress bar) and Cancelled (red badge) states with their actions disabled.
- **Service degraded** — Amber 'Stream index unavailable' notice banner above the Create Stream card while lists can't load; the form remains usable.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft green identity — bright brand #16c784 for the hero badge, soft chips (#e4f8f0), active token pill text (#0b5e3e), progress bars and claimable emphasis; #0a8050 filled white-text primary CTA; status semantics layered in (Active=green, Completed=blue #3e8cff/#e6f0ff, Cancelled=red #ea3943/#fde7e9, cliff/degraded warnings=amber #b25e09/#fff4e5). Signature visual = the line-chart/heartbeat 'pulse' glyph used in the hero badge and the created-streams empty state (echoing 'streaming'), the two-stat hero strip, and the thin green release progress bars with tabular-nums figures.

## Neo Swap  `neo-swap`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Preview the live NEO/GAS cross-rate, slippage and minimum-received for a Neo N3 token swap, then settle in your wallet when an on-chain route is available.

**Purpose.** Neo Swap is a token-swap console for Neo N3 (NEO, GAS, NEP-17) that is honest about its current state: no on-chain swap router is deployed, so the always-available capability is a live planning quote pulled from the Morpheus data feed, not settlement. A user opens it to check the current NEO ↔ GAS exchange rate, tune slippage tolerance, see the exact minimum they would receive, and understand that the actual trade must be signed in their wallet or a DEX once a route exists. The design deliberately leads with the "Live price preview" so the working feature is the focus instead of a wall of disabled trade controls.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (router NOT deployed — current real state): The Swap card opens with a "Quote pending" route badge. If the wallet is disconnected it shows the "Preview a NEO ↔ GAS trade before you sign" intro block (icon badge + three labelled steps: "Live cross-rate" / "Adjustable slippage" / "Settle in your wallet") with a green block CTA "Connect wallet to preview" (dispatch connectWallet). Below/instead sits the "Live price preview" focus block: eyebrow "LIVE PRICE PREVIEW", an optional "Rate as of HH:MM" (or "Rate may be stale") timestamp, a big rate line that is either "Refresh to load the live rate", "Loading rate…", or "1 NEO buys X GAS", a body note explaining settlement is not enabled on this network yet, and a "Refresh rate" button (green/primary when connected, secondary otherwise; spins while loading). The full trade apparatus is folded behind a disclosure "Set up the trade (settles when a route is enabled)". Expanding it reveals: a From token field (token pill button NEO, amount NeoInput with placeholder "Enter amount", a "MAX" button, "Balance: …" line), a circular ArrowDownUp switch-tokens control, a To field relabelled "You receive (estimated)" showing a read-only computed figure (defaults "0.00") + balance, a Slippage Tolerance group (label + current value e.g. "0.5%", preset chips 0.1% / 0.5% / 1%, a custom "%" input, and a hint that turns into a "High slippage" warning above 1%), a 2-up detail panel ("Exchange Rate" / "Minimum Received"), a "Transaction details" sub-disclosure (Network: NEO N3 / Route: Direct NEO/GAS / Est. settlement: ~15s after wallet confirmation / Network fee: Paid in GAS at signing), a "Preview only — review every figure in your wallet before you sign" note, and a DISABLED "Settlement unavailable" button. Secondary: the right "Market / Popular Pairs" panel lists NEO/GAS and GAS/NEO as selectable rows (dispatch selectPair) which sets the active pair; a token-picker modal (dispatch openFromSelector/openToSelector) lists NEO and GAS with balances. Router-enabled flow (future/conditional): the same trade form renders inline (no disclosure), the badge reads "Route ready", and the bottom CTA becomes an active green "Swap NEO → GAS" that on click runs executeSwap (swapTokenInForTokenOut with sender, amount, minOutput, path, deadline), showing "Swapping…" then a success/error toast.

**Interface description.** Light "Neo Soft" theme, green accent. Three stacked regions on a #f7f8fb page. (1) Hero band: a rounded 20px card with a white→mint gradient (linear-gradient 135deg #ffffff→#f1fbf6), soft shadow. Left: a 46px mint rounded-square icon badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 ArrowRightLeft glyph); then green uppercase eyebrow "SWAP", a 28px/800 title "Neo Swap — live NEO/GAS rate & trade preview" (letter-spacing -0.02em), and a muted 14px subtitle ("Preview the live NEO ↔ GAS rate, slippage and minimum received — settle in your wallet/DEX when a route is available"). Below the head, a pair row: two white token chips ("N" circle + NEO) joined by a grey ArrowRight, ending in (G + GAS). (2) Two-column workspace (desktop grid ~0.66fr / 0.34fr min 310px). Left = the main Swap card (white, radius 20, hairline border rgba(20,22,38,0.06), shadow): section header with eyebrow "SWAP" + bold "NEO → GAS" on the left and a route-status pill on the right ("Quote pending" muted / "Syncing quote" / "Route ready" green). Inside, conditionally: the disconnected intro block (rounded inset, Wallet icon badge, three icon+label steps, green block CTA), the "Live price preview" block (eyebrow with TrendingUp icon, as-of/stale chip, large rate line, body copy, full-width Refresh button), and the "Set up the trade" disclosure summary row (SlidersHorizontal icon). Trade fields use #f7f8fa insets, rounded 14px; slippage uses pill chips (radius 9999) with an active green state; eyebrow micro-labels are 11px/700 uppercase .12em muted; figures are tabular-nums. Right column = "Market / Popular Pairs" card (eyebrow "MARKET" + bold "Popular Pairs", list of pair rows NEO/GAS, GAS/NEO with an active highlight). A centered modal dialog overlays for the token selector (NEO/GAS rows with first-letter circle, symbol, balance). Filled CTAs use #0a8050 with white text; disabled CTA is a muted secondary.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Disconnected / first-run (router unavailable)** — Hero band + Swap card showing the 'Quote pending' badge, the 'Preview a NEO ↔ GAS trade before you sign' intro block (Wallet badge, three steps: Live cross-rate / Adjustable slippage / Settle in your wallet) and green 'Connect wallet to preview' CTA; below it the 'Live price preview' block reading 'Refresh to load the live rate' with a secondary 'Refresh rate' button; the 'Set up the trade' disclosure collapsed. Right column shows the Market / Popular Pairs panel. This is the true current rendered state (matches the screenshot).
- **Loading rate** — After tapping Refresh rate: the preview block rate line shows 'Loading rate…' (muted), the Refresh button shows its loading/spinner state, route badge reads 'Syncing quote'.
- **Quote loaded (preview only)** — Preview block shows '1 NEO buys X.XXXXXX GAS' as a large figure with a 'Rate as of HH:MM' timestamp chip; badge still 'Quote pending'. If the trade disclosure is expanded: From/To fields, slippage chips with 0.5% active, Exchange Rate + Minimum Received populated, Transaction details rows, and the disabled 'Settlement unavailable' button.
- **Stale rate** — Quote present but old: the as-of chip turns into a 'Rate may be stale' warning state and the rate-source line reads '… as of HH:MM — may be out of date'; swap remains non-tradable.
- **Slippage tuning + high-slippage warning** — Trade form expanded with the Slippage Tolerance group: preset pills 0.1% / 0.5% / 1% plus a custom % input; selecting >1% flips the hint to the amber/warning 'High slippage — you may receive notably less than quoted' and Minimum Received drops accordingly.
- **Token selector modal** — Centered dialog overlay 'Select Token' (From/To context) listing NEO and GAS rows, each with a first-letter circle, symbol and 'Balance: …', plus an X close button.
- **Router-enabled ready state (future/conditional)** — When a router address exists: trade form renders inline (no disclosure), badge reads green 'Route ready', and an active green 'Swap NEO → GAS' CTA is shown — with a 'Swapping…' submitting state and success/error toast after execution.

**Visual identity.** Green default Neo Soft accent: bright #16c784 for decorative badges/chips, #0fb174 for the hero/eyebrow glyphs, #0a8050 filled CTAs (white text), soft #e4f8f0 mint fills, mint hero gradient (#ffffff→#f1fbf6). Signature visuals: the ArrowRightLeft swap badge, the NEO→GAS token-chip pair row joined by an arrow, the circular ArrowDownUp direction-switch control between From/To fields, and the route-status pill that shifts from muted "Quote pending" to green "Route ready". DeFi-honest tone — leads with a "Live price preview" rather than a teasing disabled swap.

## Neo Treasury  `neo-treasury`
*Category: defi/finance*

> A foundation-wallet transparency dashboard that tracks public Neo treasury balances in real time and lets an operator sign controlled NEP-17 payouts from their own connected wallet.

**Purpose.** Neo Treasury gives the Neo ecosystem a read-only window into the public on-chain balances of known foundation wallets (Da Hongfei and Erik Zhang groups), aggregating their NEO, GAS, and USD totals from live chain data with no placeholder numbers. Separately, it provides a "Disbursement console" where an operator can prepare and sign a real NEP-17 transfer — funded strictly by their own connected wallet, never the watched treasury — and verify the exact native contract, fixed-point amount, and recipient hash before signing. A user opens it to audit treasury holdings transparently and, optionally, to execute an authorized payout with full pre-signature visibility.

**Functional description.** Two parallel, independent flows. (A) TRANSPARENCY (read-only, loads automatically): on open, the app reads live mainnet balances for ~44 watched addresses across two founder groups, then renders aggregate Total USD / Total NEO / Total GAS in the hero and per-group cards below. USD comes from a Morpheus on-chain price feed (may be delayed/stale — disclosed via a note); if the feed is down, USD shows "—" and only native NEO/GAS render. A single "Refresh" pill beside the live-sync signal re-fetches; unreachable wallets surface a "{count} wallets unreachable" warning and failed reads show an em-dash, never a misleading 0. (B) DISBURSEMENT (write path, in the console card): the operator (1) optionally clicks "Connect Wallet"; (2) picks Asset via a GAS/NEO selector; (3) types an Amount (or taps a preset chip: 0.1 / 1 / 5 + the chosen asset suffix); (4) enters a Recipient address (caption clarifies "Any Neo address — paid from your wallet, not the watched treasury"), or clicks "Use as recipient" on any watched wallet row to auto-fill it; (5) optionally edits the Memo (default "treasury-disbursement"). As soon as amount + recipient are filled, a collapsible "Signing intent" panel derives and previews the exact Native contract, Fixed amount (8-decimal integer), Recipient Hash160, and Signer — auto-expanding in red with a "Fix payout details" message if the input is invalid. The primary CTA is staged: disconnected + empty = "Connect Wallet"; drafted-but-disconnected = "Connect & Sign Disbursement"; connected = "Sign Disbursement". On submit it dispatches a real NEP-17 transfer; on success a toast fires and a "Last tx" receipt shows the compacted txid plus "{amount} {asset} -> {recipient}". Status field cycles Draft ready -> Awaiting wallet signature -> Transfer submitted / Disbursement failed.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout on a light #f4f5f7 page, ~1200px wide, all cards white with hairline borders and soft shadows. TOP HERO BAND (white rounded card, two-column): left = round green "N" badge in soft-mint circle + eyebrow "FOUNDATION WALLET TRANSPARENCY" (11px/700 uppercase, tracked) + H2 "Neo Treasury" (24px/800, -0.02em) + description paragraph + a signal row ("● Live balances synced · Last updated …" with a small colored dot — green=live, amber=cached, grey=pending — and a quiet rounded "Refresh" pill pushed to the right) + a muted price-source disclosure note; right = a stacked metric panel of three inset (#f7f8fa) rows: "TOTAL USD" (large, e.g. 35,603,019), "NEO" (35,693,278), "GAS" (37,727,484.0074), right-aligned tabular-nums figures. DISBURSEMENT CONSOLE (white card, max 720px wide): section heading eyebrow "CONNECTED WALLET" + title "Disbursement console" + boundary subtext; a 3-up wallet strip of inset tiles ("FROM: YOUR CONNECTED WALLET" -> "Wallet required", "NETWORK" -> "Neo N3 Mainnet", "STATUS" -> "Draft ready"); a 2-column form grid — Asset select + Amount input (with asset suffix), then full-width Recipient input ("N…") with a muted caption, then full-width Memo textarea; a row of three pill preset chips "0.1 GAS / 1 GAS / 5 GAS" (soft-mint fill, green text); a collapsible "SIGNING INTENT / Waiting for payout details" disclosure (chevron) that expands to a 2x2 grid of mono-font dt/dd cards (Native contract, Fixed amount, Recipient Hash160, Signer); then the primary full-width green CTA ("Connect Wallet" filled #0a8050) plus an optional underlined "change wallet" text link; a brand-soft "LAST TX" receipt appears after signing. LOWER VIEWPORT: before balances load, a compact "WATCHLIST DATA: MAINNET / Watched groups" reference card listing "Da Hongfei — N addresses" and "Erik Zhang — N addresses"; once live, a "WATCHLIST DATA: MAINNET" tag over a 2-column grid of group cards. Each GROUP CARD shows the founder name + "22 addresses" pill, a NEO/GAS/USD dl, and a scrollable wallet list (each row: Wallet N label + compact mono address on the left; "NEO / GAS" balance + a "Use as recipient" pill on the right). FOOTER: slim "TRANSPARENCY AND PAYOUT ROUTE / 44 addresses" card reiterating the read-only boundary. Accent = Neo green (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 CTA, #086a45 green text, #e4f8f0 soft fills); radii 12/16/20px; eyebrows everywhere in muted uppercase.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First load / skeleton** — Hero metrics show 'Live data pending' / '—', grey signal dot reads 'Live data pending'; lower viewport shows the compact 'Watched groups' reference card (Da Hongfei / Erik Zhang address counts) and a loading state for the watchlist. Disbursement console fully usable, Status 'Draft ready', CTA 'Connect Wallet'.
- **Live & filled dashboard** — Balances resolved: hero shows green 'Live balances synced · Last updated …' dot, real Total USD/NEO/GAS figures; lower grid shows two founder group cards with NEO/GAS/USD and scrollable wallet rows each offering 'Use as recipient'. This is the primary screenshot state.
- **Payout drafted (intent preview)** — Operator has entered Asset/Amount/Recipient; 'Signing intent' panel is expanded showing 'NEP-17 transfer ready' with the 2x2 derived grid (Native contract, Fixed amount, Recipient Hash160, Signer). CTA reads 'Connect & Sign Disbursement' (disconnected) or 'Sign Disbursement' (connected).
- **Submitting / signing** — After tapping the CTA: Status tile shows 'Awaiting wallet signature', primary button in loading state, form locked. Captures the disbursementSubmitting moment.
- **Success receipt** — Post-signature: success toast 'Transfer submitted', brand-soft 'LAST TX' receipt card with compacted txid and the '{amount} {asset} -> recipient' line; Status 'Transfer submitted'.
- **Error / degraded states** — Mock (a) load timeout/empty: 'Public balances did not load' empty state + Retry; (b) price feed down: neutral note 'USD conversion paused — showing native NEO/GAS balances', USD '—'; (c) wallets unreachable warning; (d) invalid input: red-expanded intent panel 'Fix payout details'; (e) 'Disbursement failed' inline error.

**Visual identity.** Neo green default identity — soft-mint #e4f8f0 fills with #086a45/#0fb174 green text and a #0a8050 filled CTA. Signature visual is the round green "N" hero badge plus the tri-color live-sync status dot (green live / amber cached / grey pending). The product feel is institutional-transparency-meets-fintech: large tabular USD/NEO/GAS figures, mono-font address/hash chips, and a hard visual separation between the read-only transparency cards and the contained, max-720px write-path "Disbursement console".

## Neo X Bridge  `neo-x-bridge`
*Category: defi/finance*

> A unified AxLabs/BaneLabs console that prepares Neo N3 ↔ Neo X cross-chain handoffs — asset bridges, arbitrary messages, and a lifecycle preview — without moving any funds itself.

**Purpose.** Neo X Bridge is a "prepare, don't execute" console for the official Neo N3 ↔ Neo X bridge. A user opens it to shape a valid GAS bridge handoff, build an arbitrary cross-chain MessageBridge payload, or preview the source-to-destination lifecycle of an operation. It deliberately moves no funds — it emits a JSON intent the user then submits/signs on the official bridge app — so the design must constantly communicate this "handoff, not transfer" honesty (the hero disclaimer, the "Next step — bridge on the official app" card, and the "This console moves no funds" footnote). It targets two audiences in one flow: NEP-21/NeoLine users on Neo N3 and EVM/MetaMask users on Neo X.

**Functional description.** No wallet connect is required (walletRequired:false). The workspace is a single card with three pill tabs — Asset / Message / Track — that swap the form in place. ASSET tab: pick Direction (dropdown: "Neo N3 -> Neo X" or "Neo X -> Neo N3") → enter Amount (number input, suffix "GAS", GAS-only with chip + note "GAS only — this is the production token path for the native Neo X bridge"); three preset pills 0.1 GAS / 1 GAS / 5 GAS set the amount → enter Destination address (validated against the target chain: 0x… EVM for n3-to-neox, N… for neox-to-n3, with direction-specific error copy) → a live 3-row preview (Route / You bridge / Recipient, em-dash "—" until valid) → a "Costs & timing" panel (Bridge protocol fee: None, Network gas: Paid on each chain at submit, Settlement ETA: ~1–2 min after source confirmation, plus an estimate footnote) → press "Prepare asset handoff". MESSAGE tab: Direction → Target Contract (0x… or Neo script hash) → Target Method (default onCrossChainMessage) → Gas Limit (number, default 250000, min 21000) → Payload (textarea, e.g. {"type":"signal","value":"..."}) → preview (Route / Target / Payload=Ready) → "Prepare message intent". TRACK tab: Bridge Type (Asset/Message) → Direction → Operation ID (N3X-ASSET-…) → Source Tx (0x… 64-hex, validated) → preview (Bridge / Route / Source tx) → "Refresh tracking" (secondary button). The primary CTA is disabled until valid, with an inline gating hint below explaining what's missing; on submit it shows a loading state, then a success/error feedback strip. A successful prepare reveals a two-column output region: left "Prepared bridge intent" card (operation id + title, a <pre> JSON payload, a Copy button, and a green "Next step" block with "Open official bridge to submit" external link) and right "Lifecycle preview" card (a note that this is a local preview not live status, then a 5-step vertical timeline: Intent prepared → Source transaction → Bridge observation → Validator attestation → Destination finalized, each step done/active/waiting, plus a "View source tx on explorer" link when a valid hash exists). Below sit four resource links (Official Bridge, Asset Bridge Docs, MessageBridge Docs, BaneLabs SDK) and a "Recent operations" list (kind chip + title + compact digest hash).

**Interface description.** Light "Neo Soft" finance theme, green accent #16c784, comfortable density, console shell. Top is a two-column HERO band on a faint mint-tinted rounded card: left column has a green square icon badge (ArrowLeftRight glyph), eyebrow "AXLABS / BANELABS BRIDGE CONSOLE" (11px/700 uppercase, tracked), H2 title "Neo N3 and Neo X cross-chain control" (~26px/800, -0.02em), a body paragraph, and an amber/soft disclaimer note "No funds move from this console — it only prepares a handoff you submit on the official bridge." Right column is a "How the bridge moves GAS" route card with two white nodes — "SEND FROM / Neo N3 / NEP-21 · NeoLine" and "RECEIVE ON / Neo X / EVM · MetaMask" — joined by a small ArrowLeftRight. Below the hero is a thin full-width METRICS STRIP with white cells: Route (e.g. "Neo N3 -> Neo X"), Bridge Type ("Asset Bridge"), Status ("Bridge console ready"), and a Digest cell (mono, compacted) that only appears once a real digest exists. The main WORKSPACE is a white rounded card titled with kicker "BRIDGE WORKSPACE" + H3 "Build cross-chain handoff" and a right-aligned 3-pill tablist (Asset / Message / Track) where the active pill is filled. Forms use a responsive grid: select fields, NeoInput text/number/textarea fields on #f7f8fa insets with hairline borders, preset pills, a compact key/value preview block, and the bordered "Costs & timing" definition list. The primary button is a full-width filled green CTA (#0a8050 text white); Track uses a secondary style. Output region is a 2-column grid of white cards (intent JSON in monospace pre, green "Next step" handoff block; lifecycle timeline with circular state icons — green CheckCircle for done, Clock for active, dashed circle for waiting). Footer: a row of four pill resource links each with an external-link icon, then a "Recent operations" card. Cards use 12–20px radius and soft shadows; eyebrows/labels uppercase muted.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (Asset tab)** — Default load shown in screenshot: hero + route card, metrics strip reading Route 'Neo N3 -> Neo X' / Bridge Type 'Asset Bridge' / Status 'Bridge console ready' (no Digest cell). Asset form has direction set, empty amount (placeholder 0.1) with GAS chip + 'GAS only' note, three preset pills, empty destination field, preview rows all '—', Costs & timing panel visible, the green 'Prepare asset handoff' CTA disabled with the gating hint 'Enter a positive GAS amount and a valid destination address for the selected chain to continue.' No output/timeline/recent cards yet.
- **Asset filled / ready** — Amount entered (or preset selected, e.g. 1 GAS pill active), valid destination address; preview shows Route / You bridge '1 GAS' / Recipient; CTA enabled and filled green, gating hint hidden.
- **Message tab** — Workspace swapped to Message form: Direction, Target Contract (0x…/script hash), Target Method (onCrossChainMessage), Gas Limit (250000), Payload textarea with JSON placeholder, preview Route/Target/Payload, 'Prepare message intent' CTA + its gating hint when incomplete.
- **Track tab** — Track form: Bridge Type + Direction selects, Operation ID (N3X-ASSET-…) and Source Tx (0x…) inputs with hash validation error state, preview Bridge/Route/Source tx, secondary 'Refresh tracking' button.
- **Submitting / loading** — CTA in loading state (spinner, disabled) while the intent is prepared; rest of form locked-feeling.
- **Success / prepared result** — Success feedback strip ('Asset bridge handoff prepared.'), then the 2-column output: left 'Prepared bridge intent' card with operation id + title, JSON <pre>, Copy button, and green 'Next step — bridge on the official app' block with 'Open official bridge to submit' link; right 'Lifecycle preview' card with preview-note + 5-step timeline (mixed done/active/waiting states) and 'View source tx on explorer' link; below, resource link row and 'Recent operations' list with kind chip + title + digest.
- **Error / invalid input** — Field-level errors (e.g. wrong-chain address: 'This direction settles on Neo X. Enter a Neo X (0x...) address.', gas limit < 21000, malformed source tx) and a red action-feedback strip when a prepare/track call fails.

**Visual identity.** Default green brand accent #16c784 (bright decorative badges/chips/icon fills, #0a8050 filled CTA, #0b5e3e green text, #e4f8f0 soft fill). Finance/DeFi family, comfortable density. Signature visuals: the bidirectional ArrowLeftRight (⇄) glyph used in the hero badge, the route card connector, and throughout — it is the app's emblem; the two-node "SEND FROM → RECEIVE ON" route card mapping Neo N3 (NEP-21/NeoLine) to Neo X (EVM/MetaMask); the 5-stage lifecycle timeline with done/active/waiting state dots; an amber/soft "no funds move" disclaimer that gives the console its honest, advisory tone. Use the em-dash "—" as the empty-value placeholder. No secondary theme override — this app stays on the standard green.
