## NeoPay Shared Runtime  `neo-pay-shared-example`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Create and manage funded, time-released payment streams (payroll, vesting, contributor pay) in GAS or NEO through the shared MiniApp vault runtime.

**Purpose.** NeoPay Shared Runtime lets a user lock GAS or NEO into a payment "stream" that releases to a recipient on a fixed daily schedule over a chosen duration — the on-chain primitive behind payroll, contributor pay, and token vesting. It is the reference example for composing the shared funding-vault and vesting modules through the platform runtime. A user opens it to fund a new stream to someone, to watch streams they created release over time (and cancel to reclaim what is still unreleased), or to claim funds from streams where they are the beneficiary.

**Functional description.** Primary flow — Create Stream: the user fills the composer with Amount (number, suffix shows the chosen token, placeholder "0.00") and toggles the asset with a two-option segmented control GAS ("Fees + streams") / NEO ("Whole-token streams"); enters Recipient Address (Neo N3 base58, validated against /^N.../; inline error "Enter a valid Neo N3 address") and Duration in days (1–365, suffix "days", error "Use 1 to 365 days"); optionally opens a "Stream details" disclosure to add a Stream name ("Monthly payroll stream") and Notes (optional). Three preset chips prefill the whole form: "7d GAS" (0.7 GAS / 7d / "Weekly contributor stream"), "30d GAS" (20 GAS / 30d / "Monthly payroll stream"), "90d NEO" (90 NEO / 90d / "Quarterly NEO vesting stream"); the active preset is highlighted. As the user types, a live "Transaction preview" disclosure derives the exact on-chain schedule and shows: Recipient (shortened N…), Total amount, Release per day / Release per interval, Interval (e.g. "7 days"), Network (Neo N3 Testnet/Mainnet), and Network fee ("Estimated in GAS at signing"). The primary CTA reads "Complete stream details" while invalid and becomes "Create Stream" only when recipient+amount+duration are valid AND the derived per-interval rate doesn't round to zero (otherwise an inline alert: "Amount is too small for this duration — increase the amount or shorten the duration."). A secondary "Clear" button appears once the draft is dirty. Submitting locks the funds into the stream on-chain. Secondary actions on existing streams: on a created/outgoing stream, "Cancel" (danger) reclaims unreleased funds; on an incoming stream where you're the beneficiary, "Claim" (green/success) withdraws released funds — each button shows a per-row loading spinner and disables when the stream is cancelled/completed. Streams hydrate from chain into two lists; a "Stream index unavailable" notice appears if the indexing service is down.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout on a light theme (the screenshot shows no separate right rail — everything is full-width cards). Top: a soft mint-tinted HERO band with a small circular green wave/stream glyph badge, an eyebrow "SHARED RUNTIME" (11px uppercase, tracked), title "NeoPay shared streams" (~24-30px/800), subtitle "Create a funded payment stream through the shared vault and vesting modules.", and a 4-up stat row rendered as label-over-figure pairs: TOTAL STREAMS, ACTIVE, CREATED BY YOU, YOU'RE BENEFICIARY (figures bold tabular-nums; all show a "—"/0 empty treatment with an "Awaiting activity" note before any activity). Below: the white "Create Stream" card (NeoCard, erobo variant) containing the composer — a large Amount field with a GAS suffix on the right, the GAS/NEO segmented switch (each option stacks the symbol bold over a hint line), a 2-column compact row (Recipient Address | Duration), a collapsible "Stream details" section (Stream name | Notes (optional), shown as a full-width textarea-like Notes input in the screenshot), then a row of three pill preset chips (7d GAS / 30d GAS / 90d NEO). Beneath sits the inset "Complete stream details" full-width primary button (disabled grey state in screenshot) and a collapsible "Transaction preview" row showing a left label and right value ("Enter details" placeholder when invalid). Final card: "How streaming payments work" — a numbered 3-step list with small green circular number badges (1,2,3) and a muted footnote "Your created and incoming streams will appear here once the first one is on-chain." When streams exist, the how-to card is replaced by a 2-up grid of "Your Created Streams (n)" and "Streams You Receive (n)" cards, each listing stream rows with title + colored status pill, meta line (From/To · total · duration · per-day rate), a green progress bar with "released / total TOKEN (xx%)" copy, an optional Claimable callout, and the Cancel/Claim action. Colors: Neo Soft light surfaces (white cards, #f7f8fa insets, hairline borders), green brand accent #16c784 (badge, progress fill, number chips), #0a8050 filled CTA, success-green Claim and danger-red Cancel buttons; pill chips/buttons at 9999px radius, cards 16-20px radius with soft shadows.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (How it works)** — Hero with all four stat figures at 0 and an 'Awaiting activity' note; empty Create Stream form (placeholders only, CTA reads 'Complete stream details' and is disabled, no Clear button, Transaction preview collapsed showing 'Enter details'); below it the 'How streaming payments work' card with the 1-2-3 numbered steps and footnote. This is the current screenshot state.
- **Filled / ready to submit** — A preset chip (e.g. '30d GAS') is active and highlighted; Amount '20' with GAS suffix, recipient filled (valid, no error), Duration '30 days', Stream details disclosure open with a name; the Transaction preview is auto-expanded showing Recipient (N…), Total 20 GAS, Release per day, Interval '30 days', Network, Network fee; primary CTA now reads 'Create Stream' (green, enabled) with a 'Clear' secondary button beside it.
- **Validation error / too-small amount** — Inline field errors (invalid Neo N3 address under Recipient, 'Use 1 to 365 days' under Duration) and/or the red alert under the preview: 'Amount is too small for this duration — increase the amount or shorten the duration.'; CTA stays disabled at 'Complete stream details'.
- **Submitting** — Create Stream button in loading/spinner state and disabled (isCreating); form fields locked; preview still visible.
- **Populated dashboard (streams exist)** — Hero stats now show real counts; the how-to card is replaced by the 2-up grid: 'Your Created Streams (n)' with outgoing rows (To: N…, total, per-day rate, green progress bar with percent, red Cancel button) and 'Streams You Receive (n)' with incoming rows (From: N…, Claimable callout, green Claim button). Include a per-row loading state for an in-flight Claim/Cancel.
- **Service notice / list unavailable** — A status banner above the form: bold 'Stream index unavailable' with the service-notice message, while the Create form below stays fully usable.
- **Mobile 390px** — Single column: hero badge+title stack with the 4 stats wrapping to 2x2; Amount + GAS/NEO switch stacked; Recipient and Duration stack vertically; preset chips wrap; full-width CTA; stream cards stack one per row.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand accent (#16c784 bright for the hero stream-glyph badge, progress-bar fill, and the 1/2/3 step number chips; #0a8050 filled primary CTA). The hero band carries a subtle mint wash. Signature visual is the streaming-payment motif: a horizontal wave/pulse line icon in the hero badge and a green left-to-right progress bar on each stream card expressing funds releasing over time. Action color coding: green/success for Claim, red/danger for Cancel. Pill chips for presets and the GAS/NEO segmented asset switch.

## ProfitAnchor  `profitanchor`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Stake whole NEO into a yield-maximizing anchor pool, then claim the GAS your stake earns from 21 anchor-agent council votes.

**Purpose.** ProfitAnchor is a user-facing NEO staking app on the "defi" category. A user deposits whole NEO into the ProfitAnchor pool; that NEO is voted for Neo council candidates by 21 AA "anchor agents" under a profit-maximizing policy, and the GAS those votes generate is funded into a reward reserve and split across stakers pro-rata by share of total staked NEO. The user opens it to stake NEO, watch their pending GAS rewards accrue, claim that GAS, and redeem their NEO. The app is deliberate about honesty: it pays a variable rate (never a fixed APR) and only accrues yield when the reserve is funded. It is a distinct pool from TrustAnchor, and its selected route is readable on-chain by SelfLoan without ceding custody.

**Functional description.** Single "Overview" tab; wallet required. Core flow: (1) Connect a wallet (until then all metrics show "—" and a hint reads "Your stake, rewards and rate appear here once you connect a wallet."). (2) In the "Stake, redeem, or claim" panel, enter a NEO amount in the single number field labeled "NEO amount" (default value "1", step 1, min 1). The field enforces WHOLE NEO ONLY — NEO is indivisible, decimals are rejected; the hint "Whole NEO only — NEO is indivisible, no decimals." sits under the input, and an aria-live inline status line below shows either "{n} NEO" + "Whole NEO amount is ready for stake or redeem." (green/ready) or "Whole NEO required" + "NEO cannot be fractional. Use a positive whole number." (red/blocked). (3) A "Preview / Review the wallet effect" preflight card summarizes the pending transaction as a checklist (dl): "Whole NEO" → the planned amount or "Blocked"; "AA route" → selected agent "#id" or "Awaiting route" (this row is informational only — staking does NOT require a selected route, noted by "Informational — a selected route is not required to stake."); "Claimable GAS" → the pending GAS display or "No claimable GAS". Below the checklist two note lines explain: "NEO transfer to PlatformAnchor" — "Stake submits a NEO transfer with memo `stake:miniapp-profitanchor`"; and the claim plan ("GAS ready to claim"/"No claimable GAS" with copy). When no wallet/chain data is loaded the preflight shows "Preview only — connect a wallet to stake." (4) Primary action: full-width green "Stake NEO" button (disabled until amount is valid; label becomes "Submitting..." in flight) — submits a NEO transfer to the PlatformAnchor contract. (5) Two secondary ghost buttons in a 2-up row: "Redeem NEO" (withdraws whole NEO from the stake) and "Claim GAS" (disabled unless pending rewards > 0, claims accumulated GAS). (6) A small round refresh icon button (top-right of the action panel) re-reads on-chain status. (7) Conditional "Recover NEO" card appears ONLY when a bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit: shows "Claimable NEO" label, the pending-withdraw amount, hint "A bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit.", and a green "Recover NEO" button. After any action a status strip shows a live message ("Ready for wallet action" / "Submitting..." / "Status refreshed" / error text) and, when present, "Last tx: {truncated txid}". A "Recent actions" list (last 4) shows action name, amount or status, and truncated txid. All amount actions normalize/truncate to whole NEO before dispatch. NOTE: 21-agent routing/rebalancing is NOT in this user app — it lives in ProfitAnchor Admin; here the route is read-only context.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling column of stacked cards (the app renders one workspace column, not a 2-col split), ~16px page gaps, all on white #ffffff over a white body. From top: (1) HERO card — soft green radial wash (#e4f8f0) in the top-left corner, a 46px round soft-green badge holding a line "anchor" glyph (lollipop/anchor: small circle on a stem with a curved arc base), eyebrow "PROFITANCHOR" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em, muted), title "Yield-maximizing NEO staking" (28px/800, -0.02em ink), a ~560px description paragraph, then two pill "hero facts" on the subtle #f7f8fa fill: one with a green dot + "Awaiting route" (or "Route selected"), one reading "21/21 AA agents registered for ProfitAnchor." (count is N/21). (2) VARIABLE REWARD RATE band — bordered card with a green-tinted top-right radial wash; left column has green eyebrow "VARIABLE REWARD RATE" + honesty copy; right column is a right-aligned figure: tiny uppercase label "RESERVE PER NEO", a big 30px/800 green number with a small "GAS / NEO" unit suffix (or "—" placeholder), and a small caption "Reward reserve ÷ total staked NEO — variable and not guaranteed." (3) STAT GRID — four stat chips (2×2 on narrow, 4-across ≥860px), each a white rounded card with a 20px/800 green tabular figure over an 11px uppercase muted label: "My stake", "Claimable GAS", "Reward Reserve", and "GAS / NEO (cumulative)" (this last chip adds a 10px caption "Cumulative GAS distributed per NEO since launch — not a current rate."). Before connect all four show "—" in muted color with the hint line beneath. (4) "HOW YIELD WORKS" earn card — green diagonal gradient wash, green eyebrow title, a 3-item bullet list (NEO voted by 21 AA agents → GAS funded to reserve → split pro-rata; yield variable, no fixed APR), and a top-bordered composability note about SelfLoan reading the route without custody transfer. (5) ACTION card "Stake, redeem, or claim" — section head with eyebrow "WALLET ACTIONS" + 18px/700 title, plus a 38px round refresh icon button at right; then the NEO amount field, whole-NEO hint, inline ready/blocked status, the "PREVIEW / Review the wallet effect" preflight sub-panel (subtle #f7f8fa inset, checklist + memo/claim notes), the full-width green "Stake NEO" CTA, a top-bordered 2-up row of outlined "Redeem NEO" / "Claim GAS" ghost buttons, the optional Recover NEO inset, a status strip (subtle pill with a status dot + message + optional last-tx code; turns red/danger on error), and a "RECENT ACTIONS" list. Identity: Neo Soft light theme, single green hue — bright #16c784 for decorative dots/badges, #0fb174 for figures/eyebrows, deep #0b5e3e for green text needing AA contrast, #0a8050 (hover #086a45) for the filled white-text CTA, soft #e4f8f0 for fills; radii 12/16/20px, soft shadows 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,.06); danger red #ea3943 on #fde7e9 for invalid/error states.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / not connected** — Wallet not connected. Hero, rate band figure, and all four stat chips render the muted '—' placeholder; the hint 'Your stake, rewards and rate appear here once you connect a wallet.' sits under the stat grid. Preflight shows the 'Preview only — connect a wallet to stake.' note. Amount field pre-filled '1', inline status green-ready, Stake button enabled (preview), Claim GAS disabled. Hero fact pill reads 'Awaiting route'.
- **Connected / ready to stake** — On-chain data loaded. Stat chips show real figures (e.g. My stake '12 NEO', Claimable GAS '0 GAS', Reward Reserve, cumulative GAS/NEO); rate band shows a real 'Reserve per NEO' number with 'GAS / NEO' unit. Amount field holds a valid whole number, inline status green ('12 NEO — Whole NEO amount is ready…'), preflight checklist rows resolve (Whole NEO ready, AA route #id or informational, Claimable GAS). Full-width green 'Stake NEO' CTA active.
- **Invalid amount / blocked** — User typed a fractional or non-positive value. Amount input border turns danger-red with #fde7e9 fill (aria-invalid), inline status line turns red: 'Whole NEO required' + 'NEO cannot be fractional. Use a positive whole number.', preflight 'Whole NEO' row shows 'Blocked', and the Stake / Redeem buttons are disabled.
- **Submitting** — Transaction in flight. The pressed button label switches to 'Submitting...', all action buttons + refresh + amount field are disabled, and the status strip reads 'Submitting...' with the neutral status dot.
- **Success / claimable rewards** — After a successful action: status strip shows a success message (e.g. 'Stake transaction submitted' / 'Status refreshed') with 'Last tx: 0x1234ab…cd' code, and the 'Recent actions' list lists the latest entries (action name, amount or status, truncated txid). When pending GAS > 0 the 'Claim GAS' button enables and the claim-plan note reads 'GAS ready to claim'.
- **Error** — Action failed. Status strip switches to the danger variant (coral #ea3943 text on #fde7e9 fill, red status dot) showing the error message (e.g. 'Action failed' / 'PlatformAnchor contract is not configured' / 'NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number').
- **Recover NEO credit** — A bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit (pendingWithdraw > 0). The conditional Recover card appears inside the action panel: 'CLAIMABLE NEO' label, the amount in bold, hint 'A bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit.', and a green 'Recover NEO' button.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme on white, single green hue used with disciplined contrast tiers: decorative bright brand #16c784 (hero/status dots, badge ring), #0fb174 for stat figures and eyebrows, deep #0b5e3e for green body/label text that must meet WCAG AA on light surfaces, and #0a8050 (hover #086a45) for the one filled white-text CTA. Soft brand fill #e4f8f0 powers corner radial washes on the hero, rate band, and earn card. Signature visual = the round soft-green "anchor" badge glyph (a small circle on a vertical stem over a curved arc — an anchor/lollipop motif) tying the name to the mark, plus the honest variable-rate band (big 30px green "GAS / NEO" figure framed as 'Reserve per NEO', never an APR). Danger red #ea3943 on #fde7e9 for invalid-amount and error states. Rounded 12/16/20px cards, pill buttons/tabs, soft shadows.

## SelfLoan  `self-loan`
*Category: defi/finance*

> Lock NEO as collateral, borrow GAS instantly at a chosen LTV tier, and repay anytime — collateral held in custody with no liquidation and no interest.

**Purpose.** SelfLoan is a self-custodial DeFi lending miniapp where a user locks NEO collateral and borrows GAS against it. Unlike market lending protocols, there is NO liquidation, NO interest, and NO minimum lock — the borrow size is computed from the locked NEO value at a tiered loan-to-value ratio (20% / 30% / 40%), minus a flat 0.5% origination fee that the lending pool keeps. The collateral simply sits in the SelfLoan contract and is returned in full the moment the GAS debt is repaid. A user opens it to get GAS liquidity without selling their NEO, accepting that the NEO->GAS rate is operator-set (not a live oracle) in exchange for guaranteed no-liquidation safety.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (Borrow tab): connect Neo N3 wallet -> the "Your Balance: N NEO" hint and platform stats hydrate -> type a NEO amount into "Collateral Amount" (NEO suffix, whole-number NEO) -> pick one of three LTV tier pills: Conservative 20% / Balanced 30% / Aggressive 40% (one is always selected; default Conservative) -> the panel live-previews "Rate: 1 NEO = {price}", "Pool available: {x} GAS", and "Estimated Borrow (after fee): {y} GAS" (computed as collateral x neoPrice x LTV%, minus 0.5% fee), plus an "Origination fee (0.5%): {z} GAS" line and the operator-rate disclosure note -> an "LTV Ratio" gauge bar fills to the chosen % (green->amber->red, Conservative<->Aggressive endpoints) -> press "Borrow" (full-width green CTA) -> deposit-then-borrow runs and GAS disburses to the wallet ("Loan taken — GAS disbursed to your wallet"). One loan per address: when a loan is active the Borrow CTA is replaced by a lock-icon notice "One loan per address — repay or add collateral to your active loan first".

Once a loan exists, three more cards appear above/around the form: (1) "Your Loan" status card showing Collateral (NEO), Borrowed (GAS), Current LTV, and a Health Factor gauge with a colored bar and endpoint labels "Under-collateralized" <-> [Safe/Caution/Danger live status] <-> "Safe"; (2) "Repay Loan" card — GAS amount input with a "Max" chip that fills the exact outstanding debt, then a green Repay button (repaying caps at debt, refunds excess, and releases collateral on full repayment); (3) "Add Collateral" card — shows "Available: N NEO", an "Additional Collateral" NEO input, and a secondary "Add Collateral" button.

Recovery / edge actions: a "Pending confirmation" card appears when a borrow/repay tx was broadcast but its on-chain confirmation was not observed (tells the user not to resubmit). Two reclaim cards appear only if the deposit-then-act model stranded funds: "Reclaim Collateral" (NEO deposited but never borrowed against) and "Reclaim Repay Credit" (GAS deposited but never applied), each showing a "Reclaimable: {amount}" figure and a secondary reclaim button. There is also a secondary "Stats" tab (bar-chart icon) surfacing the platform/loan stat grid.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling column of stacked cards on a light theme, green-accented. TOP HERO BAND: a soft mint gradient rounded card spanning full width. Left: a circular white badge holding a green safe/vault line icon, then eyebrow "SELF LOAN" (11px uppercase tracked muted), title "SelfLoan" (~28px/800), subtitle "Tiered LTV self-loans with auto-repayment". Right: three plain stat tiles in a row — "0 / TOTAL LOANS", "0.00 / TOTAL BORROWED", "0.00 / TOTAL REPAID" (figure 18-24px/800 tabular over an uppercase muted label).

WORKSPACE (below hero, white rounded cards radius 16-20 with hairline border + soft shadow):
- "Borrow" card (always present). Section title top-left. Rows in order: balance hint line "Your Balance: 0 NEO" (NEO value rendered in green/bold, right-aligned); "Collateral Amount" labeled number input, placeholder 0.00, NEO suffix; an LTV TIER SELECTOR — three equal-width pill/button tiles each stacking a label over a bold percent (Conservative / 20%, Balanced / 30%, Aggressive / 40%), the active one filled with soft-green tint + green border (screenshot shows Conservative active); then right-aligned key/value hint lines "Rate: 1 NEO = {price}", "Pool available: 0.00 GAS", "Estimated Borrow (after fee): 0.00 GAS", and conditionally "Origination fee (0.5%): {z} GAS"; a small muted note "Rate is set by the operator (not a live market oracle); the 0.5% origination fee is retained by the lending pool."; an "LTV RATIO" gauge block on a #f7f8fa inset — uppercase label left, bold percent right (e.g. 20%), a thin track with a green->risk-color fill, and "CONSERVATIVE" / "AGGRESSIVE" endpoint micro-labels. CTA zone: when disconnected, a soft-mint full-width notice row with a lock icon and "Connect wallet to use Self Loan" (centered); when connected+no loan, a full-width green "Borrow" button; when loan active, the lock/info notice instead. Footer of the card: a soft-mint "COLLATERAL CUSTODY" disclosure block — eyebrow then "Returned in full on repayment · No third-party voting".
- "Your Loan" card (only with active loan): 3 inline status items (Collateral NEO / Borrowed GAS / Current LTV) then a Health Factor gauge (label + colored value, colored track bar, three endpoint markers).
- "Repay Loan" card: GAS input + "Max" chip + green Repay button.
- "Add Collateral" card: Available hint + NEO input + secondary Add Collateral button.
- Conditional "Pending confirmation", "Reclaim Collateral", "Reclaim Repay Credit" cards (secondary buttons, info copy).
Colors: green brand (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 filled CTA, soft #e4f8f0 chips/notices); risk semantics use AA-safe greens/amber/red (#0a6e4a / #a15c07 / #b91c1c) for LTV and health text. Loading overlay = centered spinner + "Loading...".

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / disconnected** — Hero with zeroed stats (0 / 0.00 / 0.00). Borrow card visible with empty Collateral Amount (0.00), Conservative 20% tier preselected, Your Balance: 0 NEO, Pool available 0.00 GAS, Estimated Borrow 0.00 GAS, LTV Ratio gauge at 20%, and the soft-mint lock-icon 'Connect wallet to use Self Loan' notice in place of the CTA. Custody disclosure footer present. No loan/repay/add-collateral cards yet.
- **Connected, ready to borrow (no loan)** — Wallet connected: Your Balance shows real NEO, Rate/Pool/Estimated-Borrow lines populated with live numbers, a selected tier (e.g. Balanced 30%), LTV gauge filled to that %, Origination fee line visible, and a full-width green 'Borrow' button enabled (disabled/greyed until an amount is typed).
- **Borrowing / submitting** — Borrow button in loading state (spinner, disabled); inputs retained. Optionally the full-screen 'Loading...' spinner overlay during state hydration.
- **Active loan dashboard** — Hero stats incremented. 'Your Loan' card shows Collateral NEO / Borrowed GAS / Current LTV plus a Health Factor gauge (colored bar + Safe/Caution/Danger status between 'Under-collateralized' and 'Safe' markers). Borrow CTA replaced by info notice 'One loan per address — repay or add collateral to your active loan first'. 'Repay Loan' card (GAS input + Max chip + green Repay) and 'Add Collateral' card (Available + NEO input + secondary button) shown.
- **Pending confirmation** — A 'Pending confirmation' card with body copy explaining the loan/repay tx was submitted but its on-chain confirmation hasn't been observed — 'Don't resubmit — refresh in a moment to see the updated position.'
- **Reclaim / stranded-funds recovery** — 'Reclaim Collateral' and/or 'Reclaim Repay Credit' card(s) showing explanatory copy, a 'Reclaimable: {amount}' figure, and a secondary reclaim button — shown only when a deposit landed but its second step never completed.
- **Error / insufficient pool** — Inline failure feedback such as 'Pool can't cover this borrow — only {pool} GAS available. Try a lower amount or LTV tier.', 'Insufficient NEO balance', or 'NEO amount must be a whole number'.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft green default theme (no secondary palette). Brand green #16c784 decorative / #0a8050 filled CTAs / soft mint #e4f8f0 for notices, chips and the hero gradient. Signature visuals: (1) a circular white hero badge holding a green safe/vault line icon (lock-box motif reinforcing 'custody, no liquidation'); (2) the three-pill LTV tier selector (Conservative/Balanced/Aggressive at 20/30/40%) as the central choice control; (3) two horizontal gauge bars — an 'LTV RATIO' bar (green->amber->red along Conservative->Aggressive) and a 'Health Factor' bar that shifts color green/amber/red with Safe/Caution/Danger labeling. Risk text uses AA-contrast deep green/amber/red. Overall feel: calm, reassuring, finance-grade — emphasizing safety (no liquidation) over aggressive trading energy.

# Social

## Breakup Contract  `breakup-contract`
*Category: social*

> A stake-backed promise between two people: both lock matching GAS, honor it and both get refunded in full, break it and your stake goes to your partner.

**Purpose.** Breakup Contract turns a personal commitment into an on-chain pact with real skin in the game.

**Functional description.** PRIMARY FLOW (create): The user fills the "New Contract" form top-to-bottom — (1) Partner Address: a NEO address field, placeholder "N...", inline-validated against /^N[0-9a-zA-Z]{33}$/ (error "Invalid partner address"; cannot be your own address). (2) Stake: a numeric GAS input (suffix "GAS", min 1, placeholder "5") with three quick-pick preset chips "1 GAS / 5 GAS / 10 GAS" beneath; selecting a chip fills the field and highlights it green. (3) Duration: a numeric "Days" input (suffix "Days", min 30, placeholder "90") with preset chips "30 Days / 90 Days / 365 Days" (90 is the default). Stake and Duration sit side-by-side in a 2-up row. (4) Contract Title: text, required, placeholder "Our covenant", live counter "0/100 characters" (error "Title must be 100 characters or less"). (5) Terms: a multi-line textarea, optional, placeholder "Optional notes, expectations, and exit rules", counter "0/2000 characters". The primary CTA "Create Contract" is full-width and stays disabled until the form is valid; below it a single live helper line names the first unmet requirement and updates as the user fills fields: "Add your partner's NEO address to begin." -> "Set a stake of at least 1 GAS for each of you." -> "Choose a term of at least 30 days." -> "Give your pact a title so you'll both recognise it." -> ready state "Looks good - create your pact whenever you're ready." (turns green). On submit the button enters a loading state; on success the form clears and a green status note appears in the Contracts card ("Last submitted: {title}"). SECONDARY ACTIONS (per contract card, role/status-gated): Sign Contract (named partner matches the stake on a pending pact, primary green button), Cancel & reclaim (creator only, while still pending, ghost button - reclaims their locked stake), Break Contract (either party while active and not yet expired, red/danger button - forfeits the breaker's stake to the other side), Settle (any caller on an honored expired pact, green success button - permissionlessly refunds both stakes). RECOVERY: a "Withdraw your GAS" card appears only when the wallet has recoverable credit (a deposit that landed without its pact completing, or a break payout / settlement refund owed); it shows the credit balance ("{amount} GAS") and a "Withdraw GAS" button. ON-CHAIN RESULTS reflected in UI: status transitions pending -> active (after partner signs) -> broken / ended / cancelled; the hero metrics strip and stats recount; GAS movements are settled by the contract (forfeit, full refund, or reclaim).

**Interface description.** Single centered column (max ~1040px) of white rounded cards (radius 20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba) on a subtle #f7f8fa canvas — NOT a 2-column workspace. TOP: a hero card with a faint mint-to-white diagonal gradient containing, left-to-right: a 60px soft-mint rounded-square icon badge holding the custom "PactGlyph" SVG (two heads leaning together over a shared white heart, green gradient tile), then a copy block with green uppercase eyebrow "A PROMISE, ON-CHAIN" (11px/700/.12em), title "Breakup Contract" (22px/800/-0.02em ink), subtitle "Put skin in the game for the people you care about."; and right-aligned a row of three green pill value-prop chips "STAKE-BACKED / ON-CHAIN / REFUNDABLE". Once contracts exist, a single compact metrics line sits at the hero foot: "{n} Active · {n} Pending · {n} Broken · {n} Total" (figures 16px/800 green, divided by thin separators). NEXT: the "New Contract" card — section title (18px/700), a body description "Draft a stake-backed relationship agreement with a partner, duration, and terms before confirming the wallet intent.", then a highlighted economics note in a soft-mint box with a 3px green left rail: "Two people, one promise: you each lock a matching GAS stake. Honor it to the end and both stakes come back in full — break it, and yours goes to your partner." Below it the form fields described above, with preset chips (pill, radius 999px, active = mint fill + green text), the full-width "Create Contract" CTA (disabled = desaturated mint #9fd9c1 solid + white text, enabled = full brand green), and the centered helper line. EMPTY STATE filler: a "How it works" card with a numbered 1-2-3 list (green circular number badges): "Both partners stake / If someone breaks it / If it runs its term" with one-line explanations. CONTRACTS card (rendered only when there are pacts or a notice): each pact is an inset card with a header row (title or "#id" + an uppercase status chip — Active=mint/green, Pending=amber, Broken/danger=mint, Ended/Cancelled=ghost grey), a 3-up meta grid of labeled inset tiles (PARTNER = truncated mono address, STAKE = "{n} GAS", DAYS LEFT = "{n} Days"), an optional terms paragraph, an optional amber "device-local terms" warning note for partners, and an action button row. Color identity: Neo Soft GREEN throughout (#16c784 bright, #0fb174 strong, #e4f8f0 soft, white surfaces, hairline borders) — no red/pink hero despite the "breakup" theme; amber is reserved only for warning/pending tones.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero (badge + 'A PROMISE, ON-CHAIN' eyebrow + title + subtitle + STAKE-BACKED/ON-CHAIN/REFUNDABLE chips) with NO metrics line; the 'New Contract' form fully empty with placeholders, economics note shown, disabled mint CTA, helper 'Add your partner's NEO address to begin.'; and the 'How it works' 1-2-3 explainer card filling the lower viewport. No Contracts card yet.
- **Form filling / validation** — Partial input showing inline field errors (e.g. 'Invalid partner address', 'Stake must be at least 1 GAS and duration at least 30 days', title counter approaching 100), an active preset chip highlighted green, CTA still disabled, and the live helper line naming the current first unmet requirement.
- **Ready to create** — All fields valid: a chosen partner address, '5 GAS' preset active, '90 Days' active, a title with counter (e.g. '12/100 characters'), optional terms filled; CTA now full brand green and enabled, helper line green 'Looks good - create your pact whenever you're ready.'
- **Submitting** — CTA in loading/spinner state (aria 'Preparing wallet confirmation for "{title}" with {amount} stake.'), form inputs still populated (kept for retry on failure), helper line hidden.
- **Created / contracts list** — Form cleared back to defaults; hero now shows the metrics line (e.g. '1 Active · 1 Pending · 0 Broken · 2 Total'); Contracts card visible with a green 'Last submitted: {title}' status note and a pact card showing header+status chip, PARTNER/STAKE/DAYS LEFT meta tiles, terms, and the appropriate action buttons.
- **Pact card states & actions** — Mock the variants: PENDING (amber chip) with 'Sign Contract' for the named partner OR 'Cancel & reclaim' for the creator, plus the amber 'device-local terms' verify note on a partner's card; ACTIVE (green chip, green inset ring) with 'Break Contract' (danger); honored EXPIRED with 'Settle' (success); and BROKEN/ENDED/CANCELLED (de-emphasized, ghost/mint chip, no actions).
- **Withdraw GAS / credit recovery** — The 'Withdraw your GAS' card appearing above the list: explanatory copy about credit held on the contract (break payout / settlement refund / incomplete-deposit stake), a mint balance row showing '{amount} GAS' with a green 'Withdraw GAS' button.
- **Service notice / load error** — Contracts card showing the demoted inline amber hint: bold 'Couldn't load pacts' + the service message, with the 'Pacts you create or are named in will appear here. Connect your wallet to load them from the chain.' empty hint when no contracts loaded.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the standard GREEN accent intentionally kept (#16c784 bright, #0fb174 strong, #e4f8f0 soft) — deliberately NOT a heartbreak red/pink palette, so the pact reads as warm and trustworthy rather than ominous. Signature visual: the custom "PactGlyph" — two heads leaning together above a single shared white heart on a green gradient rounded tile (replaces the generic social chat-bubble icon). Hero uses a faint mint-to-white diagonal gradient; green pill chips, mint number badges, and a 3px green accent rail on the economics note carry the single-hue identity. Status semantics use green for honored/active/refunded outcomes, amber for pending/warnings, and a subtle de-emphasis (reduced opacity, mint danger chip) for broken pacts rather than aggressive red fills.

## Dev Tipping  `dev-tipping`
*Category: social*

> Send GAS tips directly to Neo developers on-chain — 100% of each tip, no platform fee, accrues to the developer's claimable balance.

**Purpose.** Dev Tipping lets supporters thank Neo open-source developers with direct GAS tips recorded on-chain. Every tip (100%, no platform fee) accrues to the recipient developer's claimable on-chain balance, which they withdraw to their wallet. It is two-sided: supporters tip by developer ID (optionally anonymously), and developers self-register, watch tips accumulate, and claim them. A user opens it to support a builder they appreciate, or to register themselves and collect tips.

**Functional description.** Two roles share one screen. SUPPORTER flow: in the "Send Tip" card, either tap a developer card in the "Top Developers" board (selecting it; pure local state, no chain call) OR — when the board is empty — type a numeric "Registered developer ID" into the "Who are you supporting?" field. Then enter a GAS amount in "How much GAS?" (free-form "Custom amount..." number input; no presets — minimum tip is 0.001 GAS). Choose a "Tip visibility" via a two-button toggle: "Show my name" (default) vs "Anonymous". The primary CTA reads "Pick a developer & amount" while disabled (idle), becomes "Send Tip" when a dev + amount are set, and "Sending..." in flight; on success it shows "Tip sent successfully!", fires fireworks, and clears the form (prevents accidental duplicate tip). On-chain this deposits GAS then settles the tip to the developer's claimable balance. DEVELOPER flow (in the "Developer Zone" card): if no wallet, shows "Connect your wallet to register as a developer." + a Connect button. Connected + unregistered: a register form with "Display Name" (e.g. "Neo Core", 1-64 chars) + "Role (optional)" (e.g. "Protocol Maintainer") and a "Register as Developer" button (→ on-chain registerDeveloper; "Registering..."/"Registered successfully!"). Connected + registered: shows "Registered as developer #<id>", "Claimable Tips <n> GAS", a hint, and a "Withdraw Tips" button (disabled→"No tips to withdraw" when zero; "Withdrawing..." in flight → "Tips withdrawn to your wallet"). RECOVERY: if a deposit landed but the tip step failed, an "Unused tip credit: <n> GAS" row appears with a "Withdraw credit" button (on-chain withdraw(account)). A collapsed "How it works" accordion holds 4 steps (1 connect wallet · 2 choose/enter dev ID · 3 enter amount + optional message · 4 confirm — tip accrues to claimable balance). A collapsed "Recent Tips" accordion (with a count badge) lists up to 5 rows: tipper name (or "Anonymous") → recipient dev, and "<amount> GAS".

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft layout, ~1365px, single green accent (#16c784 bright / #0a8050 filled). TOP: a full-width white hero card (radius ~20px, soft shadow) with a left rounded-square mint-gradient icon badge holding the social/coffee glyph; beside it the eyebrow "SUPPORT DEVELOPERS" (11px, 700, uppercase, tracked), the title "Dev Tipping" (~28px/800, ink), and subtitle "Support developers with direct GAS tips". Right side: three inset stat tiles (#f7f8fa, hairline border) — "0 DEVELOPERS", "0.00 GAS TOTAL DONATED" (success-green figure), "0 RECENT TIPS" — figures 18-24px/800 tabular-nums over uppercase muted labels. When connected, a copy-to-clipboard "Wallet 0x…" pill row sits under the hero head. BODY: a 2-column workspace. LEFT column "TOP DEVELOPERS" (uppercase section title): either a grid of developer cards (round monogram avatar with a deterministic green-family hue, name, role, and a "TOTAL TIPS <n> GAS" footer; cards are clickable/selectable) or, empty, a centered card with the friendly robot mascot illustration ringed by small "N" and Neo coin badges, "Be the first to say thanks" (bold) + hint "No developers on the board yet. Enter a developer's ID in the tip form to support them directly.", followed by a collapsed "How it works" disclosure (chevron). A collapsed "Recent Tips" disclosure with count badge appears below when tips exist. RIGHT column: section title "SEND TIP" over a white card containing the tip form — label "Who are you supporting?" + "Registered developer ID" input + helper "Ask the developer for their registered ID, or register yourself in the Developer Zone below."; label "How much GAS?" + "Custom amount..." input; "TIP VISIBILITY" eyebrow over a segmented pill toggle (active "Show my name" = filled deep green w/ white text, "Anonymous" = light); a full-width CTA (idle state = dashed-outline soft-green pill "Pick a developer & amount"); helper "Enter a developer ID and a tip amount, then send your support." Below: section title "DEVELOPER ZONE" over a white card (connect prompt / register form / registered-status+withdraw). All cards radius 16-20px, hairline borders, soft shadows; pill buttons.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty board (default)** — No developers registered, wallet not connected. Hero stats all 0 / 0.00 GAS. Left: robot-mascot empty state 'Be the first to say thanks' + collapsed 'How it works'. Right: Send Tip form with the manual 'Registered developer ID' field (board empty), idle dashed CTA 'Pick a developer & amount'; Developer Zone shows 'Connect your wallet to register as a developer.' + Connect button.
- **Populated board + ready to tip** — Top Developers shows a grid of selectable dev cards (monogram avatar, name, role, 'TOTAL TIPS n GAS'). One card selected (active green ring). Right form: dev selector list highlights the chosen dev, amount filled, visibility toggle on 'Show my name', CTA now solid green 'Send Tip'. Hero stats reflect real counts. Optional collapsed 'Recent Tips' with count badge.
- **Sending / submitting** — Send Tip CTA in loading state showing 'Sending...' with spinner, form inputs disabled; wallet-signature in flight.
- **Tip success** — Success toast 'Tip sent successfully!' with fireworks; form cleared (amount blank, no dev selected); stats incremented; new entry visible if Recent Tips opened.
- **Developer registered + claimable balance** — Developer Zone shows 'Registered as developer #N', 'Claimable Tips <n> GAS', claimable hint, and an enabled 'Withdraw Tips' button. (Zero-balance variant: disabled button reading 'No tips to withdraw'.)
- **Register form (connected, unregistered)** — Developer Zone with register hint, 'Display Name' input (placeholder 'e.g. Neo Core'), 'Role (optional)' input (placeholder 'e.g. Protocol Maintainer'), and 'Register as Developer' button.
- **Unused credit recovery** — Within Developer Zone, an 'Unused tip credit: <n> GAS' row with a 'Withdraw credit' button — exit path for a deposit that landed but whose tip step failed.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft green identity, single accent hue (#16c784 bright for the icon badge, avatar tints, and soft chips; #0a8050 deep green for the filled visibility toggle and CTAs). Signature visual: the friendly robot mascot empty-state illustration flanked by small "N" and Neo coin badges ("Be the first to say thanks"), plus per-developer monogram avatars with deterministic green/teal-family hues. Supportive, gratitude-themed (coffee/tip) tone; success-green TOTAL DONATED figure; idle CTA uses a distinctive dashed soft-green outline pill.

## Event Ticket Pass  `event-ticket-pass`
*Category: social*

> Spin up an event, issue free NEP-11 ticket passes your guests actually keep, and check people in at the door — all on-chain in seconds.

**Purpose.** Event Ticket Pass is an organizer tool for running an event end-to-end on Neo N3: publish event metadata (name, venue, schedule, supply) on-chain, mint free NEP-11 ticket passes to attendee addresses with seat/memo data, and verify + check people in at the gate by token ID. A user opens it to hand out collectible, transferable keepsake passes without selling anything (no on-chain payment is taken) and to control admission — only the organizer can issue and check in tickets, and each pass flips to Used once scanned.

**Functional description.** Three workflows, signposted in the hero as "Create event → Issue attendee ticket → Lookup and check in". (1) CREATE EVENT (left form, header "CREATE / Create Event"): a live ticket-stub preview ("ADMIT ONE", EVENT PASS) mirrors typed input; fields are Event name (placeholder "Neo Builder Summit"), Venue ("Neo Community Hall"), a 3-up row of Start time / End time (datetime, e.g. "2026-06-20 09:00" / "18:00") and Max tickets (number, e.g. 120), plus a collapsible "Notes (optional)" disclosure (textarea, e.g. "VIP access, badges, or extra info"). Submitting fires the green "Create Event" CTA → publishes event on-chain, after which it appears in Your Events. (2) ISSUE / CHECK-IN desk (right column, a pill tab pair "Issue Ticket | Check-in"): Issue mode shows an event picker (radiogroup of event cards, each with name, Active/Inactive chip, venue, schedule, and "{minted} / {maxSupply} Issued" e.g. 0/120) or an empty "Select an event first" state; then Recipient address (Neo N3 address), a 2-col Seat / Zone (e.g. "A-12", default "General") + Memo (optional, e.g. "Backstage pass"), a green "Issue" button (disabled until an active event + valid recipient), and the footnote "Free passes — issued to the addresses you choose. No payment is taken on-chain." Check-in mode: a "Ticket Token ID" field ("Enter token ID from QR"), a "Lookup" (secondary) button that resolves a ticket card showing Token ID, Event name, Seat, Owner, and a Valid/Used badge, then a "Check-in" (primary) button — disabled if no lookup or already used — that marks it Used. (3) YOUR EVENTS list (right, with Refresh): selectable event cards (name, venue, date, minted/max, live/inactive status) each with "Issue Ticket" + "Deactivate/Activate" actions; sold-out events disable issuing. (4) MY TICKETS grid (full-width, with Refresh): each issued pass is a perforated ticket card with ADMIT ONE stub, event/seat, Valid/Used badge, a real QR of the token ID ("Show this QR at the door"), the token-id code with a copy button, and (for unused passes) a "Transfer" action revealing a "Transfer to" address field + Transfer/Cancel. (5) REQUEST AND RESULT EVIDENCE (collapsible footer): two columns, Latest Request / Latest Result JSON with a "View transaction on explorer" link. A header KPI row (Events / Tickets / Active counts) appears once any metric is non-zero. Wallet must be connected (top-right card); chain-warning shows if the contract address isn't configured for the network.

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft layout (~1200px). HERO BAND (white rounded card): left — a green soft-fill rounded badge with a ticket glyph, eyebrow "A KEEPSAKE FOR EVERY GUEST" (11px uppercase muted), title "Event Ticket Pass" (~26px/800, -0.02em), subtitle "Hand out passes your guests actually keep — issue them in seconds and check people in at the door.", and a 3-step flow row of soft chips with line icons: "Create event · Issue attendee ticket · Lookup and check in"; right — a bordered "WALLET" card reading "Wallet not connected / Ready" and a secondary pill "Connect Wallet". Optional KPI strip below: three tiles Events / Tickets / Active (tabular-nums figures). WORKSPACE = two columns. LEFT: "Create Event" panel (eyebrow CREATE + title, calendar icon) containing the live ticket-stub preview card (green ADMIT ONE stub on the left edge, "EVENT PASS", event name, venue, calendar + ticket meta rows) above the field stack and the full-width green "Create Event" button. RIGHT COLUMN stacks two panels: "Your Events" (header row + ghost Refresh; here an empty-state inset #f7f8fa card with a centered calendar icon + hint "Start with an event on the left. Once it's live you can issue passes…"), and the desk panel with the green pill tab pair (Issue Ticket active = soft green fill + deep-green text; Check-in inactive) over the issue form (event dropdown "Select an event first", Recipient address, Seat/Zone + Memo grid, full-width green "Issue", free-passes footnote). FULL-WIDTH "My Tickets" panel below: header "MY TICKETS / No passes yet" + Refresh, then an empty state pairing a sample green-stubbed ticket card (PREVIEW flag, NEO COMMUNITY HALL / Neo Builder Summit, VALID badge, SEAT "Row A · Seat 12", HOLDER "Your guest", QR mock + code "1-1042") with copy "What your guests will keep / A sample of the keepsake pass attendees receive — once you issue one, real passes appear below." Footer: collapsible "Request and result evidence". Palette: green brand #16c784 (badges/chips/icon fills), filled CTAs #0a8050 white text, deep green #0b5e3e for green text on light, soft fill #e4f8f0; white cards, #f7f8fa insets, hairline borders, 12–20px radii, pill tabs/buttons, soft shadows. (Note: a dead orange theme file exists but is NOT applied — the live UI is green.)

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (default)** — Wallet not connected. Create Event form pre-filled with placeholder-style sample (Neo Builder Summit / Neo Community Hall / 2026-06-20 times / 120). Your Events shows empty inset with calendar icon + hint; Issue tab shows 'Select an event first' dropdown; My Tickets shows the sample keepsake preview card + 'What your guests will keep' copy. No KPI strip yet.
- **Event created / Your Events populated** — KPI strip visible (e.g. Events 1, Tickets 0, Active 1). Your Events lists a selectable card (name, venue, date, '0 / 120 Issued', Active chip) with Issue Ticket + Deactivate actions; selected card highlighted. Issue tab now shows the event picker radiogroup with the live event selected.
- **Issue ticket — ready** — Issue tab active: event picked, Recipient address filled (Neo N3), Seat/Zone 'A-12', Memo 'Backstage pass', green Issue button enabled, free-passes footnote shown.
- **Submitting / loading** — In-flight state: Create Event / Issue / Lookup / Check-in / Transfer buttons show spinner+loading label (e.g. 'Issuing…', 'Looking up…', 'Checking in…'); wallet card small text shows workflow status; relevant actions disabled.
- **My Tickets — passes issued** — Grid of real ticket cards: ADMIT ONE stub, event/seat, Valid badge, scannable token-ID QR ('Show this QR at the door'), token-id code + copy button, and a Transfer action (expands to 'Transfer to' field + Transfer/Cancel) on unused passes.
- **Check-in lookup result** — Check-in tab: token ID entered, Lookup resolved → ticket detail card (Token ID, Event name, Seat, Owner, Valid/Used badge). Check-in button enabled when Valid; a Used ticket disables Check-in and shows the Used badge.
- **Error / chain warning** — Red alert banner (#fef2f2 / #b91c1c) for validation or load errors (e.g. 'Recipient address required', 'Ticket not found'); contract-deployment-pending notice when no contract address is configured for the selected network.
- **Evidence expanded** — Footer 'Request and result evidence' open: two JSON columns Latest Request / Latest Result with monospace payloads and a 'View transaction on explorer' link when a txid is present.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft green identity (brand #16c784, deep-green text #0b5e3e, soft fill #e4f8f0) on a light theme — NOT the orange theme file in the repo, which is dead/unapplied. Signature visual: the perforated TICKET-PASS card with a green vertical "ADMIT ONE" stub on the left edge, a real scannable QR of the token ID, and an EVENT PASS / Valid·Used badge system. The live ticket-stub preview in the Create form (updating as you type) and the keepsake "sample pass" in the empty My Tickets state reinforce the physical-ticket metaphor.

## Forever Album  `forever-album`
*Category: social*

> A private, wallet-scoped photo album that keeps your memories on this device only — with optional one-click local encryption, no transaction, no gas.

**Purpose.** Forever Album lets a user keep a small set of lightweight photos privately, scoped to their connected wallet address, stored locally on the device (browser) rather than on-chain.

**Functional description.** Primary flow: connect wallet (album is keyed to wallet address) -> in the "Memory upload" card, tap the "Choose images" dropzone ("Tap to Select (Up to 5, <60KB total)") to pick up to 5 images -> selected thumbnails appear with a per-thumb "Remove" button, and a "Selected: N / Payload: X KB" meta row plus a green gradient progress meter fill toward the 60KB cap -> optionally tick the "Encrypt photos" checkbox, which reveals a password field ("Encryption password") -> a live readiness line at the panel footer reflects state ("Ready to save to this device." / "Password required for encryption." / "Total exceeds the per-batch size limit." / "Uploading...") -> press "Upload" to save locally (button disabled until at least one image is selected, password present if encrypting, and payload under cap). Hero actions: "Select photos" (opens file picker) and "Refresh album" (reloads the device album). Browsing: saved photos render in a gallery grid inside the "Album" card; public photos show the image, encrypted photos show a locked "Encrypted" tile; a trailing "+ Add Photo" card scrolls/pulses focus back to the dropzone. Tapping a photo opens the "Photo viewer" overlay showing the image (or an "This photo is encrypted." notice with a "Decrypt" button), "Photo ID" + "Created" date metadata, and a "Delete" action. Decrypt overlay ("Decrypt Photo"): enter password -> "Decrypt" -> shows decrypted image inline or a "Decryption failed." error. Four live stats update on every change: Album count, Encrypted count, Public count, Payload (bytes/KB). A collapsible "Privacy route" section explains the 3-step timeline (Pick compact images » Encrypt locally » Save to device) and three safety facts (Wallet scoped, Local privacy, Size guarded).

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked workspace (~1200px) on a near-white #f4f5f7 ground with a faint top-right mint radial glow. (1) Hero card: rounded 20px white surface with a soft mint radial wash in the top-left; left a 24px square-rounded mint icon tile holding a line camera/landscape glyph; eyebrow "FOREVER ALBUM" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em); title "Your private memory album, kept on this device" (~24-30px/800, -0.02em ink #1e1e2e); a body paragraph about lightweight images / no transaction, no gas / no sync; a calm warning chip with a small triangle-alert glyph reading "On this device only — back up originals elsewhere."; a muted facts line "Pick compact images » Encrypt locally » Save to device". Hero CTAs sit at the bottom: filled green "Select photos" (#0a8050 white text) and outlined "Refresh album". (2) Stat strip: 4 equal cells in a row — ALBUM 0 · ENCRYPTED 0 · PUBLIC 0 · PAYLOAD 0 B — uppercase muted labels over bold tabular figures. (3) "Memory upload" NeoCard containing a nested "Album" card: when empty it shows a centered mint-bordered photo frame with a small image glyph and caption "Your first memory", then "Your memories will live here" (bold) and "Save your first memory to this device." with a filled green "Add your first memory" button. Below the album card sits the uploader block: a large dashed-style "Choose images" dropzone (centered "Choose images" + "Tap to Select (Up to 5, <60KB total)"), a size readout "0 B / 60 KB total", a "Selected/Payload" meta row + thin rounded green-gradient meter (shown once images selected), an "Encrypt photos" checkbox (reveals password input when checked), a selected-thumbnails strip with Remove buttons, a footer row pairing the readiness microcopy with the outlined/filled "Upload" button, and a fine-print storage note. (4) Collapsible "Privacy route" disclosure (chevron at right) revealing a 3-step grid and a 3-fact safety strip. Overlays for Photo viewer and Decrypt use the same white rounded card styling. Identity: green Neo Soft accent #16c784 / strong #0fb174 / soft fill #e4f8f0, mint radial gradients, 16-20px radii, soft shadows, pill buttons.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty album** — Wallet connected but no photos: hero with title, durability chip and CTAs; stat strip all zeros (Album 0, Encrypted 0, Public 0, Payload 0 B); 'Memory upload' card showing the empty 'Album' state — mint photo frame with 'Your first memory' caption, 'Your memories will live here', 'Save your first memory to this device.', green 'Add your first memory' button; below it the 'Choose images' dropzone reading '0 B / 60 KB total' with Upload disabled.
- **Images selected / ready to save** — One-to-five thumbnails in the selected strip each with a Remove button; meta row 'Selected: 3 / Payload: 42.0 KB'; green-gradient meter ~70% filled; footer readiness line 'Ready to save to this device.' with Upload enabled (filled green). Show the variant with 'Encrypt photos' checked: password field 'Encryption password' visible and footer note about encrypted ciphertext.
- **Uploading / submitting** — Upload button in loading spinner state, footer readiness line shows 'Uploading...', controls disabled.
- **Filled album (gallery)** — Stats reflect saved counts (e.g. Album 4 · Encrypted 2 · Public 2 · Payload 38.4 KB). 'Album' card shows a gallery grid: public photos as images with a 'Public' meta tag, encrypted photos as locked tiles labeled 'Encrypted' with an 'Encrypted' meta tag, plus a trailing '+ Add Photo' card that refocuses the dropzone.
- **Photo viewer overlay** — White rounded card titled 'Photo viewer' with Close; for a public photo it shows the full image; for an encrypted photo it shows 'This photo is encrypted.' with a 'Decrypt' button; metadata row 'Photo ID: …' and 'Created: <date>'; danger-styled 'Delete' at the footer.
- **Decrypt overlay** — Card titled 'Decrypt Photo' with a password input ('Enter password'), a 'Decrypt' button (loading state available), an inline decrypted image on success or a red 'Decryption failed.' error on failure.
- **Error / needs attention** — Inline upload error block ('Upload needs attention' + specific message such as 'Total exceeds the per-batch size limit.', 'Image too large to store on this device.', 'You already selected the max photos.', or 'Password required for encryption.'), red-soft styling; meter may show over-cap; Upload disabled.
- **Privacy route expanded** — The collapsible 'Privacy route' disclosure open, revealing the 3-step timeline (Pick compact images / Encrypt locally / Save to device) with step descriptions, and the 3-fact safety strip (Wallet scoped / Local privacy / Size guarded).

**Visual identity.** Standard green Neo Soft accent: brand #16c784, strong #0fb174, soft mint fill #e4f8f0, deep-green text on light. Light theme on #f4f5f7 with signature soft mint radial gradient washes (top-right of page, top-left of hero, top-left of empty album). Signature visuals: a square-rounded mint icon tile with a line camera/landscape glyph (hero + empty frame), a calm amber-tinted triangle-alert "on this device only" durability chip (privacy-first, not alarmist), a green-gradient horizontal payload meter, and locked "Encrypted" photo tiles in the gallery. The mood is a private keepsake vault, not a financial dApp.

## Memorial Shrine  `memorial-shrine`
*Category: social*

> Create permanent on-chain memorials for departed loved ones and pay symbolic GAS offerings that are recorded forever on the blockchain.

**Purpose.** Memorial Shrine ("Blockchain Memorial" / 区块链灵位) lets a user inscribe a deceased person's name, photo, life dates, biography and obituary permanently onto the blockchain, then return to "pay tribute" by leaving a message and choosing a symbolic offering (incense, candle, flowers, fruit, wine, feast). The offering is paid in GAS but is explicitly non-refundable and routed to no one — it is consumed purely as an on-chain record of remembrance. A user opens it to create a lasting memorial, to honor someone with recurring tributes, or to browse and visit other people's memorials and recent obituaries. The whole experience is reverent and gentle, not a marketplace.

**Functional description.** PRIMARY FLOW — Create Memorial: User clicks the "Create Memorial" CTA (top-right of hero, or the ghost button inside the empty state). A create-form card expands with title "Create a Memorial" / subtitle "Memorial will be permanently stored on blockchain", containing fields in order: Name of Deceased (required), Photo (IPFS hash or HTTPS image URL), Your Relationship (e.g. Father, Mother...), a 2-up Birth Year / Death Year row (placeholders 1940 / 2024), Biography textarea, Obituary textarea. Inline year validation surfaces a red error line under the row only after typing — "Enter a valid year (numbers only).", "Year cannot be in the future.", or "Birth year must be before the death year." The submit button "Create Memorial" stays disabled until name is present and years are valid; it shows a loading state while submitting. On success the form collapses and a new tombstone card appears in the grid. Creating a memorial is FREE (no GAS).

SECONDARY FLOW — Pay Tribute: User clicks a tombstone card (or an obituary chip) to open a Selected Memorial detail card. It shows the photo, name, year range (e.g. 1940-2024), Share / Close ghost actions, biography, an "Offerings Received" tally row (green pill per offering type with a count), then a tribute form titled "Offer Tribute": a Message input ("Leave a short tribute message"), an Offering selector, and a live Offering-cost card. The 6 offerings map to fixed GAS costs: Incense · 0.01, Candle · 0.02, Flowers · 0.03, Fruit · 0.05, Wine · 0.10, Feast · 0.50 GAS. A muted disclosure note always reads "Tributes are permanent and non-refundable. The offering is recorded on-chain, not transferred to anyone — it is not income to the family." On mainnet only, an extra tinted note explains the host batches payment + tribute and an additional "Payment receipt ID" field appears (placeholder "Required for mainnet tribute"). The "Offer Tribute" primary button shows a paying/loading state; on success the message clears.

OTHER ACTIONS: Share copies a link ("Link copied. Share to let others pay tribute."); Close dismisses the detail card; clicking a Recent Obituaries chip opens that memorial. A chain receipt row ("Last transaction" + txid) appears under the hero after any on-chain action. Two tabs exist: Memorials (default) and My Tributes.

**Interface description.** Single centered column, max-width ~1120px, on a near-white #f8fafc canvas with two faint diagonal green wash gradients (top-left and bottom-right corners). NOTE: this is NOT a 2-column workspace — it is a vertical stack of full-width cards, all radius 20px.

HERO CARD (white, radius 20px, soft shadow): a header row with a 50px rounded-square (16px) mint badge (#e4f8f0 fill, green #16c784 heart outline icon), then a text stack — green uppercase eyebrow "MEMORIALS" (11px/700/.12em), title "Blockchain Memorial" (28px/800, -0.02em, ink #1e1e2e), subtitle "Inscribe memories on the blockchain forever" (13px, #5b6478). A filled dark-green CTA "Create Memorial" (background #0a8050, white text, radius 12px) is pinned to the far right (toggles to "Cancel"). Below the header, a 4-column stat grid of inset chips (#f7f8fa, radius 16px, hairline border, centered): each chip shows a big tabular figure (22px/800) over an uppercase muted label — MEMORIALS, MY TRIBUTES, RECENT OBITUARIES, VISITED (all "0" in the empty state shown). An optional chain-receipt strip (inset pill, "LAST TRANSACTION" label + monospace txid) sits at the bottom of the hero after a transaction.

OBITUARY BANNER (conditional, white card radius 20px): uppercase title "RECENT OBITUARIES" over a horizontal scroll row of obituary chips (inset, radius 14px, each = bold name + truncated single-line text).

EMPTY STATE (current screenshot): a white card with a dashed hairline border, radius 20px, centered — a 56px mint rounded-square badge with the green heart icon, italic muted line "No memorials yet. Create the first one above.", and a pill ghost button "Create Memorial".

MEMORIAL GRID (when populated): "MEMORIALS" eyebrow over a responsive grid of TombstoneCards (auto-fill, min 190px). Each card is white, radius 20px, centered column: a 44px mint rounded-square (14px) with heart icon (or the person's photo), name (17px/800), year range (12px muted), an optional green relationship pill, and tiny green offering-tally pills (e.g. "Incense 2").

CREATE FORM & DETAIL/TRIBUTE cards render as white "erobo"-variant NeoCards stacked in the same column. Form fields are inset (#f7f8fa) inputs with small labels; offering cost shows in a dedicated inset card (label + 16px/800 figure).

IDENTITY: strictly single green hue — bright #16c784 for badges/icons/eyebrows/pills + soft #e4f8f0 fills + deep #0fb174 for offering counts; the only filled CTA color is the AA-safe #0a8050 (hover #086a45). All offering tallies and relationship tags are green soft-fill pills (radius 9999px).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero card (heart badge, MEMORIALS eyebrow, 'Blockchain Memorial' title, subtitle, filled 'Create Memorial' CTA, 4 stat chips all '0': MEMORIALS / MY TRIBUTES / RECENT OBITUARIES / VISITED) above a dashed-border empty card with centered 56px mint heart badge, italic 'No memorials yet. Create the first one above.' and a ghost 'Create Memorial' pill. This is the current rendered state.
- **Create Memorial form open** — Hero CTA flipped to 'Cancel'; an expanded white form card titled 'Create a Memorial' / 'Memorial will be permanently stored on blockchain' with fields Name, Photo, Relationship, Birth Year + Death Year (2-up), Biography & Obituary textareas, and a disabled-until-valid 'Create Memorial' submit. Show the inline red year-error variant ('Birth year must be before the death year.').
- **Populated memorial grid** — Hero with non-zero stats, optional 'RECENT OBITUARIES' scroll banner, then 'MEMORIALS' eyebrow over a grid of TombstoneCards — each with heart/photo badge, name, 1940-2024 year range, green relationship pill (e.g. Father), and green offering-tally pills.
- **Memorial detail + tribute** — Selected-memorial card: 64px photo, name (20px/800), year range, Share/Close ghost actions, biography, 'OFFERINGS RECEIVED' green pill tallies, then 'OFFER TRIBUTE' form — Message input, Offering selector + live cost card (e.g. 'Wine · 0.10 GAS'), the non-refundable disclosure note, and the filled 'Offer Tribute' button.
- **Paying / loading** — Tribute being submitted: 'Offer Tribute' button in loading state; create form would similarly show the submit button loading. Designer should mock the in-flight spinner/disabled affordance.
- **Success / receipt** — After an on-chain action: chain-receipt strip under the hero ('LAST TRANSACTION' + txid), share status line 'Link copied. Share to let others pay tribute.', updated stat figures and offering tallies reflecting the new tribute.
- **Mainnet tribute variant** — Detail tribute form on mainnet: adds a tinted green note explaining the host batches payment + tribute, plus an extra 'Payment receipt ID' field (placeholder 'Required for mainnet tribute').
- **Error state** — Validation/payment errors: red field-error line under the year row, or a failure message ('Payment failed' / 'Mainnet tribute requires a payment receipt ID.'). Designer should mock the inline error treatment.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme, strict single green accent hue. Signature visual is the green HEART (not a candle, despite the "candle" manifest icon) rendered as a stroked SVG inside mint #e4f8f0 rounded-square badges at three sizes (50px hero, 56px empty state, 44px tombstone). Decorative greens: #16c784 bright for icons/eyebrows/relationship & offering pills, #e4f8f0 soft fills, #0fb174 deep green for tribute/offering count numbers; filled CTA uses AA-safe #0a8050 with white text. Tone is reverent and calm — generous whitespace, rounded 20px cards, soft shadows, two faint diagonal green washes on the #f8fafc canvas. The six offerings (incense/candle/flowers/fruit/wine/feast) are the secondary iconographic motif and could each gain a distinct gentle line glyph.

## Neo Message  `neo-message`
*Category: social*

> Write a private note only one person can open, or seal one that unlocks itself on-chain at a moment you choose — encrypted end-to-end by the Morpheus confidential oracle on Neo X.

**Purpose.** Neo Message is a confidential messaging miniapp on Neo X (EVM) where a user composes a note and chooses one of two delivery modes. "Just for them" encrypts the note before it leaves the device so only the recipient's wallet can ever decrypt it (plaintext never hits the chain in the clear). "Open later" seals the note until a reveal time the sender picks, after which anyone can trigger an on-chain public reveal. Users open it to send a heartfelt or sensitive note to a specific address, or to schedule a time-locked public message (a future-self letter, an announcement), and to read notes others sent them in the Inbox.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (Compose): connect an EVM wallet on Neo X Mainnet → if no wallet / wrong network, a Network warning card blocks with "No EVM wallet detected…" or "Switch to Neo X" → enter recipient "Send to (Neo X address)" (0x… input) → optionally add a private "Nickname (optional, just for you)" stored on-device only (shows "Saved as '{name}' on this device") → type the body in "Message" with a live "{count}/2000" counter (error "Message is too long." past 2000) → pick a delivery mode via two large selectable cards: "Just for them — Only the person you send it to can ever open it" (lock icon, default/active) or "Open later — Opens for everyone on-chain at a time you set" (clock icon). RECIPIENT MODE: a soft info note "Sealed for their eyes only…Secured by the Morpheus confidential oracle on Neo X." then primary CTA "Encrypt & send" (→ "Sending…"). TIMED MODE: reveals a red/alert "Public reveal: after the unlock time the message body is posted on-chain in cleartext, readable by anyone…" banner, a "Reveal at" datetime-local picker (min = now, rejects past), and a required acknowledgement checkbox "I understand this message will become public on-chain at the reveal time." — the CTA "Schedule public reveal" stays disabled until checked. Send dispatches sendMessage(address,bytes,uint64) to MiniAppMessageEVM.

Inbox/Sent flow: the Inbox card header shows the connected account identicon + short address (or "Not connected") and a "Connect wallet" / "Refresh" button. Empty: "No notes yet — they'll appear here…" (connected) or "Connect your wallet to see notes sent your way." (not connected); "Load older" paginates. Each message row shows a colored identicon monogram, the correspondent's nickname-or-From/To label + short address, a status badge, and either an "Unlocks: {date}" line (timed) or "Just for them" line. Per-row actions by status: SEALED recipient-only inbox row → "Decrypt for me" (oracle recipient-reveal via wallet signature; non-recipients see "Only the recipient can read this."); UNLOCKABLE timed row → "Reveal on-chain" (anyone may trigger; oracle posts plaintext); LOCKED → "Not unlocked yet"; REVEALED → plaintext shown, recipient-only adds "Decrypted on this device. Re-open with your wallet to read again elsewhere." A "Sent" card mirrors the same rows for outgoing messages when any exist.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling column of stacked rounded cards (~1180px content width), no two-column split. Top: a hero band with a soft mint→white gradient (linear-gradient 135deg #e4f8f0→#fff), a white rounded-square icon tile holding an envelope-with-lock glyph (green #0a8050), eyebrow "PRIVATE MESSAGES" (11px/700/uppercase/.12em, green), title "Send a note only they can open" (~26px/800/-0.02em ink #1c2030), and muted subtitle. Below, a conditional "Network" NeoCard with an amber-left-border warning row (warning triangle icon, "No EVM wallet detected…" copy on #fff4e5) plus optional "Switch to Neo X" button. Main "Write a note" NeoCard: a 2-up row of inputs ("Send to (Neo X address)" with 0x… placeholder | "Nickname (optional, just for you)" with "e.g. Mom, Alex, my future self…"), a full-width "Message" textarea with bottom-left "0/2000" counter, then the delivery-mode toggle = two side-by-side selectable cards each with a line-icon chip (padlock / clock), bold title + small hint; active card gets a green border + soft-green fill (#e4f8f0) and green text. Timed mode injects: a red alert banner (#fef3f2/#b42318 with triangle icon), a "Reveal at" datetime field, and a checkbox acknowledgement row. A muted inset note paragraph sits above the full-width primary CTA. Bottom: "Inbox" NeoCard with a header row (account identicon + short addr left, secondary "Connect wallet"/"Refresh" pill right) and either a centered empty state (no mascot icon — title + hint only) or a list of message rows; each row is a white sub-card with hairline border + soft shadow showing a square identicon monogram (deterministic warm hue, 2-hex label), party name/label + short address, a pill status badge, a meta line, optional plaintext, and a contextual small action button. Optional "Sent" card identical in structure. Status badge palette: Sealed = soft-green bg/green text; Locked = amber #fff4e5/#c47d18; Ready(unlockable) = soft-green/deep-green #0b5e3e; Revealed = neutral grey. Identity is the green Neo Soft accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 CTAs); note the current rendered screenshot still shows the indigo shared-button default — designer should restore green per the SCSS.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / not connected** — Hero band + 'Network' warning card ('No EVM wallet detected…' amber banner) + 'Write a note' compose form in its default 'Just for them' state + Inbox card showing 'Not connected' header and the empty hint 'Connect your wallet to see notes sent your way.' with a 'Connect wallet' button. (Matches current screenshot.)
- **Compose ready — recipient mode** — Wallet connected (no network warning), recipient address + optional nickname filled, message typed with live '247/2000' counter, 'Just for them' card active (green border/fill), soft info note 'Sealed for their eyes only…', primary CTA 'Encrypt & send' enabled.
- **Compose — timed/public mode** — 'Open later' card active; reveals the red 'Public reveal…' alert banner, the 'Reveal at' datetime-local picker, and the required acknowledgement checkbox 'I understand this message will become public on-chain…'. Show both states of the CTA 'Schedule public reveal': disabled (unchecked) and enabled (checked).
- **Sending / submitting** — CTA in loading state showing 'Sending…' with spinner; form inputs disabled. Optionally reflect status copy like 'Encrypting message…' / 'Sending transaction…'.
- **Inbox populated with mixed statuses** — Inbox list with several rows: a 'Sealed' recipient-only row with a 'Decrypt for me' button, a 'Locked' timed row showing 'Unlocks: {date}' + 'Not unlocked yet', a 'Ready' (unlockable) row with 'Reveal on-chain', and a 'Revealed' row showing plaintext (recipient-only adds 'Decrypted on this device…'). Header shows account identicon + short address + 'Refresh'; a 'Load older' button at the bottom. Include the 'Sent' card variant.
- **Empty inbox (connected)** — Connected header (identicon + short address + Refresh) with centered empty state 'No notes yet — they'll appear here the moment someone writes to you.' (no mascot icon, title-only empty).
- **Error / validation** — Compose-level inline errors: invalid recipient ('Enter a valid Neo X (0x) address.'), empty/too-long body ('Message is too long.'), and the network warning card variant 'Neo Message runs on Neo X Mainnet…' with a 'Switch to Neo X' button. Also a row-level non-recipient state 'Only the recipient can read this.'

**Visual identity.** Green Neo Soft default accent: #16c784 bright for decorative badges/chips, #0a8050 (hover #086a45) for the filled CTA, #0b5e3e deep green text, soft fill #e4f8f0; hero uses a mint→white gradient. Signature visual = the envelope-with-padlock hero glyph reinforcing "sealed note," paired with deterministic warm-hued square identicon monograms (palette of 7: indigo/violet/coral/green/rose/blue/gold) giving each 0x… correspondent a stable face. Two-mode duality is core identity: a padlock (private, recipient-only) vs a clock (time-locked public reveal), with timed mode shifting to amber/red caution accents to signal its inverted, public privacy guarantee.
