## OneGate Vault  `gas-lucky-pool`
*Category: social*

> Fund a surprise GAS reward pool, share a OneGate QR claim link, and each friend taps once to unwrap a random 1-50 GAS reward.

**Purpose.** OneGate Vault is a social gifting tool for distributing random GAS rewards (1-50 GAS) on Neo N3. A campaign owner opens the app to create and fund a reward pool, then shares single-use OneGate QR claim links; recipients who open a claim QR see only a clean "you won" claim screen, tap once, and watch their random GAS payout land. Users open it either to GIVE (create/fund/manage a pool and recover leftover GAS) or to CLAIM (unwrap a reward a friend sent). The frontend never decides the amount — a backend reward server picks the random payout and the dApp verifies it on-chain.

**Functional description.** The app renders one of TWO shells depending on how it is opened.

CREATOR WORKSPACE (default — app opened directly, no claim QR): User reads the gifting hero and a two-path chooser ("Give a lucky reward" vs "I have a claim link"), sees a sample "what your friends will see" preview card, then works in a 2-column workspace.
- LEFT, "Create reward pool": fill Total GAS (number, min 1, hint "How much GAS to put in the pool"); a min/max row Minimum claim / Maximum claim (hint "Smallest and largest random reward (GAS)", defaults 1 and 5); a second row Claim slots (hint "How many friends can win") and Expiry hours (hint "When the offer closes", default 24) -> press primary green "Create pool" (-> "Creating pool" while submitting) which transfers GAS and creates the on-chain pool. Validation surfaces errors as a toast: "Enter at least 1 GAS.", "Reward range must stay within 1-50 GAS.", "Claim slots must be 1-100.", "Expiry must be 1-720 hours.", "Total GAS cannot cover the minimum payout for all slots.", etc.
- RIGHT, "Pool controls": enter a Pool ID (placeholder "e.g. 42") and optional Top up amount; three actions — "Inspect pool" (load state), soft-green "Add GAS" (top up), outline "Recover remaining GAS" (refund leftover to creator). Below, a "Recoverable GAS" block shows a GAS figure (e.g. "0 GAS") with "Check credit" (soft) and "Withdraw credit" (outline) to reclaim prepaid GAS stranded by an interrupted create. Loading labels swap in ("Loading pool", "Adding GAS", "Recovering GAS", "Checking credit", "Withdrawing credit"). A status chip shows "Pool topped up" / "Remaining GAS returned" / failures.
- Bottom: a collapsible "Campaign owner setup" disclosure with 3 backend steps.

CLAIM-ONLY VIEW (opened via a OneGate QR launch URL carrying a claim key): A single focused card. Before claim: GAS badge + eyebrow ("OneGate scan detected" / "OneGate ready"), title "Claim Reward", review copy ("Tap Claim Reward. We will send GAS and keep this screen updated."), a Reward range row ("1-50 GAS"), a server-pays note, and a receipt list (Claim key masked e.g. ogv_ca...x4f2 or "Awaiting scan", Network "Neo N3 MainNet/TestNet", "Server guarded" -> "One claim per address per pool"). Primary "Claim Reward" button -> submits key + injected wallet address. A 4-step progress tracker then animates: Wallet ready -> Submitting claim -> Waiting for GAS transfer -> GAS received. On success: congrats state ("Congratulations, reward received", "Reward key {key} awarded {amount} GAS", "Luck beat {percent}% of users"), a receipt (Reward amount, Claim key, Network, Transaction ID with "View on explorer" link). Fireworks celebrate the win.

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft theme over a soft mint-green hero gradient (#f8fbf4 -> #edf8ee -> #dcebe1 with a faint grid texture); white cards (radius 20px, hairline border rgba(20,22,38,0.06), soft shadow 0 8px 24px). Green identity throughout: brand #16c784 (icon badges/chips), filled CTAs #0a7a52 hover #0a6a4f with white text and pill radius 9999px, soft fills #e4f8f0, brand-strong #0fb174 / #0c8f5c for green text on light surfaces.

CREATOR WORKSPACE layout (top to bottom):
1) Hero band: 52px green gift-icon logo badge (rounded 14px, green glow shadow) + eyebrow "SEND A LITTLE LUCK" (uppercase, tracked) + title "Gift a lucky GAS reward" (24-30px/800, -0.02em) + subtitle about setting up a surprise GAS pool.
2) Two-path chooser card titled "What would you like to do?": a 2-up grid of mini-cards each with a line icon — primary "Give a lucky reward" (gift icon, green-tinted) and "I have a claim link" (QR icon).
3) Sample preview card: header eyebrow "What your friends will see" + a small "Sample" tag pill; inside, a recipient-style card with a green gift badge, "You unwrapped a reward!", body copy, a reward row showing "Your reward / 12.40 GAS", and a "Luckier than most" luck chip.
4) Two-column workspace grid: LEFT primary card "Create reward pool" (description + Total GAS field, min/max row, Claim slots + Expiry hours row, full-width green "Create pool" button); RIGHT card with kicker "Manage a pool you created", heading "Pool controls", Pool ID + Top up amount fields, a hint line when no pool is targeted, a 3-button action cluster (Inspect pool / Add GAS soft / Recover remaining GAS outline), then a "Recoverable GAS" mini-panel with figure + Check credit / Withdraw credit buttons.
5) Collapsible "Campaign owner setup" footer.
Fields use inset #f7f8fa surfaces; labels are small caps eyebrows; figures are tabular-nums.

CLAIM-ONLY VIEW: a single centered card with green GAS badge, eyebrow, large title, receipt definition-list rows, a prominent green "Claim Reward" pill button, an animated 4-dot stepper, and on success a celebratory congrats block with reward figure, luck line, and explorer link. Errors render in a red alert with an optional monospace [ogvdiag ...] diagnostics line. Tabs: Pool / Activity.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Creator workspace — first run / empty** — Default landing: mint-green hero 'Gift a lucky GAS reward' + 'Send a little luck' eyebrow, two-path chooser, Sample preview card (12.40 GAS), and the 2-column workspace with the Create reward pool form pristine (defaults: min 1, max 5, expiry 24, Total/Claim slots empty) and Pool controls showing the 'Enter a pool ID to inspect, top up, or recover' hint plus Recoverable GAS at 0 GAS.
- **Creator — create pool ready / submitting** — Form filled with Total GAS, Claim slots, etc.; primary CTA shows 'Creating pool' in a disabled/loading state. Show a validation error variant too (red toast e.g. 'Reward range must stay within 1-50 GAS.').
- **Creator — pool inspected & topped up** — Pool controls with a Pool ID entered, actions enabled (Inspect pool / Add GAS / Recover remaining GAS), a success status chip 'Pool topped up' or 'Remaining GAS returned', and the Recoverable GAS panel populated with a real figure.
- **Claim-only — ready to claim** — Focused recipient card from a OneGate QR launch: GAS badge, 'OneGate scan detected' eyebrow, 'Claim Reward' title, Reward range 1-50 GAS, server-pays note, receipt rows (masked claim key, Neo N3 MainNet, One claim per address per pool), and the green 'Claim Reward' button.
- **Claim-only — submitting (progress stepper)** — 4-step tracker active: Wallet ready -> Submitting claim -> Waiting for GAS transfer -> GAS received, with the current step highlighted and prior steps marked done.
- **Claim-only — success / reward received** — Celebratory state with fireworks: 'Congratulations, reward received', 'Reward key … awarded N GAS', 'Luck beat X% of users', and a receipt (Reward, Claim key, Network, Transaction ID with View on explorer link).
- **Claim-only — error / retry** — Red alert with a human message (e.g. OneGate wallet-address-missing copy) plus an optional monospace [ogvdiag …] diagnostics line; 'Claim needs retry' state in the stepper.

**Visual identity.** Green Neo Soft default theme, leaning warm/celebratory for the gifting framing. Brand bright green #16c784 for the gift-icon badges and luck chips; filled CTAs #0a7a52 (hover #0a6a4f) white pill buttons; soft fills #e4f8f0 and brand-strong text #0fb174/#0c8f5c. Signature visuals: a green rounded gift-box icon badge (with green glow), a soft mint-green hero gradient with a faint dot/line grid texture, a "GAS" circular badge on claim/congrats cards, a 4-dot claim progress stepper, a "Sample / 12.40 GAS" preview reward card, and fireworks on a successful claim.

## Red Envelope  `red-envelope`
*Category: social*

> Scan, claim, or send lucky GAS red envelopes on Neo N3 — each recipient draws a random share of the pool, paid straight to their wallet.

**Purpose.** Red Envelope is a claim-first social gifting miniapp: someone arriving from a OneGate QR link sees a single job — claim their GAS packet — front and center, with the envelope ID prefilled. Creators can secondarily fund a "lucky money" pool that splits into N random packets (WeChat-style) for friends to claim. A user opens it either to receive GAS from a friend's link or to send a festive GAS gift; the on-chain contract draws the random amount for fairness and pays GAS to the recipient atomically in the same transaction.

**Functional description.** The surface is a single Claim/Create toggle inside one action card. CLAIM (default, primary): the Envelope ID field auto-fills from a OneGate QR / launch param (envelopeId | poolId | id | packet) or is pasted/typed manually; when the ID resolves to a known active envelope, a 3-cell read-only summary appears above the field showing "Envelope ID" (#shortId, e.g. #6f3a92ab10...c4cca4), "Packets left" (e.g. 5/8), and "Pool Progress" (e.g. 37%); a contextual helper line below the field changes state — "Scan a OneGate QR or enter an envelope ID to receive GAS." (no ID) → "Claim the selected GAS envelope. OneGate QR links prefill this ID." (ID, unresolved) → "This envelope is ready. Confirm once to receive GAS." (resolved); the red "Claim now" CTA is disabled until an ID is present, shows a loading spinner while opening, and dispatches claimEnvelope. CREATE (secondary tab): a 3-field form — "Total GAS" (amount, suffix GAS, min 0.1, placeholder 1), "Packet count" (number, 1–100, placeholder 8), "Expiry hours" (number, min 1, suffix h, default/placeholder 24); a 2-cell live preview shows "Average packet" (Total/Count formatted as GAS, with the italic note "Average only — each recipient draws a random share, not this exact amount.") and "Expiry hours" (e.g. 24 h); inline red validation replaces the helper when Send is blocked ("Enter at least 0.1 GAS" / "Enter 1-100 packets" / "Each packet must be at least 0.01 GAS" / "Enter a valid expiry in hours"), otherwise the helper reads "Ready to send. Each recipient draws a random share of the pool."; the "Send Red Envelope" CTA dispatches createEnvelope. After a successful create, a green "Share this envelope" card slots in below the button with the hint "Created envelope #{id}. Copy the claim link and send it to recipients." and two pill buttons "Copy claim link" / "Done". On-chain behavior: claiming calls the contract directly (draws one random packet + pays GAS atomically); creating deposits GAS first, then opens from prepaid credit — a degraded path surfaces "Prepaid credit" with a "Withdraw credit" recovery action. The result of a claim is a celebration modal ("Lucky you!" / "You opened a packet worth" / the GAS amount / "Tap to keep the luck") with confetti/fireworks (features.fireworks).

**Interface description.** Single centered column, width min(680px, 100%), on a soft background: a warm coral radial glow at the top fading into #f7f8fb. Top to bottom: (1) HERO — a white rounded card (radius 18, soft shadow) with a left 46px rounded-square coral-soft badge holding an outlined envelope-with-coin glyph, and right copy: coral uppercase eyebrow "ONEGATE SHARE-READY" (11px/700/.12em), title "Red Envelope" (22px/800, -0.02em ink), subtitle "Scan, paste, or select an active Neo N3 GAS envelope, then review the wallet request before claiming." (2) METRICS STRIP — three white stat tiles (radius 14): "ACTIVE ENVELOPES" (count), "CLAIMABLE POOL" (e.g. 0 GAS), "RECENT CLAIMS" (count); uppercase muted labels, 19px/800 tabular-nums figures. (3) ACTION PANEL — white card with a pill-segmented inset toggle ("Claim" active = white pill + coral text, "Create" inactive) on a #f7f8fa track, then the active tab body (claim summary + ID input + helper + CTA, or create grid + preview + helper + CTA + optional share card). (4) ACTIVITY PANEL — white card titled "OPEN ENVELOPES" (uppercase eyebrow) with a count on the right; empty state = an inset coral-tinted panel (coral-ring border, coral radial wash) with a hand-drawn envelope-with-coin line illustration, heading "No envelopes to open yet", hint "Create one to send a little GAS luck, or paste an ID from a friend's link."; filled state = two columns of compact rows (#shortId mono left, packets/total or GAS amount in coral-strong right; selectable rows highlight coral-soft). (5) RECOVERY PANEL (conditional) — "RECLAIM EXPIRED ENVELOPES" rows each with a "Reclaim" pill, plus a "Prepaid credit" row with "Withdraw credit". (6) SAFETY DISCLOSURE — a collapsed <details> row with a coral shield badge, label "TRANSACTION SAFETY" and value "On-chain contract" + chevron; expands to explain the atomic claim path and show "Contract route → claim -> atomic GAS payout", "Created GAS", "Claimed GAS". Components: NeoCard (variant erobo), NeoInput (with suffix chips GAS / h), NeoButton (primary coral / secondary). Identity color is coral-red #d8392f / strong #b42318 / soft #fdecea, NOT the platform green — coral drives BOTH chrome and the primary CTA; gold #c98a16 / soft #fbf0d4 appears only on the coin/seal/celebration motifs.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (Claim tab)** — Default landing from a cold open: hero + metrics all reading 0 (ACTIVE ENVELOPES 0, CLAIMABLE POOL 0 GAS, RECENT CLAIMS 0). Action panel on Claim with an empty Envelope ID field, helper 'Scan a OneGate QR or enter an envelope ID to receive GAS.', disabled (soft-fill) Claim now button. Activity panel shows the coral-tinted empty state: envelope-with-coin line art + 'No envelopes to open yet' + hint. This is the rendered screenshot state.
- **Claim ready (envelope resolved)** — Envelope ID prefilled from a OneGate QR. The 3-cell summary appears (#shortId, Packets left 5/8, Pool Progress 37%), helper reads 'This envelope is ready. Confirm once to receive GAS.', and the coral 'Claim now' CTA is enabled. Activity panel may list selectable open-envelope rows + recent claims.
- **Create tab — filled & ready** — Create selected: Total GAS=1, Packet count=8, Expiry hours=24 with GAS / h suffix chips; preview shows 'Average packet 0.125 GAS' (+ random-share note) and 'Expiry 24 h'; helper 'Ready to send. Each recipient draws a random share of the pool.'; enabled 'Send Red Envelope' CTA.
- **Create — validation error** — Invalid input (e.g. amount < 0.1 or packets > 100): the helper is replaced by the red inline alert pill (danger-soft fill, dot marker) showing the specific message; the Send CTA sits in disabled soft-fill state.
- **Submitting / loading** — Claim now (spinner via openingId) or Send Red Envelope (loading) in progress — button shows loading state, inputs effectively locked while isLoading.
- **Success — celebration modal** — Centered modal over a blurred dark backdrop (width 360): gold coin icon in a radial gold→coral glow with sparkle stars, 'Lucky you!' title (coral-strong), caption 'You opened a packet worth', the GAS amount at 30px/800 coral-strong, and a coral pill 'Tap to keep the luck'. Fireworks fire behind it.
- **Post-create share card** — After a successful create, the green 'Share this envelope' card appears under the Send button: hint 'Created envelope #{id}. Copy the claim link and send it to recipients.' with 'Copy claim link' (primary) and 'Done' (secondary) pills.
- **Creator recovery** — Recovery panel visible when expired envelopes or prepaid credit exist: 'RECLAIM EXPIRED ENVELOPES' rows (#shortId, remaining GAS, 'Reclaim' pill) and a 'Prepaid credit' row with amount + 'Withdraw credit'.
- **Safety disclosure expanded** — The 'TRANSACTION SAFETY / On-chain contract' details row opened: explanatory copy about the atomic claim + prepaid-deposit create path, plus signal rows 'Contract route → claim -> atomic GAS payout', 'Created GAS', 'Claimed GAS'.

**Visual identity.** Intentional secondary theme: festive coral-red + gold (overrides the default green). Accent coral #d8392f, strong #b42318, soft fill #fdecea, ring rgba(216,57,47,.2). Gold companion #c98a16 / soft #fbf0d4 used ONLY for decorative coin/seal/sparkle motifs, never as a second interactive hue — the coral is the single interactive accent and carries the primary CTA so the app unmistakably reads as a red envelope. Signature visuals: the envelope-with-coin glyph (hero badge + empty-state line art), and the celebration modal's gold coin-in-radial-glow icon with two sparkle stars. Light Neo Soft base otherwise (white cards, #f7f8fb canvas, hairline borders, 14–18px radii, soft shadows, pill toggle/buttons, tabular-nums figures).

## Time Capsule  `time-capsule`
*Category: social*

> Seal a secret message on-chain with a future unlock date, locking a refundable 0.2 GAS deposit you get back when you reveal — and tip the owners of other people's public, still-sealed capsules.

**Purpose.** Time Capsule lets a user lock a private message behind a future unlock date. Only the message hash goes on-chain (full text stays local on-device), backed by a 0.2 GAS refundable deposit that returns on reveal. Users bury a personal note / gift / memorial / announcement / secret for their future self or publicly, watch it stay "Locked" until the date passes, then reveal. A social layer lets users tip (0.05 GAS) owners of other people's public, still-sealed capsules to cheer them on without seeing the message, and lets owners collect accrued tips.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (Create New Capsule, left column): the user fills (1) Capsule Title (placeholder "Give your capsule a name"), (2) Secret Message textarea (placeholder "Enter your secret message"), (3) Lock Duration as a number of days (default 30, min 1 / max 3650), (4) Category dropdown (Personal / Gift / Memorial / Announcement / Secret), and (5) a Visibility checkbox toggle whose helper text flips between "Only you can reveal after unlock" (Private) and "Anyone can reveal after unlock" (Public). Two muted notes warn the full message is stored locally on this device (keep a backup) and that "The 0.2 GAS is a refundable deposit, locked in the vault and returned to you when you reveal — not a spent fee." The full-width primary CTA reads "Create Capsule (0.2 GAS deposit)"; while submitting it shows "Sealing capsule...". Success seals the capsule on-chain ("Capsule sealed on-chain!"). Right column has two stacked panels. "Tip a Public Capsule" panel: explains tipping is NOT a reveal — sending 0.05 GAS credits the owner on-chain, the message stays sealed, the tipper only gets back the capsule id. It has a "Collect tips" button (owners pull accrued 0.05 GAS tips; busy state "Collecting...") plus a "Public capsules you can tip" sub-list with a "Refresh list" action (loading "Looking for public capsules..."); each candidate row shows #id, a category badge, an "Unlocks: <date>" line, and a "Tip (0.05 GAS)" button. Empty state: "No public, unrevealed capsules from other users right now." "Your Capsules" panel: empty shows a lock-icon mascot + "No capsules yet. Create your first one!"; populated shows cards each with #id, a status pill (Locked / Unlocked / Revealed), category badge, title, "Unlocks: <date>", revealed content, and either an "Open Capsule" button (when unlock date passed) or a "Not unlocked yet" hint. A conditional mint recovery banner ("Reusable Deposit Credit · <n> GAS") appears between hero and form when a 0.2 GAS deposit landed but its capsule never sealed, offering a "Withdraw Credit" button.

**Interface description.** Desktop ~1040px max-width, centered. Top: full-width hero card (white, radius 20px, soft shadow) with a left 56px rounded mint badge holding a small two-tone capsule pill graphic, then eyebrow "ON-CHAIN VAULT" (11px/700 uppercase, mint-strong #0fb174), title "Time Capsule" (22px/800, -0.02em ink #1e1e2e), subtitle "Lock content until future date". Below the head, a 3-up stat grid of inset #f7f8fa tiles, each a centered 24px/800 tabular figure over a tiny uppercase muted label: "TOTAL CAPSULES", "LOCKED", "REVEALED" (all 0 in the empty screenshot). Under the hero, a 2-column workspace (left 1.15fr / right 0.85fr, min 320px). Left = "Create New Capsule" white card containing the vertical form: text input, textarea, number input, a custom select with a single ink chevron, a custom rounded checkbox toggle row (inset card, mint check when on) with bold "Visibility" label + helper, two muted 12px storage/deposit notes, and the mint full-width pill CTA. When disabled the CTA uses a soft mint fill (#e4f8f0) with muted green text rather than flat grey. Right column stacks: a "Tip a Public Capsule" panel (white card with faint mint radial corner glow, eyebrow-style heading, descriptive paragraph, an inset "Collect tips" row, and the refreshable candidate list with #id / category-pill / unlock-date rows), then a "Your Capsules" white card showing either the centered lock-icon empty mascot or a vertical stack of capsule cards. Capsule cards: inset #f7f8fa, 14px radius, colored left border (mint = locked, green = ready, grey = revealed), header with #id + uppercase status pill, category badge, title, unlock meta. Identity is the mint/green Neo Soft palette: brand #16c784, strong #0fb174, soft #e4f8f0, on a light theme with hairline borders rgba(20,22,38,0.06) and soft shadows. Mobile 390px collapses to a single column (hero → form → tip panel → capsules).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero with all three stats at 0 (TOTAL CAPSULES / LOCKED / REVEALED), empty Create New Capsule form at defaults (Lock Duration 30, Category Personal, Visibility unchecked = Private hint), tip panel showing 'No public, unrevealed capsules from other users right now.', and Your Capsules showing the centered lock-icon mascot + 'No capsules yet. Create your first one!'. This is the rendered screenshot state.
- **Filled / ready to seal** — Form completed: a title, a typed secret message, a custom Lock Duration, a chosen Category, Visibility toggled (show both the Private and Public helper-text variants), with the mint 'Create Capsule (0.2 GAS deposit)' CTA active.
- **Sealing / submitting** — CTA in loading state reading 'Sealing capsule...' (disabled soft-mint state), form inputs locked while the on-chain bury is in flight.
- **Success — capsules populated** — After sealing, Your Capsules shows one or more capsule cards: a freshly created 'Locked' card (mint left border, mint 'Locked' pill, #id, category badge, title, 'Unlocks: <date>'), an 'Unlocked' card past its date with an 'Open Capsule' button, and a grey 'Revealed' card displaying the revealed message content. Stats reflect new counts.
- **Tip a public capsule — candidates present** — Tip panel with a populated 'Public capsules you can tip' list: rows of #id + category badge + 'Unlocks: <date>' each with a 'Tip (0.05 GAS)' button; 'Refresh list' visible (and its 'Looking for public capsules...' loading variant); a tip-in-progress button state.
- **Reusable deposit-credit recovery** — The conditional mint recovery banner between hero and form: 'Reusable Deposit Credit · <n> GAS' with the hint that a deposit landed but the capsule was not sealed, plus a 'Withdraw Credit' button (and its 'Withdrawing...' loading state).
- **Error / wrong chain** — Guard states surfaced as inline messaging: 'Connect your Neo wallet to seal a capsule.', 'Lock duration must be between 1 and 3650 days.', and the wrong-chain warning 'This app requires Neo N3. Please switch networks.'

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the app's signature mint/green accent (brand #16c784, strong #0fb174 for text/CTA, soft #e4f8f0 for chips and disabled fills). Signature visual = the small two-tone capsule pill graphic in the hero's 56px mint badge (a mint top cap over a translucent green body with a center band), echoing the "vault" metaphor. Reinforced by the lock/unlock iconography (manifest icon "lock"; empty-state lock mascot), uppercase mint eyebrows, rounded cards (12/14/16/20px) with soft shadows, pill status badges, and faint mint radial glows on the tip and recovery panels. Status color language: mint left-border = Locked, green = Unlocked/ready, grey = Revealed.

# NFT & Asset

## Soulbound Certificate  `soulbound-certificate`
*Category: nft*

> Issue permanent, non-transferable NEP-11 certificates that live in the recipient's wallet forever and can be verified by anyone via token ID.

**Purpose.** An issuer workspace for minting soulbound (non-transferable) NFT credentials on Neo N3. Organizations create reusable certificate templates (issuer name, category, supply cap, description), then issue named certificates to recipient wallets recognizing a specific achievement. Each certificate is a permanent NEP-11 badge that cannot be transferred or sold; anyone can verify a certificate by token ID, and the original issuing wallet can revoke one. Users open it to award credentials (course completions, event participation, memberships) and to let third parties trust and check them.

**Functional description.** Three logical flows, all on one workspace page. (1) CONNECT: header shows a green "Connect Wallet" button; once connected it becomes a green "Wallet connected: <hash>" chip. Wallet is required to create/issue/toggle/revoke; Verify is permissionless. (2) CREATE TEMPLATE (right column, "Create Template" card): fill a 2-col form — Certificate name (e.g. "Neo Course Completion"), Issuer name (e.g. "Neo Academy"), Category (e.g. "Course"), Max supply (numeric, 1–100000, default 1000), and a full-width Description textarea (default "Issued to graduates who completed the Neo builder track."). Press full-width green "Create Template" (→ "Creating…") to sign the template on-chain. New templates appear in "Your Templates". (3) ISSUE (left column, "Issue Certificate" card): pick an active template from a row of template chips (or it shows a "Selected template: <name> #id" row), then fill Template ID, Recipient address (Neo N3 address), Recipient name (e.g. "Alex Chen", max 60), Achievement (e.g. "Advanced track", max 120), and optional Memo (max 160). A "Live preview" renders the framed certificate artifact in real time as fields are typed. Press full-width green "Issue" (→ "Issuing…") to mint. Each issued certificate is added to the "My Certificates" list. (4) TEMPLATE MANAGEMENT ("Your Templates" card): each template card shows name + Active/Inactive status pill, description, and a 3-metric row (Issuer name / Category / Issued e.g. "0/1,000"); actions are "Issue Certificate" (or "Sold out" when issued≥maxSupply), a "Deactivate"/"Activate" toggle (→ "Updating…"), and a copy-icon button that copies an issuing shortcut deep-link. A "Refresh" button sits top-right. (5) VERIFY ("Verify" card): enter a Token ID and press "Lookup" (→ "Looking up…"). Result renders the full certificate artifact with a Valid/Revoked status chip, a soulbound note, a facts grid (Token ID / Recipient address / Generated time, + Revoked time if applicable), a token-ID QR code with caption "Scan to verify by token ID", and "Copy verify link" / "Share" buttons. If the connected wallet is the issuer of a valid certificate, a red "Revoke" button appears (→ "Revoking…"); otherwise it shows the note "Only the issuing wallet can revoke this certificate." Deep links auto-prefill: ?issueTemplateId pre-selects a template in the Issue panel; ?verifyTokenId auto-runs Lookup. A status strip below the hero echoes success/error messages and the tx hash.

**Interface description.** Light Neo Soft theme, green brand accent (#16c784 bright / #0fb174 strong / #e4f8f0 soft). Desktop ~1200px. HERO BAND (white card, radius-lg, soft shadow): left = a 48px green gradient rounded-square badge holding a custom "certificate person" glyph (rounded card outline + head + shoulders), then eyebrow "ISSUER WORKSPACE" (11px/800 uppercase, green-strong), title "Soulbound Certificate" (24px/800, -0.02em), subtitle "Celebrate real achievements with certificates that belong to the recipient forever and can never be sold.", and an inline metrics strip when data exists ("<n> Templates · <n> Active · <n> Certificates", figures bold tabular). Right edge = Connect Wallet button or green wallet-connected chip. Below hero: optional status strip (green soft for success with "tx <hash>" code, red-danger soft for errors). WORKSPACE = 2-equal-column grid (gap 16px), collapses to 1 col under 900px. LEFT COLUMN: "Issue Certificate" card (help copy, optional template-chip picker / selected-template row, 2-col form of 5 fields, a "LIVE PREVIEW" eyebrow + the certificate artifact, full-width green Issue button) then "My Certificates" card (list of cert rows: name + recipient on left, a Valid/Revoked badge + "Lookup" button on right; empty state otherwise). RIGHT COLUMN: "Your Templates" card (Refresh button top-right; list of template cards or empty state) then "Create Template" card (5-field form + full-width green Create button) then "Verify" card (Token ID input + green Lookup button inline, then result artifact or empty state). SIGNATURE COMPONENT — the CERTIFICATE ARTIFACT: a framed parchment keepsake that deliberately breaks from the green UI — warm cream radial-gradient background (#fffdf7→#f6efdc), gold hairline border (#e7dcc0) with an inset gold inner rule (the "frame"), gold-brown text (#9a7b32 uppercase issuer line, #3b3520 title, large #2c2615 recipient name 22px/850, #5a4f30 achievement), and a footer divided by a gold dashed rule holding a gold rosette/seal SVG (#c79a2e) + "SOULBOUND" seal label. Form fields = inset #f7f8fa fill, hairline border, 44px min height, green focus ring. Buttons = pill green primary (white text, soft green shadow), neutral secondary; status pills use green-soft (Active/Valid) and red-soft (Inactive/Revoked). QR is a 132px white-bordered square.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First run / wallet not connected** — Hero with green Connect Wallet button, no metrics strip. Left column: Issue Certificate card with empty fields and a faded draft certificate artifact showing placeholders ('Issuing organization', 'Certificate of Achievement', 'Recipient's name', 'Their achievement'); My Certificates shows empty state 'No certificates yet'. Right column: Your Templates shows the wallet-not-connected empty state ('Wallet not connected' + 'Connect a wallet to load your certificate templates.' + a Connect Wallet action button); Create Template form prefilled with defaults; Verify card empty state 'Nothing to show yet'.
- **Connected with templates (ready to issue)** — Hero shows wallet-connected green chip and metrics strip (e.g. '2 Templates · 1 Active · 14 Certificates'). Your Templates lists template cards with Active/Inactive pills, description, Issuer/Category/Issued metrics, and Issue/Toggle/Copy actions. Issue panel shows template chips to pick from; once selected, the 'Selected template: Name #id' row and a live (non-draft) certificate artifact filled with typed recipient/achievement.
- **Submitting / loading** — Active operation states: primary buttons show loading label and spinner ('Creating…', 'Issuing…', 'Updating…', 'Looking up…', 'Revoking…') and become disabled-grey; Verify card shows a centered spinner row with 'Looking up…'; disabled primary CTA renders as neutral grey fill with slate label.
- **Issue / create success** — Green status strip below hero with the success message ('Template created' / 'Certificate issued') and a 'tx <hash>' code chip; metrics increment; newly issued certificate appears in My Certificates list with a green 'Valid' badge.
- **Verify result — valid** — Verify card renders the full certificate artifact with a green 'Valid' status chip, the soulbound note ('Soulbound — this certificate is bound to the owner wallet and cannot be transferred or sold.'), a 3-up facts grid (Token ID / Recipient address / Generated), a 132px QR with caption 'Scan to verify by token ID', and Copy verify link / Share buttons. If the viewer is the issuer, a red Revoke button shows; otherwise the note 'Only the issuing wallet can revoke this certificate.'
- **Verify result — revoked** — Same artifact but with a red 'Revoked' status chip and an added 'Revoked' time row in the facts grid; no Revoke button (already revoked).
- **Error state** — Red-danger status strip below hero echoing the failure message (e.g. 'Certificate issue failed', 'Certificate lookup failed', 'Contract address not configured'); inline field validation marks invalid Max supply / required fields.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the platform green brand (#16c784 / #0fb174 strong / #e4f8f0 soft) for all chrome, CTAs, focus rings, and Active/Valid status. Signature visual is the framed CERTIFICATE ARTIFACT — a warm cream/parchment keepsake card (gold border + inset frame rule, gold-brown serif-feeling typographic hierarchy, gold rosette wax-seal SVG with "SOULBOUND" label) that intentionally contrasts the green UI so the credential reads as a treasured document, not a data row. Header identity glyph is a green-gradient badge with a custom "person on a certificate" line icon.

# Governance

## Council Governance  `council-governance`
*Category: governance*

> Create, inspect, and vote on Neo Council proposals — text or executable policy-parameter changes — from one focused on-chain workspace.

**Purpose.** Council Governance is the single workspace where Neo's top-21 council members manage on-chain governance: drafting proposals, watching live quorum, and casting For/Against votes that resolve through the governance contract. A user opens it to see active proposals, review a proposal's vote split against the pass line, vote when eligible, or draft a new text or policy-parameter-change proposal. Non-council wallets still read everything; the app drops into a clearly-labeled read-only mode rather than hiding data.

**Functional description.** Flow: (1) Land on the Active tab. All proposal data is read live from the governance contract, so the board populates with or without a wallet. (2) An access banner under the hero states eligibility — "Connect a council wallet to create proposals or vote. Proposal data remains readable." for no wallet; "This wallet is not a current council member, so the app is in read-only mode." for an outsider; "Council member verified. Proposal writes are enabled." (green) plus the truncated address for a verified seat. (3) Three pill tabs switch the workspace: Active (default), Create Proposal, History.

ACTIVE / HISTORY board: a vertical list of proposal cards. Each card shows a colored status badge (Active/Passed/Rejected/Revoked/Expired/Executed/Pending), the proposal id "#3" (mirrored Neo Community entries append "· neo.community"), an optional purple "Voted" chip, the title and description, then a horizontal vote bar — green For segment, red Against segment, both sized against the council-of-21 denominator, with a dark vertical "Pass line" tick marking the majority threshold. Below: counts "12 For / 3 Against", a caption "Needs 11 of 21 For to pass", and a meta row "Quorum: 15/21 · Council of 21", "Creator: NXa…b2c9", "Voting Ends: Jun 22, 14:30" (appends "· Ending soon" in amber when <24h left). Card actions: a full-width secondary "View Details", and for an active, non-mirrored proposal a two-button row — green "Vote For" and red "Vote Against" (both disabled unless a verified council member who hasn't voted). Mirrored neo.community proposals show a read-only note instead of vote buttons.

CREATE PROPOSAL tab: a workbench. A live "Draft" preview card (vertical "DRAFT" rail + type/duration meta + title + description, and for policy a method/value row) updates as the form is filled. Then: Proposal Type as two large radio cards — "Text" ("For discussion, signaling, and non-executing council decisions.") and "Policy Change" ("For executable Neo policy parameter changes after approval."). Duration as three chips — 3 Days / 7 Days (default) / 14 Days. Title input + Description textarea. When Policy Change is selected, a Policy Details group appears: a hint, six method chips (Set Fee Per Byte, Set Exec Fee Factor, Set Storage Price, Set Max Block Size, Set Max Transactions/Block, Set Max System Fee), and a numeric Policy Value field. A full-width primary "Submit Proposal" button (disabled until title+description filled and the wallet is a verified council member); on success the form clears and returns to Active.

DETAILS panel (opens on View Details): status badge + title + close ×, the full description, a definition grid (Type, Voting Ends, Created, Quorum), conditional lifecycle actions — "Finalize Proposal" (passed), "Execute Policy" (passed policy proposal), "Revoke Proposal" (own active proposal) — and an expandable "Proposal Details" with Proposal ID, Creator, Policy Method, Policy Value.

On-chain results: votes and proposals are real transactions; success toasts read "Vote recorded", "Proposal submitted", "Proposal finalized", "Policy change executed on-chain", "Proposal revoked". The manifest enables fireworks/confetti on success.

**Interface description.** Single centered column, max ~760px, on a #f7f8fa page. HERO is one white rounded card (radius 20, soft shadow): left a 46px circular soft-green (#e4f8f0) badge holding a green landmark/building glyph; center the eyebrow "LIVE ON-CHAIN GOVERNANCE" (11px, 700, uppercase, .12em, muted) over the H2 title "Council Governance" (~25px/800, -0.02em ink #1e1e2e) over the subtitle "Create council proposals, inspect live quorum, and cast on-chain for or against votes from one focused workspace."; top-right a small ghost "Refresh" pill with a green hairline border and deep-green text. Below sits a 4-up STAT GRID of #f7f8fa inset tiles (radius 14): "0 TOTAL PROPOSALS", "0 ACTIVE PROPOSALS", "0 HISTORY PROPOSALS" (figures 18-21px/800 tabular ink, labels 11px uppercase muted), and a fourth COUNCIL SEAT tile that swaps content/tint by state — "Not connected" (neutral) / "Verified" (green) / "Read-only" — each with a small status dot.

Under the hero: a full-width ACCESS BANNER — a subtle pill-rounded strip with a leading dot and one eligibility sentence (neutral when read-only, green-tinted "council-access--ok" when verified, with the short address in bold at the right). Then a 3-segment pill TAB BAR (radius 9999): Active / Create Proposal / History, each with a line icon; the active segment is a white pill with shadow on a #f7f8fa track.

WORKSPACE (white card, radius 20): Active/History render either the empty state, a loading skeleton, or the proposal list. Proposal cards are white insets with the status badge + mono id + optional "Voted" chip on top, title (~17px/700), description, the rounded vote-bar track (height ~10px, green fill + red against + dark pass-line tick + inset hairline), For/Against counts (green/red), the muted "Needs N of 21" caption, a wrapping meta row, then the secondary View Details button and the green/red vote button pair. Create tab is the workbench described above. The Details panel renders as an aside card below with a close ×.

Color/identity: Neo Soft green default — brand #16c784, strong #0fb174, deep #0b5e3e, soft #e4f8f0; #0a8050 filled primary CTAs (Submit Proposal, Create Proposal). Status badge palette: Active = soft-green on green text; Passed/Executed = blue (#e8f0ff / #2759a5); Rejected/Revoked = red (#ffecec / #b42318); Expired/Pending = grey (#f0f2f5 / #5b6472); Voted chip = purple (#f4f0ff / #5f3dc4). Vote bar For = #16c784, Against = #ea3943, pass-line tick = ink. Ids in monospace. Everything on white/#f7f8fa with hairline borders rgba(20,22,38,0.06).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / read-only empty (no wallet)** — Hero with all-zero stats and a 'Not connected' Council Seat tile. Neutral access banner: 'Connect a council wallet to create proposals or vote. Proposal data remains readable.' Active tab selected. Workspace shows the empty state: centered soft-green circular icon (document-check), heading 'No active proposals', hint 'The list is read from the governance contract. Create a proposal when there is a real council action to review.', and a row with a filled-green 'Create Proposal' button + ghost 'Refresh'.
- **Loading / first read** — Active board during the very first contract read: three skeleton proposal rows (pill + tag placeholders, a title line, a body line) plus a bottom note with a small spinner, bold 'Loading proposals…' and 'Reading the latest entries from the governance contract.' Shown only on first load, never as a terminal state.
- **Active board, verified member (filled/ready)** — Green 'council-access--ok' banner 'Council member verified. Proposal writes are enabled.' with truncated address. Stats show real counts and a green 'Verified' seat tile. List of proposal cards: status badges, vote bars with green/red segments and the dark pass-line tick, 'N For / M Against' counts, 'Needs 11 of 21 For to pass', quorum/creator/ends meta (one card flagged amber 'Ending soon'), and enabled green 'Vote For' / red 'Vote Against' buttons. Include one card with a purple 'Voted' chip (buttons disabled) and one mirrored '#x · neo.community' card showing the read-only note instead of vote buttons.
- **Create Proposal — policy change** — Create tab workbench: live Draft preview card (DRAFT rail, Policy Change · 7 Days meta, title, description, method/value row); Proposal Type as two radio cards with Policy Change selected; duration chips (7 Days active); Title input + Description textarea filled; Policy Details group with hint, six method chips (Set Fee Per Byte selected) and a numeric Policy Value field; full-width green 'Submit Proposal' CTA enabled.
- **Proposal details panel** — Opened detail aside: status badge + title + close ×, full description, definition grid (Type, Voting Ends, Created, Quorum 15/21 · Council of 21), and conditional lifecycle CTAs for a passed policy proposal — primary 'Finalize Proposal', secondary 'Execute Policy', plus a red 'Revoke Proposal' for an own active proposal — and an expandable 'Proposal Details' with Proposal ID, Creator, Policy Method, Policy Value.
- **Success / vote recorded** — Post-action confirmation: success toast ('Vote recorded' or 'Proposal submitted' / 'Policy change executed on-chain') with manifest fireworks/confetti; the voted card now shows the purple 'Voted' chip and its vote bar advanced past or short of the pass-line tick.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand accent (#16c784 bright for badges/Active status/For votes, #0a8050 filled primary CTAs, #0b5e3e deep green text, #e4f8f0 soft chips). Signature visuals: a green landmark/building (council house) glyph in the hero badge and tab; the horizontal quorum vote bar with a green For segment, red (#ea3943) Against segment, and a distinctive dark vertical 'Pass line' tick that keeps a split vote reading as undecided rather than failing. A semantic status-badge spectrum (green Active, blue Passed/Executed, red Rejected/Revoked, grey Expired/Pending, purple Voted) and a stateful Council Seat tile (Not connected / Verified / Read-only) anchor the governance identity. Monospace proposal ids reinforce the on-chain, civic feel.

## Custom Anchor  `custom-anchor`
*Category: governance*

> Stake NEO into a community-run 21-agent voting anchor to direct your voting power and earn a pro-rata share of the GAS it generates.

**Purpose.** Custom Anchor is the user-facing staking surface for NEO governance anchors created by teams or communities. A user opens it to lock NEO into a chosen anchor (identified by a "custom-anchor:slug:nonce" appId), keep their NEO's council voting power working through the anchor's 21 deterministic AA agents, claim accumulated GAS rewards, and redeem their NEO at any time. Power users can also register a brand-new anchor and provision its 21 agents. The flow deliberately hides low-level agent internals — agent routing/candidate changes live in a separate admin console.

**Functional description.** Core flow assumes a connected wallet (walletRequired + chain warning). FIRST RUN (no anchor): the action panel shows a "Get started" onboarding note with two buttons — "Browse anchors" (opens the Registered anchors disclosure) and "Register a new anchor" (opens the Register disclosure). LINKING AN ANCHOR: the user either pastes an id into the "Anchor appId" field (hint "Format: custom-anchor:slug:nonce"), or expands "Registered anchors", taps "Refresh list", and on a row (truncated id + a "Trust"/"Profit" mode chip) taps "Use". PRIMARY ACTIONS (under "Stake, redeem, or claim"): set "NEO amount" (whole integers only — NEO is indivisible, default "1"), then one of three token-tagged buttons in a row: "Stake NEO" (tag "spend NEO", filled green, the form submit), "Redeem NEO" (tag "return NEO"), "Claim GAS" (tag "collect GAS"). A demoted ghost "Refresh status" sits in a separate "Status" maintenance row. Stake requires the strict id shape + valid amount; redeem/claim/refresh accept any 1-64 char id so already-staked NEO stays reachable. INERT-ANCHOR GATE: if the linked anchor is registered but has 0/21 agents, an inline red alert "0/21 agents provisioned" appears with a checkbox the user must tick ("Stake anyway — I understand this anchor earns nothing yet") before Stake enables. A status strip below the buttons shows neutral guidance ("Enter or open an anchor link to begin"), the live workflow status ("Submitting stake transaction" -> "Stake transaction submitted"), or red error text. REGISTER FLOW (disclosure "Register a custom anchor"): enter a "New anchor appId", pick mode via a two-button toggle "Trust (voting)" / "Profit (yield)" (mode description updates below), supply the "Council candidates (21 public keys)" textarea (one compressed 02/03+64hex key per line) with a live "{n}/21 valid candidate keys" counter and a "Use default candidate set" ghost button (auto-filled when switching to Profit), then "Register anchor (1 GAS)". Registration runs 3 sequenced wallet txns with step status ("Step 1/3 — registering AA agent accounts" ... "Step 3/3 — provisioning the 21 agents"). CREDIT RECOVERY: when over/failed deposits leave recoverable credit, a "Contract credits" card appears with NEO/GAS credit rows and "Recover NEO"/"Recover GAS" buttons.

**Interface description.** Single-column stacked layout (~1200-1365px desktop, 16px vertical gaps), console shell, green finance theme, tabular-nums everywhere. (1) HERO BAND: rounded 20px card, subtle mint-to-white diagonal gradient (rgba(228,248,240,.7) -> white) on white, soft shadow. Left: eyebrow "NEO GOVERNANCE" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em, deep green #0fb174); title 30px/800 -0.02em ("Stake NEO to vote" when empty, "Anchor ready" when linked); muted 14px subtitle/body; a mint callout box (rgba(228,248,240,.5), green hairline) with bold green "What is an anchor?" + explainer; a facts row (12px/600 muted with ink-bold values) — "Anchor status: Waiting for anchor", "Agents: —", "Last tx: —". Right: an 84px circular brand-soft (#e4f8f0) "orbit" badge with deep-green ring; before linking it shows a 4-node AA-cluster glyph, once linked a big 28px/800 agent number; "AA" label (11px/700 uppercase) beneath. (2) ACTION PANEL (white card): section head = eyebrow "WALLET ACTIONS" + h3 "Stake, redeem, or claim" + muted body; optional onboarding note with two pill buttons; form with "Anchor appId" text field (hint line) + "NEO amount" numeric field; a 3-up button grid where each button has a stacked label + small token tag; one filled green CTA (Stake) and two soft/outline siblings; a demoted "Status" row with a ghost "Refresh status"; status strip(s) at the bottom (neutral grey / green / red error variants). (3) METRICS CARD (white, 20px): 4 equal tiles in inset panels (16px radius, #f7f8fa) — "YOUR STAKE 0 NEO", "CLAIMABLE GAS 0 GAS", "REWARD RESERVE 0 GAS", "TOTAL STAKED 0 NEO" (labels 11px uppercase muted, figures 18-24px/800 tabular). (4) REWARD-MODEL CARD: head "How rewards work" + optional right-aligned "GAS / NEO (cumulative): 0" pill; paragraph + caption. (5) Optional CREDIT card. (6) Three collapsible disclosure rows (white, chevron summaries): "Registered anchors", "Register a custom anchor", "Routing details". Borders are hairline rgba(20,22,38,.06); pill buttons radius 9999px.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (no anchor linked)** — Hero title 'Stake NEO to vote' + full 'What is an anchor?' explainer, facts row all '—', orbit shows the AA-cluster glyph. Action panel shows the 'Get started' onboarding note with 'Browse anchors' + 'Register a new anchor' buttons; all three action buttons disabled; status strip neutral 'Enter or open an anchor link to begin'. Metrics all 0; three disclosures collapsed.
- **Browse anchors expanded** — 'Registered anchors' disclosure open: header with 'Browse anchors' label + 'Refresh list' button, then either empty text 'No registered anchors found yet.' or a list of rows each with truncated appId, a 'Trust'/'Profit' mode chip (tooltip = mode description), and a 'Use' button.
- **Anchor linked / ready to act** — Hero title 'Anchor ready' with truncated appId, facts show 'Anchor linked' + real agent count + last tx; orbit shows the big agent number. Amount field filled (e.g. 10), Stake CTA green/enabled, Redeem + Claim enabled; reward-model card shows the 'GAS / NEO (cumulative)' rate. Metrics populated with real stake/rewards.
- **Inert-anchor stake gate (0/21 agents)** — Linked anchor that is registered but has 0 agents: inline red alert 'X 0/21 agents provisioned' with body warning + a required checkbox 'Stake anyway — I understand this anchor earns nothing yet'; Stake stays disabled until checked, then flips to 'Confirmed — staking enabled'.
- **Submitting / loading** — Active action button shows 'Submitting...', buttons disabled (busy), status strip shows the live step text e.g. 'Submitting stake transaction' or, for registration, 'Step 2/3 — registering the anchor app'.
- **Success result** — Status strip in green/normal variant showing 'Stake transaction submitted' (or redeem/claim/register submitted), hero 'Last tx' fact + Routing-details 'Launch source' updated to the truncated txid/appId, metrics refreshed.
- **Error state** — Red status strip with the failure message (e.g. 'Anchor appId must look like custom-anchor:slug:nonce.', 'NEO is indivisible. Enter a positive whole number.', or a contract error). For an unregistered id, a separate red strip 'This anchor id is not registered yet. Register it below...' plus a 'Not registered' status badge in the hero facts.
- **Register a custom anchor expanded** — Disclosure open: 'New anchor appId' field, Trust/Profit two-button mode toggle with live description, the 21-key 'Council candidates' textarea with '{n}/21 valid candidate keys' counter + 'Use default candidate set' button, and the 'Register anchor (1 GAS)' submit; two explanatory notes below about 3-tx provisioning.
- **Contract credits card** — Appears only with recoverable credit: section head 'Contract credits' + body, then NEO/GAS credit rows each with the amount and a green 'Recover NEO' / 'Recover GAS' button (shows 'Submitting...' while recovering).

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand accent — bright #16c784 for decorative chips/rings, deep green #0fb174 / #0a7a50 for eyebrows and accent text, soft mint #e4f8f0 fills, one filled-green CTA per row. Signature visual: the 84px circular "orbit" badge in the hero that morphs from a 4-node AA-cluster line glyph (empty) into a large tabular agent count (linked), with an uppercase "AA" caption — the emblem of the 21-agent model. A mint diagonal gradient on the hero card and a mint 'What is an anchor?' callout reinforce the governance/civic, finance-density feel. Trust vs Profit modes are distinguished by neutral pill chips, not separate hues.

## Gov Merc  `gov-merc`
*Category: governance*

> A two-sided governance auction where you stake NEO to earn the GAS yield, or bid GAS to win the epoch's on-chain influence title.

**Purpose.** Gov Merc ("Governance Influence Auction") is a recurring, epoch-based two-sided market on Neo N3. NEO stakers lock NEO and split each epoch's winning GAS bid pro-rata; "mercenaries" bid GAS to be recorded on-chain as that epoch's influence holder. A user opens it to either earn passive GAS yield by staking NEO, or to spend GAS to win a public, on-chain signal of governance support. The contract is honest that the title is a SIGNAL, not an executed vote — staked NEO is never delegated or voted by the contract.

**Functional description.** Core loop runs against the on-chain MiniAppGovMerc contract. (1) CONNECT — wallet starts disconnected ("Wallet not connected"); a "Connect your Neo wallet to take part" note with a green "Connect wallet" CTA appears; any first action also triggers the prompt. (2) STAKE NEO — in the "Stake NEO" action card, enter a WHOLE NEO amount (suffix "NEO", validation "Enter a whole NEO amount"), press "Stake NEO"; this weights your pro-rata share of auction yield. "Unstake NEO" card releases staked NEO (disabled / "Amount exceeds your deposits" if over balance). (3) BID GAS — in the "Place Bid" card (GAS-tagged), enter GAS (suffix "GAS", placeholder = min bid, "First bid must be at least {min} GAS"), press "Place Bid"; the FIRST valid bid of an epoch opens a fixed timed window (e.g. "First bid opens a 5-minute window"). Bids during the window compete; the bidding window counts down ("Closes in m:ss") then closes ("Bidding closed — ready to settle") — later bids are rejected and the GAS is held as reusable bid credit. (4) SETTLE — once the window closes AND there is ≥1 bid, the "Settle epoch" button unlocks (permissionless): the top GAS bidder is recorded as the epoch winner, their bid is paid to NEO stakers pro-rata, and the epoch advances. Hints gate it: "Place at least one GAS bid…" (no bids) and "Settlement unlocks when the bidding window ends" (window still open). (5) STAKER REWARDS — "Claim rewards" pulls accrued GAS auction revenue ("Claimable rewards", 4-dp); "Reclaim bids" pulls back a losing bid from a settled epoch ("Reclaim epoch {n}"); "Withdraw unused credit" reclaims unspent prepaid GAS bid credit. Result feedback is toast-style copy: "Deposit submitted", "Bid submitted", "Epoch settled — winning bid paid to stakers", "Rewards claimed", "Bid reclaimed", plus failure/refund states ("Bid failed — your GAS was refunded"). Designer must keep the NEO-stake vs GAS-bid distinction unmistakable everywhere.

**Interface description.** Two-zone layout on a near-white #f5f6f3 page: a wide main column (left) + a sticky side rail (right). HERO BAND: a soft white→mint gradient card (linear 135deg #ffffff→#e4f8f0, radius 20px, soft shadow). Left side has an eyebrow ("Neo N3 governance desk"), the H2 title "Governance Influence Auction" (24-30px/800, -0.02em), a subtitle, a civic-framing paragraph (winning = on-chain influence holder, a signal not a vote), then a "Your Deposits / 0 NEO" mini-stat and a status pill with a green dot ("Ready" / "Loading…"). Right side is a SCOREBOARD of 5 stacked stat rows (label + bold tabular figure): Total Pool (NEO), Current top bid (GAS), Last GAS to stakers (GAS), Active Bids (count), Current Epoch (count). ACTION PANEL (white card, "Your actions" eyebrow + short wallet address on the right): a "Two tokens, two roles" legend row with two pills — green NEO chip ("NEO — stake for a reward share") and a GAS chip ("GAS — bid for the influence title") — then a 3-card action grid: "Stake NEO" + "Unstake NEO" (both NEO-tagged "Staked in NEO") and "Place Bid" (GAS-tagged "Paid in GAS"); each card = head label + token tag + hint + number input (NEO/GAS suffix) + button. EPOCH FLOW strip: three numbered steps "01 Stake NEO for yield / 02 Run GAS auction / 03 Settle & pay stakers" in bordered tiles with a left green accent. Below, an "How influence is used" disclosure note (left green border). SIDE RAIL (4 stacked white cards, each with green left-accent + soft shadow): (1) "Bid Leaderboard" with a count badge — ranked rows (rank • short address • GAS amount) or empty state "No active bids yet / The first valid GAS bid becomes the market signal for this epoch."; (2) "Settle epoch #{n}" — copy + a "Bidding window" status block (unopened/countdown/closed; turns into a closed treatment) + "Last settled" row + green "Settle epoch" CTA + contextual hint; (3) "Staker rewards" — claimable-rewards amount + "Claim rewards" CTA, then a "Reclaim bids" sub-block with reclaim list and "Withdraw unused credit"; (4) "Operator readiness" — a collapsed <details> ("Contract & settlement details") revealing the on-chain contract note + Settlement window / Execution path rows. Identity: single bright-green brand (#16c784) accent, deep green (#0b5e3e) for green text, soft mint (#e4f8f0) fills, dark forest ink (#102019/#122019) headings; pill buttons, rounded 14-20px cards. Two tabs at shell level: "Pool" (default) and "Stats".

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Disconnected / first-run** — Wallet not connected. Hero shows '0 NEO' deposits and 'Ready' pill; scoreboard at zeros (Total Pool 0 NEO, top bid 0.00 GAS, epoch 0). Action panel shows the 'Connect your Neo wallet to take part' note with green 'Connect wallet' CTA above the token legend + 3 action cards (inputs present but actions prompt connect). Side rail: empty Bid Leaderboard ('No active bids yet'), Settle card showing 'First bid opens a {minutes}-minute window' and 'No epoch settled yet', Staker rewards at 0, collapsed Operator readiness.
- **Connected, ready to act** — Short wallet address shown in 'Your actions' and 'Staker rewards' headings. User's staked NEO populated in hero + scoreboard live. Token legend + 3 action cards all enabled; valid amounts enable their buttons. Bid window 'unopened' until a first bid lands.
- **Bidding window open (countdown)** — Leaderboard lists ranked bids (1, short addr, GAS). Settle card's 'Bidding window' shows 'Closes in m:ss' live countdown; Settle button disabled with hint 'Settlement unlocks when the bidding window ends.' Place Bid still enabled to outbid.
- **Bidding closed — ready to settle** — Window block switches to closed treatment 'Bidding closed — ready to settle'; Place Bid card disabled with 'Bidding for this epoch has closed' field error; Settle epoch CTA now active (permissionless).
- **Submitting / busy** — Any action in flight: the pressed button shows loading spinner and all action/settle/claim buttons disable (isBusy). Hero pill may read 'Loading…' during data refresh.
- **Success / result** — Toast-style confirmation copy after an action: 'Deposit submitted', 'Bid submitted', 'Epoch settled — winning bid paid to stakers', 'Rewards claimed', 'Bid reclaimed'. Post-settle: epoch advances, leaderboard clears, 'Last settled' row + 'Last GAS to stakers' update, Claimable rewards rise for stakers.
- **Staker reward & reclaim states** — Staker rewards card with claimable amount + active 'Claim rewards'; reclaim sub-block listing 'Reclaim epoch {n}' rows when losing bids exist (else 'No losing bids to reclaim') and 'Withdraw unused credit' enabled only when unused GAS bid credit > 0.
- **Error / refund** — Failure copy: 'Bid failed — your GAS was refunded', recoverable 'Bid failed and the automatic refund did not complete. Your GAS is safe in your payment balance…', 'Amount exceeds your deposits' (unstake), 'Contract not configured'. Field-level errors render in red under the relevant input.

**Visual identity.** Green default (single bright brand #16c784, deep #0b5e3e green text, soft mint #e4f8f0 fills) on a forest-tinted ink (#102019/#122019) and a barely-warm off-white #f5f6f3 page. Signature visuals: the white→mint hero gradient card; a persistent two-pill token legend that color-codes NEO (stake) vs GAS (bid) so the two roles never blur; a live bidding-window countdown ring/badge ('Closes in m:ss' → 'Bidding closed'); a ranked bid leaderboard; numbered 01/02/03 epoch-flow tiles. Restrained, governance-desk professional rather than playful — no mascot; empty states use a heading + hint line.

## Quadratic Funding  `quadratic-funding`
*Category: governance*

> A single grant console where communities run quadratic-funding rounds — fund a matching pool, register projects, contribute GAS, and finalize matches on-chain.

**Purpose.** Quadratic Funding lets a community amplify small public-goods donations with a shared matching pool, using the quadratic-matching rule that favors breadth of donors over size of any single cheque. A round operator creates a round, funds it with GAS, tops it up, and finalizes matches; project teams register projects; donors contribute GAS. Everything happens from one console with wallet signing.

**Functional description.** Three-tab workflow driven from a hero band; wallet connection is required (walletRequired, chain-warning on wrong network). TAB 1 "Rounds (N)" — left "Create Round" form: Round title (placeholder "Public Goods Round"), Matching asset selector (GAS default / NEO), Matching pool number input with the active-asset suffix (placeholder "50", hint "Matching pool is locked at creation; you can top up later."), Start time + End time text fields (format "YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm", hint "Local time, e.g. 2026-09-01 09:00"), and a Round description textarea ("Focus on open-source infra and education."); above the fields a green mechanic note: "Matching favors projects with more unique donors: 100 people giving 1 GAS each out-matches 1 person giving 100." Primary green "Create Round" button (-> "Creating..."). Right column "Funding Rounds" list with a Refresh button: each round card shows title + a status pill (Upcoming / Active / Ended / Finalized / Cancelled), description, and a 2x2 stat grid (Matching remaining, Total contributed, Schedule = start - end, Creator address); clicking a card selects it. When a round is selected, a "Round Ops" admin panel appears below the list with four groups: Add matching (number input + button), Finalize (admin-only note "Finalize is restricted to the platform admin and only after the round ends." + a suggested-match table [Project | Contributed | Donors | Suggested match] with a caveat that suggestions are an on-chain approximation of CLR, a "Finalize with suggested matches" button, and an "Advanced: enter match amounts manually" toggle revealing Project IDs (JSON) "[1,2,3]" and Matched amounts (JSON) "[100,50,25]" with a "Use these" prefill from known project IDs), Claim unused, and Cancel round (hint: only before start with no contributions; matching deposit refunded). TAB 2 "Projects (N)" — left "Register Project" form (Project name "Open Source Explorer", Project description "Building tools for Neo developers.", Project link "https://example.org"; requires a selected round, otherwise hint "Select or create a round before registering a project."); right "Project ledger" of project cards each showing name + Active/Inactive pill, description, stats (Total contributed, Donors, Matched), link, a "Contribute" button (jumps to tab 3 with the project ID prefilled), and a "Claim" button or "Claimed" pill when applicable. TAB 3 "Contribute" — single "Quick Contribute" card: a summary row (Selected Round), a green amplify note "Why your donation is amplified" with copy about donor breadth and an optional live "Estimated match for project #{id}: {match} (approx.)" line, then fields Round ID/Project ID + Amount (placeholder "2") + Memo (optional, "For open-source tooling") and a primary "Contribute" button. NEO contributions must be whole numbers ("NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number."). Hero exposes "Contribute" (jumps to tab 3) and "Refresh rounds"; a status banner (info/success/error) appears under the hero after actions.

**Interface description.** Single scrolling column (no persistent right rail in the rendered view) on a near-white #f4f5f7 canvas with a faint top-left green wash. TOP: rounded 24px hero card with a layered mint gradient (rgba(228,248,240) -> white) and soft shadow; top-left a circular mint badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 plant/sprout icon) beside an uppercase muted kicker "QUADRATIC FUNDING"; below, hero H1 ~26-30px/800 "Fund public goods with matching power" and a grey subtitle "Create rounds, register projects, contribute GAS, and finalize matching from one clean grant console."; when a round is selected, a dot-separated facts row (Selected round / Matching pool / Live rounds N/N) plus a thin mint-to-green progress bar; two pill buttons — filled deep-green "Contribute" (#0a8050, white text) and secondary outline "Refresh rounds". BELOW HERO: a row of three pill tabs — active "Rounds (1)" filled green, "Projects (0)" and "Contribute" as light secondary pills. WORKSPACE: a 2-column content grid (collapses to single column when there are no rounds). Cards are white, radius 12/16/20, hairline border rgba(20,22,38,0.06), soft shadow. The "Create Round" card leads with an inset green mechanic note (mint #e4f8f0 fill, small bar-chart line icon) then a light hint line, a 2-column form grid of labelled inset fields (#f7f8fa) with the matching-pool field showing a GAS suffix, then the full-width description textarea and a right-aligned green "Create Round" button. The "Funding Rounds" card carries a header Refresh button and, when empty, a centered empty state: a line database/stack icon, bold "No funding round selected", body "Create a GAS matching round or refresh the contract to load active community rounds.", and a fainter preview line. Status pills are rounded with state colors; the admin "Round Ops" panel stacks four grouped sections with uppercase group titles and a compact match table. Identity = green Neo Soft: brand #16c784 for badge/pills/soft chips, #0a8050 for filled CTAs, deep green #0b5e3e for green text, mint #e4f8f0 for note/badge fills.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (Rounds)** — Wallet connected, no rounds yet. Hero with no facts row and no progress bar (hero-empty body shown). Tabs read Rounds (0) / Projects (0) / Contribute. Content grid collapses to a single column: the Create Round form, then the Funding Rounds card showing its centered empty state (database/stack line icon, 'No funding round selected', body + faint preview line). No Round Ops panel.
- **Filled / ready (Rounds with selection)** — At least one round exists and one is selected. Hero shows the facts row (Selected round / Matching pool / Live rounds 1/2) and the mint-to-green progress bar. 2-column workspace: Create Round form left; right column = Funding Rounds list of selectable cards (selected card highlighted, status pills Active/Upcoming/Ended) stacked above the expanded 'Round Ops' admin panel (Add matching, Finalize with suggested-match table, Claim unused, Cancel round).
- **Submitting / loading** — An action in flight: primary button shows its loading label and spinner ('Creating...', 'Registering...', 'Contributing...', 'Adding...', 'Finalizing...', 'Claiming...', 'Cancelling...') and is disabled; Refresh buttons disabled while refreshing. Project ledger can show a 'Loading...' row.
- **Success / result** — Post-action success status banner under the hero (green/success variant) e.g. 'Round created', 'Project registered', 'Contribution sent', 'Round finalized', 'Matching pool updated', 'Round cancelled and matching refunded'. Form fields reset on success. In Projects, finalized projects expose a 'Claim' button or show a 'Claimed' pill.
- **Error / blocked state** — Error status banner (error variant) e.g. 'Action failed — please try again', 'Invalid matching pool amount', 'End time must be in the future', 'NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number.', 'Contract address not configured'. Guard states: Register Project disabled with hint 'Select or create a round before registering a project.'; Contribute disabled with 'Select a round before contributing.'; Finalize gated by 'Connect the platform admin wallet to finalize this round.'
- **Contribute tab (donor view)** — Single 'Quick Contribute' card: summary row (Selected Round value or 'No funding round selected'), green amplify note 'Why your donation is amplified' with breadth copy and an optional live 'Estimated match for project #{id}: {match} (approx.)' line + caveat, then Round ID / Project ID / Amount (placeholder 2) / Memo fields and a primary green Contribute button.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo green identity. Brand #16c784 (badges, status pills, soft chips, progress-bar gradient end), #0a8050 filled CTAs with white text (hover #086a45), #0b5e3e deep green for green text on light surfaces, mint #e4f8f0 for note/badge/progress-track fills. Signature visuals: the circular mint hero badge with a sprouting-plant/seedling line icon (public-goods "growth"), a thin mint-to-green round-progress bar in the hero, and the green "amplify"/mechanic note blocks with a small line-chart icon that teach quadratic matching. Hero uses a layered mint-to-white 135deg gradient; canvas #f4f5f7 with a subtle green corner wash.

## TrustAnchor  `trustanchor`
*Category: governance*

> Stake your NEO into a governance-aligned pool that votes via 21 anchor agents and claim the GAS those council votes earn.

**Purpose.** TrustAnchor is a user-facing NEO staking app where a user deposits whole NEO into a shared governance-aligned anchor pool. The pooled NEO is voted by 21 AA agent accounts toward Neo council candidates, and the GAS those votes generate is funded into a reward reserve and split across stakers pro-rata by share of total NEO. A user opens it to stake NEO, watch their claimable GAS accrue (variable, no fixed APR), claim that GAS, and redeem their NEO. It is a separate pool from the sibling ProfitAnchor app, and the manual 21-agent route management lives in a separate TrustAnchor Admin app, so this surface is read-and-act only for end stakers.

**Functional description.** Core flow: connect wallet -> the hero and stat strip hydrate from chain (until then every figure shows an em-dash "—" placeholder, distinguishing "not loaded" from a real on-chain 0) -> in the "Stake, redeem, or claim" action panel, type a whole NEO amount into the "NEO amount" number field (default "1", min 1, integer only — NEO is indivisible; non-integers trigger inline error "NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number") -> choose one of four buttons. (1) "Stake NEO" (primary green) deposits whole NEO and starts earning distributed GAS; (2) "Redeem NEO" (secondary) withdraws whole NEO once the on-chain tx confirms; (3) "Claim GAS" (secondary) claims accumulated GAS — it is DISABLED until rewards have accrued, with caption "Claim GAS unlocks once your stake has accrued rewards." and tooltip "No GAS accrued yet"; (4) "Refresh status" (ghost) re-reads chain state. While a tx is in flight the pressed button shows "Submitting..." and ALL buttons disable (shared in-flight lock). Below the buttons sits a persistent note: "Redeem returns your NEO once the on-chain transaction confirms. Claim your GAS first — it stays claimable, but redeeming does not auto-claim it." A status strip (aria-live) shows workflow status ("Ready for wallet action" default), turns red on error, and appends "Last tx: <truncated 0x10…last8>" after a submission. A conditional recovery card appears only when Claimable NEO > 0: it shows the "Claimable NEO" label, the amount, the hint "A bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit." and a primary "Recover NEO" button. After actions, a "Recent actions" list (max 4 rows) shows action name, amount/status, and truncated txid, headed by the selected agent (e.g. "#3"). On-chain behavior a designer must reflect: figures are dynamic (My stake, Claimable GAS, Tracked NEO, Reward Reserve, Claimable NEO, GAS/NEO lifetime, agents X/21); the right-hand route card names the single council candidate the user's NEO currently votes for.

**Interface description.** Single "Overview" tab, light Neo Soft theme, green accent (brand strong #0fb174 / soft #e4f8f0 / deep text #0b5e3e), white cards on subtle #f7f8fa insets, rounded 16/20px cards, hairline borders. Top: a wide hero "primary card" (subtle green gradient wash) with a left lead group — a rounded green shield-check icon badge, an uppercase kicker "TrustAnchor", an H2 title "Governance-aligned NEO staking" (~24-30px/800, -0.02em), and a long descriptive paragraph; when not connected, a green connect-prompt line "Connect a wallet to stake NEO and route your council vote." On the hero's right is a "score" block: the large My-stake figure (or "—") above a small "My stake" label. Below the hero: a 4-up stat-chip grid (2-up on mobile) — "Claimable GAS", "Tracked NEO", "Reward Reserve", "Claimable NEO" — each a subtle-fill chip with a bold green tabular-nums value and a muted label. Then a full-width "How you earn" card (green-tinted) with a title and a 3-item bullet list explaining the vote-to-GAS mechanic. Then the main "TrustAnchor staking workspace": a two-column grid (~1.05fr / 0.95fr). LEFT = "anchor-status-card" containing a section head (eyebrow "Wallet actions" + H3 "Stake, redeem, or claim" + body copy), the "NEO amount" labeled number input, a row of 4 pill-ish buttons (primary green Stake; outline Redeem; outline Claim GAS; ghost Refresh status), the claim caption + redeem note, the optional green recover card, the status strip with the txid code chip, and the optional "Recent actions" history block. RIGHT = an "anchor-route-card" aside: label "Where your vote goes", eyebrow "Voting for candidate", a large candidate value (e.g. "#3" or "—"), caption "Your staked NEO votes for this council candidate…", then a definition list of Status / "Vote routes live" (e.g. "—/21") / "Reward pool" / "GAS / NEO (lifetime)", and a closing caption clarifying the lifetime metric. Below the workspace: a collapsible <details> "How routing is protected" card whose summary shows a "{count}/21 agents" hint and which expands to route lines plus an agent list (each row = truncated agent address + "candidate N" status). Footer strip reads "How routing is protected".

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / not connected (empty)** — Wallet not connected, no chain data: hero shows the green connect-prompt 'Connect a wallet to stake NEO and route your council vote.'; My-stake score and ALL four stat chips show the '—' em-dash placeholder; route card candidate value and Status/routes/pool/GAS-per-NEO all show '—' (e.g. '—/21'); action panel visible with default amount '1'; Claim GAS button disabled with caption; status strip reads 'Ready for wallet action'; no recover card, no Recent actions, routing <details> collapsed.
- **Connected / ready (filled)** — Chain data hydrated: hero My-stake shows real figure (e.g. '12 NEO'); stat chips populated (Claimable GAS, Tracked NEO, Reward Reserve, Claimable NEO); route card names a candidate (e.g. '#3', Status 'Route selected', 'Vote routes live' 21/21, Reward pool, lifetime GAS/NEO); amount field editable; Stake + Redeem enabled; Refresh enabled; status strip 'Ready for wallet action'.
- **Submitting (in-flight)** — A transaction is being submitted: the pressed button label switches to 'Submitting...' and EVERY button in the panel is disabled (shared lock); amount field disabled; status strip in neutral state. Mock for each of Stake/Redeem/Claim/Refresh/Recover.
- **Success / post-action** — After a confirmed submission: status strip shows the relevant success message (e.g. 'Stake transaction submitted' / 'Reward claim submitted') with a 'Last tx: 0x12345678…abcdef12' code chip; figures refresh; 'Recent actions' list appears with up to 4 rows (action, amount or status, truncated txid) headed by the selected agent.
- **Claimable-credit recovery** — State where Claimable NEO > 0: the green recover card appears inside the action panel showing 'Claimable NEO' label, the amount (bold), hint 'A bare NEO deposit (no stake memo) landed as recoverable credit.', and a primary 'Recover NEO' button.
- **Error** — Validation or workflow failure: status strip switches to its red error variant showing the message (e.g. 'NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number', 'PlatformAnchor contract is not configured', or 'Action failed'); the offending number input shows aria-invalid styling.
- **Routing model expanded** — The 'How routing is protected' <details> opened: shows current-route line, '{count}/21 AA agents registered' line, an operators note, and the agent list — each row a truncated agent address with a 'candidate N' status chip (up to 21 rows).

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft green identity — brand strong #0fb174 / soft fill #e4f8f0 / deep green text #0b5e3e for green-on-light, filled green primary CTA. Signature visual: a green shield-check icon (the "anchor"/trust mark) in the hero badge, reinforcing the governance/trust theme. Secondary accents: subtle green gradient wash on the hero and "How you earn" cards, green tabular-nums stat figures, and an em-dash "—" placeholder convention used everywhere until chain data hydrates. The "21 agents / X/21 vote routes" motif and a single-candidate "#N" focus on the route card are recurring identity cues.
