# Account Abstraction

## AA Account Lab  `aa-account-lab`
*Category: account-abstraction*

> A developer console for inspecting live Neo account-abstraction shells and registering new AA accounts with an explicit verifier, recovery owner, and escape timelock.

**Purpose.** AA Account Lab is the control center for Neo's Account Abstraction (AA) layer. A user opens it to (1) look up the on-chain configuration of an existing AA "account shell" — which verifier contract authorizes its transactions, which guard hook runs, who the backup recovery owner is, and whether a recovery is currently in progress — and (2) register a brand-new permanent AA account, choosing the verifier, optional hook, backup owner, and the escape-timelock recovery window. It exists so AA operators can verify recovery posture before trusting an account and can mint new self-custodial accounts with deliberate, visible guardrails rather than opaque defaults.

**Functional description.** Two parallel flows share one screen. LEFT — "Account Readiness" inspector: paste an AccountId Hash (hint "Use a 20-byte hash, public key, or deterministic seed text", placeholder "20-byte hash, pubkey, or seed text") then press the green "Inspect Account" primary button (or "Connect Wallet" secondary). Inspect performs 5 parallel chain reads (getVerifier / getHook / getBackupOwner / getEscapeTimelock / isEscapeActive) on the AA Core contract and replaces the empty "Account state" placeholder with a 6-tile detail grid: Current Verifier, Current Hook, Current Backup Owner, Escape Timelock (humanized e.g. "30 days"), Escape Status ("Escape active" / "Inactive"), and AA Core. Unset values render as an em-dash "—"; a muted explainer notes "Escape active = a backup recovery is currently in progress". If the read succeeds but no verifier exists, show "Read succeeded, but no verifier is registered for this account." Before any input, the button is disabled and shows "Enter an account id before reading AA Core state."

RIGHT — "Register New Account": a permanent-write form. Top note (always visible): "Registering creates a real, permanent AA account at the derived id — not a throwaway sandbox object. It will hold value and carry the recovery posture you set here forever." Required field "Verifier Hash" (placeholder 0x…, prefilled with the shared Web3Auth verifier) and "Verifier Params Hex" (placeholder "hex payload"). A collapsible "Optional (hook, backup owner, timelock)" group (open by default, chevron) holds: Hook Hash (placeholder "0x… or empty"), Backup Owner (placeholder "N… or 0x…", auto-prefilled from the connected wallet), and Escape Timelock in seconds (placeholder "2592000" = 30 days), with an explainer about the recovery speed-vs-safety tradeoff. Below the fields a read-only "Derived AccountId" preview computes live the only id the contract will accept (code-formatted 0x… or "—" until required fields are valid), with hint "The contract only accepts this id derived from the parameters above." Then a guardrail line: "This lab keeps optional hook and verifier params flexible, but blocks empty required fields before a write transaction." The green "Register Account" CTA is disabled until verifier + backup owner + valid timelock + a derived id are present and the backup owner matches the connected wallet. Contextual cautions stack above the CTA: a red danger alert "The backup owner must sign this transaction — connect that wallet or use your own address." on mismatch; a neutral caution "This account already has a verifier registered. A re-register will revert on-chain." after inspecting a registered id; and a red triangle-icon mainnet alert "You are on mainnet — Register Account is a real write against mainnet AA Core and spends GAS." After submit, the app polls getVerifier up to 4 times and auto-loads the new account back into the inspector ("Register transaction submitted").

**Interface description.** Console-shell layout, light theme, single green accent. HERO BAND (white card, radius 20, soft shadow): left a 46px circular soft-green badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 shield-with-check icon), an eyebrow "ACCOUNT ABSTRACTION" (11px/700 uppercase, green) above the H2 title "Account control center for Neo AA shells" (26px/800, -0.02em). Below, a body line in muted gray: "Inspect the live verifier, hook, and recovery owner before registering a new account shell with an explicit escape window." A compact meta row of two inset chips (#f7f8fa, radius 14): "NETWORK" → bold value (e.g. mainnet) and a wide "DEFAULT VERIFIER" → mono-font truncated 0x… hash with title tooltip.

WORKSPACE: a 2-column equal grid (minmax(0,1fr) twice, 20px gap, align-start so columns size independently). LEFT card "Account Readiness" (NeoCard erobo variant, radius 20): NeoInput "AccountId Hash" with hint + placeholder, a 2-up button grid (primary "Inspect Account" / secondary "Connect Wallet"), then either the 6-tile "account-detail-grid" of label/value cards (small uppercase muted label, bold ink value) or an empty StateView titled "Account state" with the blocked hint. RIGHT card "Register New Account": permanent note, blocked hint, two stacked NeoInputs (Verifier Hash, Verifier Params Hex), the open <details> "Optional…" disclosure with chevron containing Hook/Backup Owner/Escape Timelock inputs and a muted timelock explainer, the "Derived AccountId" status block (uppercase label + code value + hint), the guardrail hint, caution stack, and the full-width green "Register Account" CTA.

COLOR/IDENTITY: brand green throughout — soft #e4f8f0 fills and #0fb174/#0b5e3e green text/icons for decorative/badge elements; primary CTA fills #0a8050 with white text when enabled, and a faded soft-green (#e4f8f0 bg, green text) when disabled so it never looks dead. Cautions use a red danger variant with a warning triangle glyph. Inputs/insets sit on #f7f8fa hairline-bordered surfaces; hashes use a monospace font; stat figures use tabular-nums.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero with eyebrow+title+network/default-verifier chips. Inspector shows the AccountId input with placeholder and a centered empty 'Account state' placeholder plus 'Enter an account id before reading AA Core state.' Register card shows the permanent note, prefilled verifier hash, open optional group, 'Derived AccountId — —', and a disabled soft-green Register button.
- **Inspected / detail loaded** — Inspector's empty state replaced by the 6-tile detail grid (Current Verifier, Current Hook, Current Backup Owner, Escape Timelock e.g. '30 days', Escape Status 'Inactive', AA Core), unset values as em-dashes, plus the escape-status explainer. Show a variant with 'Read succeeded, but no verifier is registered for this account.'
- **Register ready (valid form)** — All required fields filled, backup owner = connected wallet; the 'Derived AccountId' shows a live 0x… code value; Register Account CTA is now full-saturation green (#0a8050, white text) and enabled.
- **Backup-owner mismatch / blocked** — Register form with a red danger alert 'The backup owner must sign this transaction — connect that wallet or use your own address.' and a still-disabled CTA; also show the 'Complete account id, verifier, backup owner, and timelock before submitting.' blocked hint.
- **Mainnet caution + submitting** — Above the CTA, the red triangle-icon mainnet alert 'You are on mainnet — Register Account is a real write against mainnet AA Core and spends GAS.'; CTA in loading/submitting spinner state. Optionally include the 'already registered' neutral caution after inspecting a registered id.
- **Success / confirmed** — After register polling resolves, the inspector auto-loads the freshly registered account: detail grid now shows the just-set verifier, backup owner, and escape timelock, communicating the new shell is live on-chain.

**Visual identity.** Green default ("Neo Soft" light theme), single accent hue. Signature visual: a circular soft-green shield-with-checkmark badge (security/recovery motif) in the hero, paired with a monospace hash treatment for all 0x… identifiers (verifier, hook, derived AccountId) and a live "Derived AccountId" preview block as the distinctive interaction. Red danger triangle for mainnet/recovery cautions is the only non-green color, reserved for risk.

## AA Permissions Lab  `aa-permissions-lab`
*Category: account-abstraction*

> A control room for inspecting and rotating an Account Abstraction account's verifier, hook, and backup-owner bindings under a two-phase timelock.

**Purpose.** AA Permissions Lab is a developer/owner console for managing the permission boundary of an Account Abstraction (AA) account on the shared AA core. A user opens it to first read an account's live security configuration — which verifier contract authenticates it, which hook contract enforces policy, and who the backup owner is — and then to safely rotate those bindings. Rotations are deliberately two-phase (propose → wait out a timelock → confirm or cancel), so a single compromised key cannot instantly hijack the account. It is a high-stakes, read-before-write tool, not a casual app.

**Functional description.** Two-stage flow split across a read column (left) and a write column (right).

READ / inspect: The user types a 20-byte AccountId Hash into the "Account inspector" card and clicks "Refresh State" (secondary button). They may also click "Connect Wallet" (primary) — the connected wallet must equal the account's backup owner to be allowed to write. Until a hash is entered, Refresh State is disabled and a warning caption reads "Enter an AccountId hash before reading live permission state." After a successful read, the "Current permissions" / "Live state" panel flips from an empty state to a detail list showing three mono-font values: Current Verifier, Current Hook, Current Backup Owner; the panel's status chip flips from "NOT INSPECTED" (grey) to "CONFIGURED" (green).

WRITE / rotate (two operation cards, each phase 1 then phase 2):
- Propose Verifier Update: inputs "Verifier Hash" (placeholder 0x...) and optional "Verifier Params Hex" (placeholder "hex payload", hint "Optional hex payload forwarded to the verifier."). Primary CTA "Propose Verifier Update" is disabled until an AccountId hash + verifier hash are present (and not blocked by ownership); a helper line "Enter an AccountId hash and verifier contract hash before submitting." shows when blocked.
- Propose Hook Update: input "Hook Hash" (0x...), secondary CTA "Propose Hook Update", same blocking/helper pattern.
Each propose only STARTS a timelock. When a rotation is pending, a green banner appears inside that card with a title ("Pending verifier rotation" / "Pending hook rotation"), an unlock status line (either "Unlocks at {date/time}." or, once elapsed, "Timelock elapsed — confirm to finalize."), a purpose line explaining the timelock is a safety window for the real owner to cancel, and a two-button row: "Confirm" (primary) + "Cancel" (secondary).

Ownership guard: if the connected wallet is not the inspected backup owner, both Propose CTAs are disabled and a warning alert reads "Connect the backup-owner wallet to rotate this account's bindings." On-chain results: propose emits a success toast ("Verifier update proposed — confirm after the timelock"); confirm/cancel emit "rotation confirmed" / "update cancelled". The designer must convey the read-then-write gating, the disabled/blocked states, and the propose→pending→confirm/cancel lifecycle.

**Interface description.** Console shell, centered column max ~1080px, light theme. TOP: a hero band card (radius 20px, soft shadow, subtle white→lavender gradient #ffffff→#f6f4ff) with a 48px rounded-square icon badge (soft green #e4f8f0 fill, green #0fb174 padlock-with-rotation glyph), an eyebrow "AA PERMISSION STATE" (11px/700 uppercase, green), an H2 title "Permission controls for AA accounts" (26px/800, -0.02em), and a body line about inspecting verifier/hook/backup-owner before rotating.

BELOW: a 2-column equal grid (collapses to 1 column under 980px).
LEFT column (read): (1) "ACCOUNT INSPECTOR" card (uppercase 11px card title) containing an "AccountId Hash" labeled input (placeholder "20-byte hash", hint "Required before reading or writing permission bindings."), a 2-up button row [Connect Wallet primary | Refresh State secondary], and a caption (warn variant = inset grey box when blocked). (2) "Current permissions" state panel: a section heading with eyebrow "LIVE STATE" + H3 "Current permissions" (18px/700) on the left and a pill status chip on the right ("NOT INSPECTED" grey / "CONFIGURED" green soft-fill). Body is either an empty state (robot mascot illustration + "Inspect an account to load its permissions" + hint) or a 3-row detail list on a subtle #f7f8fa inset card with uppercase mono labels and mono tabular-nums values.

RIGHT column (write): a heading block — eyebrow "PERMISSION UPDATE WORKFLOW" + H3 "Writes change the account permission boundary" — then a caption explaining two-phase rotations. Then two operation cards: "PROPOSE VERIFIER UPDATE" (Verifier Hash + Verifier Params Hex inputs, full-width primary CTA, hint caption, optional helper line) and "PROPOSE HOOK UPDATE" (Hook Hash input, full-width secondary CTA, hint). When pending, each card grows a green-bordered soft-green banner (title + unlock time + purpose + Confirm/Cancel 2-button row).

Components: NeoCard (variant erobo, radius 20px), NeoInput (label + hint + placeholder), NeoButton (pill, primary green-filled / secondary outline; disabled = grey #f7f8fa fill, muted text). Single green accent throughout (#0fb174 strong, #e4f8f0 soft, #1e1e2e ink). Note the hero gradient currently leans lavender — designer may keep it as a subtle tint or normalize to the green system.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — No AccountId entered. Account inspector input empty with placeholder '20-byte hash'; Refresh State disabled; warn caption 'Enter an AccountId hash before reading live permission state.' Live-state panel shows robot-mascot empty state ('Inspect an account to load its permissions') with grey 'NOT INSPECTED' chip. Right column operation cards visible but both Propose CTAs disabled with helper lines ('Enter an AccountId hash and verifier/hook contract hash before submitting.').
- **Ready to inspect** — A valid 20-byte AccountId hash typed in; Connect Wallet + Refresh State both active; caption switches to risk guidance 'Use a verified AccountId hash and confirm the target verifier or hook contract before signing.' State panel still empty/NOT INSPECTED until the read completes.
- **Inspecting / loading** — Refresh State button in loading spinner state after click; inputs locked; brief transitional state before the detail list populates.
- **Inspected / configured** — Read succeeded: status chip flips to green 'CONFIGURED'; detail list shows Current Verifier, Current Hook, Current Backup Owner as mono script hashes on the subtle inset card. If a wallet is connected and matches the backup owner, Propose CTAs become enabled.
- **Not backup owner (write blocked)** — Wallet connected but it is NOT the inspected backup owner: a red/warn alert 'Connect the backup-owner wallet to rotate this account's bindings.' sits above the operation cards; both Propose CTAs disabled.
- **Pending rotation (timelock counting)** — After Propose: green-bordered soft-green banner inside the operation card — title 'Pending verifier rotation', line 'Unlocks at {date/time}.', purpose line about the safety window, and a Confirm (disabled-feel) + Cancel button row.
- **Timelock elapsed / confirmable** — Same pending banner but status line reads 'Timelock elapsed — confirm to finalize.'; Confirm primary button is the clear active action, Cancel still available.
- **Success toast** — Post-action confirmation surfaced via toast: 'Verifier update proposed — confirm after the timelock' / 'Verifier rotation confirmed' / 'Pending verifier update cancelled' (and hook equivalents).

**Visual identity.** Single green brand accent (Neo Soft): strong #0fb174 for the eyebrows, icon glyph, pending banners, and CONFIGURED chip; soft #e4f8f0 fills for the icon badge, status chip background, and timelock banner; deep ink #1e1e2e titles; mono SF Mono / Fira Code for script-hash values (tabular-nums). Signature visual = the hero's padlock-with-rotation-arrows glyph (a lock crossed by a rotation stroke), reinforcing 'rotate keys, but safely behind a lock/timelock.' The pending-rotation timelock banner (green border + soft-green fill + Confirm/Cancel) is the app's most distinctive recurring component. Hero band currently uses a faint white→lavender gradient (#f6f4ff) as a subtle tint; everything else stays strictly on the green single-accent system. Serious, console/developer-tool tone — no playful color, restraint signals the high-stakes security context.

---

## AA Relay Console  `aa-relay-console`
*Category: account-abstraction*

> An operator desk to check paymaster sponsorship, request GAS coverage, and relay account-abstraction payloads on-chain without the account holding GAS.

**Purpose.** AA Relay Console is a guarded operator surface for account-abstraction (AA) gasless transactions on Neo. A user opens it to find out whether a specific AA account is covered by a paymaster (which pays the network GAS so the account can transact without holding any), to request that coverage from a shared daily budget, and then to submit a relay-ready JSON payload that a relayer broadcasts on-chain on the account's behalf. It is a focused debugging/operations console, not a wallet — nothing is signed or sent by the user's own wallet.

**Functional description.** Single shared input drives everything: the user types an AA Address (a Neo "N..." address, 34 chars, or a 0x/40-hex script hash). This is validated inline — bad input shows "Enter a valid Neo address (N...) or 0x script hash." and keeps the action buttons disabled. Below the input is a collapsible "How relaying works" disclosure explaining the paymaster benefit and that relay broadcasts via a relayer, not the user's wallet.

The workspace is two steps. STEP 1 · SPONSOR PREFLIGHT ("Check or request coverage"): a numeric "Sponsor Amount" field (GAS, default 0.1, hint "GAS amount requested from the paymaster for this AA scope.") plus two side-by-side buttons. "Check Sponsorship" (secondary) needs only a valid AA address and resolves eligibility; on success it surfaces "Eligible — {remaining} GAS of {dailyLimit} remaining today." or "Not eligible: {reason}". "Request Sponsorship" (primary) additionally needs a positive GAS amount and submits a coverage request ("Sponsor request submitted."). When blocked, an inline hint reads "Enter an AA address before checking or requesting sponsorship." or "Enter a positive GAS amount (e.g. 0.1)...".

STEP 2 · BUILD & SUBMIT ("Relay the payload"): an optional "Paymaster Dapp ID" text field (placeholder "Optional dapp id") and a "Relay Payload JSON" textarea pre-filled with a real, submittable example (a metaInvocation calling getNonce on the AA core). The "Submit Relay Payload" button enables only when the AA address is valid AND the JSON parses; otherwise an inline hint shows "Fix the JSON payload before submitting it to the relayer." or "Enter an AA address and keep the payload JSON valid before submitting." On success the relayer broadcasts on-chain ("Relay submitted successfully.").

Result behaviour: after any action a single human-readable result line appears at the bottom — a colored status dot (green ok / blue info / amber-coral warn), the headline sentence, and a dark pill showing the scoped AA address. If the relayer returns a txid/hash, an "On-chain transaction" link to the explorer is shown. A collapsible "Latest Relay Response" reveals the raw JSON. Service outages collapse to one honest line: "Sponsorship service is currently unavailable. The relay endpoint is not configured on this deployment."

**Interface description.** Single centered column, max-width ~1080px, on the light Neo Soft theme. Three stacked regions.

1) HERO band — white rounded card (radius 20px, soft shadow). Left: 48px circular green-soft badge (#e4f8f0 fill, #0fb174 shield-with-check icon). Right: eyebrow "RELAY" (11px/700 uppercase, green), title "Sponsored relay desk for AA payloads" (27px/800, ink #1e1e2e, -0.02em), and a one-line subtitle. Below sits a full-width inset fact strip (#f7f8fa, radius 14px) with three label+value facts separated by thin vertical dividers: NETWORK "mainnet", RELAY ENDPOINT "/api/aa/relay" (mono), AA CORE "0x0268a387913b250166ddec032b033..." (mono, ellipsis-truncated). Labels are 11px/700 uppercase muted; values 13px/800 tabular-nums with mono code for hashes.

2) SHARED ACCOUNT card — white rounded card with a plain-language explainer paragraph ("A paymaster can pay the network GAS for this account so it can transact without holding GAS..."), then the "AA Address" labeled input (placeholder "N...", hint "Required before sponsorship checks or relay submission."), then a collapsed "▸ How relaying works" disclosure (inset #f7f8fa pill row).

3) TWO-COLUMN WORKSPACE — two equal white cards side by side (stacks to one column below 960px). Each card has a header with a green eyebrow (e.g. "STEP 1 · SPONSOR PREFLIGHT") and an 800-weight title (16px) above a hairline divider. Left card: numeric Sponsor Amount input + a 2-up button grid (Check Sponsorship secondary / Request Sponsorship primary) + an inline hint box. Right card: Paymaster Dapp ID input, a tall Relay Payload JSON textarea showing pretty-printed JSON, an inline hint, and a full-width "Submit Relay Payload" primary button. Disabled buttons render as flat grey (#f7f8fa fill, muted text, no shadow).

Result block (conditional, full width): inset card with a status dot + bold headline + dark address pill, optional explorer tx link (green mono), and a "▸ Latest Relay Response" raw-JSON disclosure with a scrollable monospace pre.

Identity color is the standard green accent; status dots use green (#0fb174 ok), blue (#3e8cff info), coral (#f0795b warn).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero + fact strip populated (mainnet, /api/aa/relay, AA core hash). AA Address input empty with grey 'N...' placeholder. Both step cards visible: Check/Request buttons disabled (flat grey), Submit disabled. Step 1 hint shows 'Enter an AA address before checking or requesting sponsorship.'; Step 2 hint shows the blocked message. No result block.
- **Filled / ready** — Valid AA address entered, Sponsor Amount 0.1, valid pre-filled Relay Payload JSON. Check Sponsorship enabled (secondary), Request Sponsorship enabled (green primary), Submit Relay Payload enabled (green primary). Inline hints gone. 'How relaying works' optionally expanded showing the two explainer paragraphs.
- **Invalid input** — AA Address shows the error state 'Enter a valid Neo address (N...) or 0x script hash.' with actions disabled; OR Sponsor Amount non-positive showing 'Enter a positive GAS amount...'; OR Payload JSON malformed showing 'Fix the JSON payload before submitting it to the relayer.' with Submit disabled.
- **Loading / submitting** — An action in flight: the relevant button(s) show a loading spinner (Check/Request share isChecking; Submit shows isRelaying) and are disabled. Inputs remain visible/editable elsewhere.
- **Sponsorship result (success)** — Result block with green ok dot and headline 'Eligible — 4.5 GAS of 10 GAS remaining today.' (or 'Sponsor request submitted.'), dark AA address pill, and collapsed 'Latest Relay Response' raw JSON.
- **Relay submitted (on-chain)** — Result block, green ok dot, 'Relay submitted successfully.', dark address pill, plus an 'ON-CHAIN TRANSACTION' label with a green mono txid linking to the explorer; raw response collapsible below.
- **Not eligible / service unavailable** — Result block with amber-coral warn dot: either 'Not eligible: {reason}' or the single honest outage line 'Sponsorship service is currently unavailable. The relay endpoint is not configured on this deployment.'

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green accent (no secondary theme). Bright green #16c784 / #0fb174 for the decorative shield-check hero badge (on #e4f8f0 soft fill) and green eyebrows/primary CTAs; deep green for the explorer txid mono text. Signature visual is the security/shield-with-checkmark badge reinforcing the "guarded operator surface" idea, plus the operator-console aesthetic: monospace hashes/endpoints in the fact strip, a pretty-printed JSON payload textarea, and a dark monospace AA-address pill in the result line. Status semantics via three dot colors: green ok, blue info, coral warn.

## AA Session Key Lab  `aa-session-key-lab`
*Category: account-abstraction*

> A console for minting scoped, expiring session keys for a Neo account-abstraction account — generate a key in-browser, bind it to one contract + method, and write the verifier update on-chain.

**Purpose.** .

**Functional description.** Three-step linear flow plus a manage/readback region. STEP 1 — Key & sponsorship (left column): a "Wallet" status row shows connected/"not connected"; three buttons: "Generate Key" (primary) creates a 33-byte compressed P-256 session key locally in-browser and auto-fills the Session Public Key field below + reveals a one-time private-key export card; "Check Sponsorship" (secondary) queries the paymaster for the given Paymaster dApp ID (default "miniapp-aa-session-key-lab") and account, returning Eligible/Not eligible/Approved/Not approved; "Request Sponsorship" (secondary, disabled until Sponsor Amount is a positive number, default 0.1) submits a sponsor request. Two compact fields: Paymaster dApp ID, Sponsor Amount (number, GAS). A static numbered "Session setup workflow" reference card sits beneath: 1 Generate key, 2 Check sponsor, 3 Configure scope. STEP 2 — Configure Session Key (right column, the primary business action): a blocked-hint line shows until required fields are present ("Add an account ID/hash, session public key, target contract, and expiry before submitting."). Fields in groups — Identity: Account ID / Hash (seed string or 0x hash; live-derives a 0x Account ID Hash preview), Session Public Key (33-byte compressed hex). Scope group: Target Contract (0x… or N… address/script hash, live-normalized), Allowed Method (single symbol; if left blank an amber caution warns the key may call ANY method), Expiry Timestamp (unix seconds) with +1h / +24h / +7d quick-pick chips and a humanized "Expires {date}" preview. Limits & label (optional) — mainnet only, collapsed disclosure: Spending Limit (GAS, 0 = unlimited) and Description (e.g. "rewards bot"). "Configure Session Key" primary button (disabled until account+key+target+expiry set) calls aaCore.callVerifier(setSessionKey) on-chain. ONE-TIME PRIVATE KEY card (appears only after Generate Key): an alert-level caution, a Show/Hide toggle (masked as 6 chars…4 chars by default), the key in monospace, and a "Copy Private Key" button (→ "Copied", with a manual-copy fallback error if clipboard unavailable). LATEST CONFIGURATION region (full width): an "On-chain session key" card with right-aligned "Inspect Session Key" (read) and "Revoke Session Key" (danger, two-step: tap → "Revoke this on-chain session key?" Cancel / Confirm Revoke). When a key is live it shows Target Contract, Allowed Method, Expires, Spent / Limit (e.g. "0 / 5 GAS" or "0 GAS spent (no limit)"); otherwise "No session key is active for this account." Below: once a submit confirms, a detail-row list of the submitted config; otherwise a friendly empty state. A collapsible "Live state" disclosure at the very bottom reveals an environment grid: AA Core, Session Verifier, Account ID Hash, Target Contract.

**Interface description.** Console shell, light theme, ~1200px wide, single-column stack of full-width sections. HERO BAND: rounded 24px card with a subtle white→#f8fafc gradient; left a circular #e4f8f0 soft-green badge holding a green key/key-tilt line icon; eyebrow "SESSION KEYS" (11px, 700, uppercase, .12em, muted); title "Scoped session keys for safer AA actions" (~26-28px/800, -0.02em ink); subtitle "Generate a local session key, bind it to one contract and method, then submit the scoped verifier update through the connected wallet." Below the heading, a 3-up metric row (Session / Sponsor / Scope) — each a pill/inset tile with a small status dot, an uppercase label and a bold value ("pending" / "Not checked" / "claimRewards"); dot + tint goes green when settled (#0fb174) and amber (#f0a23a) while pending/broad. WORKSPACE: a two-column flow-stack. Left column has two stacked NeoCards — "KEY & SPONSORSHIP" (card title row "Key & sponsorship"): a "WALLET / not connected" status row, a 1×3 button grid (Generate Key filled green primary, Check Sponsorship + Request Sponsorship outlined secondary), then a 2-field compact row (Paymaster dApp ID, Sponsor Amount); and "SESSION SETUP WORKFLOW" — a numbered ordered list, each step a circled green-soft number + bold label + muted one-liner. Right column is the taller "CONFIGURE SESSION KEY" card: stacked labeled inputs (#f7f8fa inset fields, hairline borders, 11px uppercase labels), a "SCOPE" subgroup wrapper, three small pill chips (+1h +24h +7d) in green-soft, a muted "Expires …" line, a "LIMITS & LABEL (OPTIONAL)" disclosure with a + affordance, and a full-width filled-green "Configure Session Key" CTA. Below the workspace, the "SESSION / Latest Configuration" region: section eyebrow + h3 heading, a result card whose header reads "ON-CHAIN SESSION KEY" with right-aligned small "Inspect Session Key" (secondary) and "Revoke Session Key" (red/danger outline #d64545) buttons, then either decoded field rows or the muted line "No session key is active for this account.", then a centered empty state (the same green key icon + "Generate a key or submit a configuration to populate this board."). A bottom "LIVE STATE" collapsible row with a + toggle. Identity: standard Neo Soft green — brand #16c784, strong #0fb174 (icons/green text/chips), soft #e4f8f0 fills, #0a8050-class filled CTAs; amber #f0a23a / #fdf3e3 bg / #a35a00 text reserved for the "Any method"/pending cautions; danger red #d64545 for Revoke. Cards radius 20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,0.06), monospace for keys/hashes.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Wallet 'not connected', hero metrics all pending (Session 'pending', Sponsor 'Not checked', Scope showing default method or '—'). Configure card shows the blocked hint 'Add an account ID/hash, session public key, target contract, and expiry before submitting.' Latest Configuration shows 'No session key is active for this account.' plus the centered green-key empty-state illustration with 'Generate a key or submit a configuration to populate this board.' No private-key card present.
- **Key generated / private-key export** — After Generate Key: Session Public Key auto-filled; a prominent 'GENERATED PRIVATE KEY' card appears with an alert-level caution paragraph, a 'Ready for one-time copy' label, Show/Hide toggle (key masked as 6chars…4chars), the key in monospace, and a green 'Copy Private Key' button. Show this both masked (default) and the 'Copied' state.
- **Filled / ready to submit** — All required fields populated, Account ID Hash preview derived, Target Contract normalized to 0x…, an Allowed Method chosen (so the amber 'Any method' caution is gone), Expiry set via a quick-pick chip with the 'Expires {date}' preview line. Hero Scope metric green; 'Configure Session Key' CTA enabled. Optionally show the mainnet 'Limits & label' disclosure expanded with Spending Limit + Description.
- **Submitting** — 'Configure Session Key' (and/or sponsorship buttons) in loading state — spinner + disabled — while the on-chain verifier update is broadcast.
- **Active session live (success)** — Hero metrics green (Session 'configured', Sponsor 'Approved/Eligible', Scope = method). 'On-chain session key' card shows decoded rows: Target Contract, Allowed Method, Expires, Spent / Limit ('0 / 5 GAS' or '0 GAS spent (no limit)'); the Latest Configuration detail-row list is populated; Inspect + Revoke available.
- **Revoke confirmation** — Two-step destructive guard: the Revoke button is replaced inline by a prompt group — 'Revoke this on-chain session key?' with a ghost 'Cancel' and a red 'Confirm Revoke' (loading while revoking).
- **Warning / caution states** — Amber caution under Allowed Method when blank ('this session key will be allowed to call ANY method on the target contract'); sponsor-amount error ('Sponsor amount must be a positive number.'); and the 'broadcast but not yet confirmed on-chain — the session key was not stored' notice.

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green accent. Brand #16c784, strong #0fb174 for icons/green text/soft chips, soft fill #e4f8f0, filled-green CTAs with white text; deep green for text on light. Signature visual = a circular soft-green badge holding a key / tilted-key line icon (a tiny key with a sparkle/keyhead), reused in the hero badge and the empty-state mascot slot. A deliberate amber secondary tone (#f0a23a accents, #fdf3e3 fill, #a35a00 text) flags 'pending'/'Any method' caution and the one-time private-key warning; danger red #d64545 is reserved solely for Revoke. Status dots (green=settled, amber=pending) drive the hero Session/Sponsor/Scope readout. Monospace for keys, hashes, and the private-key value.

## Recovery Guardian  `recovery-guardian`
*Category: account-abstraction*

> A read-first social-recovery console for AA accounts: inspect an account's guardian state, then prepare shareable recovery links to hand off to the AA identity workspace for signing.

**Purpose.** Recovery Guardian is a console miniapp for inspecting and preparing account-abstraction (AA) account recovery. A user opens it to look up an account's bound verifier, backup owner, recovery timelock, and escape status before taking any ownership-changing action, then to draft a recovery (new owner + expiry, optional verifier override and credential template) and generate preview/credential links. It is deliberately non-executing — it reads state and produces links opened elsewhere (the AA identity / AA workspace) to actually sign. The value is safety: recovery links stay locked until every required field validates.

**Functional description.** Primary flow is a strict two-step gate: READ then PREPARE. (1) In the "Guardian Preflight" command card the user enters an Account Address / Script Hash (placeholder "N... or 0x...") and clicks "Query State" (secondary "Open Recovery Docs" beside it). Query State is disabled until the address validates as a Neo address or Hash160; an inline hint "Enter a valid account address or Hash160 before querying state." shows while blocked. The button shows a loading spinner while querying. (2) On success the "Recovery state" panel fills with read-only state cards (Account ID, Verifier, Backup Owner, Recovery Escape, Recovery Timelock, Checked At, plus conditionally Escape Triggered and Network Default Verifier) and a raw JSON payload block ("Latest State"). The status pill flips from "Awaiting query" (idle/grey) to "Latest State". (3) The "Prepare Recovery" side card stays LOCKED until a query succeeds (shows gate hint "Read the guardian state first. Run Query State, then prepare recovery links here."). Once unlocked, the user fills New Owner (Neo address/Hash160, required) and Recovery Expiry in minutes (required, 5–1440, default 30). An "Advanced (verifier override, template id)" disclosure holds optional Verifier Hash Override (compared case-insensitively to the bound verifier after query — badges "Override matches bound verifier" green or "Override differs from bound verifier" red) and Recovery Template ID. (4) Two link groups — "Recovery preview" and "Recovery credential" — each expose an "Open" primary button + copy icon button + share icon button. All six remain disabled until account + new owner + expiry + (optional) verifier + (optional) template all validate; blocked hint: "Complete a valid account, new owner, expiry window, and optional verifier override before using recovery links." Open/Copy/Share produce a URL handed off elsewhere; a bottom "guardian-link-preview" strip then shows the generated preview/credential URLs with the note "Open this link in the AA identity workspace to sign the recovery. The recovery transaction is not executed here." Footer buttons "Open Identity Workspace" and "Open AA Workspace" deep-link to the signing surfaces. No on-chain write ever happens in this app.

**Interface description.** Console shell, light theme, ~1200px desktop. TOP HERO BAND is two columns: left = eyebrow "RECOVERY GUARDIAN" (uppercase, tracked, muted) over title "Social recovery workspace" (24-30px/800), a description paragraph, and a 3-up metric row labeled Account / New Owner / Expiry Minutes that mirrors the form state (figures default to em-dash "—"). The hero left also carries a green shield-check icon badge. Right = a white "Guardian Preflight" command NeoCard containing the Account Address / Script Hash input with hint, and a 2-button action grid ("Query State" primary green, "Open Recovery Docs" secondary). BELOW the hero is a full-width 3-step flow strip: numbered cards 01 "Read state", 02 "Prepare recovery", 03 "Continue in AA workspace", each with a one-line description. MAIN WORKSPACE is 2 columns. LEFT (wider) = "Guardian Runtime" eyebrow + "Recovery state" h3 + status pill (right-aligned: "Latest State" active or "Awaiting query" idle); body is either the empty state (centered card with a magnifier line-icon badge, "No recovery state loaded yet." heading, hint) OR a grid of read-only state cards (label eyebrow + mono value) followed by a "Latest State" raw JSON <pre> payload card on the inset #f7f8fa surface; a green shield "Recovery guardrails" risk note sits at the bottom of this panel. RIGHT side rail = "Prepare Recovery" NeoCard (visibly dimmed/locked until query succeeds) showing a "Draft Account" summary row, then New Owner + Recovery Expiry inputs, an "Advanced (verifier override, template id)" collapsible details, and the two link groups ("Recovery preview", "Recovery credential") each as Open + copy-icon + share-icon button rows; under the card sit the two secondary workspace-link buttons. Components: NeoCard (variant erobo), NeoInput (label + hint + placeholder), NeoButton (primary green / secondary / sm icon), pill status badge, collapsible details with chevron, mono code/payload block. Color identity = Neo Soft green: #16c784 decorative badges/icons, #0a8050 filled CTAs, soft #e4f8f0 chips; verifier diagnostic uses green match / red mismatch.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero with metric row all showing em-dash; Guardian Preflight card empty with Query State disabled and the 'Enter a valid account address...' hint; left panel shows the magnifier empty state 'No recovery state loaded yet.'; right Prepare Recovery card in LOCKED/dimmed state with the 'Read the guardian state first...' gate hint and all link buttons disabled.
- **Address entered / ready to query** — Valid account address typed in Preflight, hero Account metric reflects it, Query State button enabled (primary green), blocking hint gone.
- **Querying / loading** — Query State button in loading spinner state, inputs still visible, left panel still empty/awaiting; status pill still 'Awaiting query'.
- **State loaded (read result)** — Status pill flips to 'Latest State'; state card grid populated (Account ID, Verifier, Backup Owner, Recovery Escape, Recovery Timelock, Checked At, optional Escape Triggered / Network Default Verifier) plus the JSON 'Latest State' payload block; Prepare Recovery card now UNLOCKED.
- **Preparing recovery / advanced open** — New Owner + Recovery Expiry filled; Advanced disclosure expanded showing Verifier Hash Override with a green 'Override matches bound verifier' (or red mismatch) diagnostic badge and Recovery Template ID; link buttons becoming enabled as validation passes.
- **Links ready / handoff** — All six link buttons enabled; bottom link-preview strip shows generated Recovery Preview and Recovery Credential URLs with the 'Open this link in the AA identity workspace to sign... not executed here' note; Open Identity Workspace / Open AA Workspace footer buttons present.
- **Blocked / validation error** — Invalid or incomplete fields: inline blocked hints ('Complete a valid account, new owner, expiry window...'), expiry out of 5-1440 range, link buttons disabled.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default green brand accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 filled CTAs, #e4f8f0 soft chips). Signature visual = a green shield-with-checkmark icon, used in the hero badge and repeated in the "Recovery guardrails" risk note, reinforcing the security/guardian theme. Secondary identity cues: a magnifier line-icon for the empty "no state loaded" state, a numbered 01/02/03 flow strip, an idle-grey vs active-green status pill, and green/red verifier match-vs-mismatch diagnostic badges. Monospace treatment for hashes/addresses and the JSON payload block.

# Oracle Consoles

## Automation Copilot  `automation-copilot`
*Category: oracle-console*

> A focused console for building, registering, and operating price-triggered Morpheus automation triggers from live on-chain oracle data.

**Purpose.** Automation Copilot lets a user define a "recipe" — when asset X crosses target price Y on this schedule, run action Z — and register it as a keeper-executed automation trigger driven by the Morpheus on-chain datafeed. A user opens it to set up hands-off, condition-based actions (e.g. auto-repay a self-loan, rebalance a vault, claim rewards) and to manage/enable/disable/delete the triggers they have registered. It deliberately separates the fast pricefeed read from the slower keeper execution, and is honest when the automation gateway is unavailable: such recipes are saved as draft "handoff intents" that will NOT run until re-registered.

**Functional description.** Primary flow (Recipe Builder, left column): (1) pick Asset from a select — NEO / GAS / BTC (default NEO); (2) enter Target Price as a number (default "20", validated > 0, e.g. 20 or 20.5); (3) set Schedule as a 5-field cron string (default "0 */6 * * *") via free-text input plus three preset pills — Hourly ("0 * * * *"), Every 6h ("0 */6 * * *", active by default), Daily ("0 0 * * *"); (4) choose Action Name from a select with presets Repay self-loan / Rebalance vault / Claim rewards / Notify webhook, plus "Custom action…" which reveals a "Custom Action Name" free-text field (placeholder auto_repay_self_loan). Primary CTA "Register Trigger" (filled green, check-circle icon) submits the form through the host automation gateway. Two secondary pill buttons: "Fetch Price" (refresh icon — pulls the live on-chain price into the hero + Current Price stat) and "Build Recipe" (list-checks icon — assembles the payload JSON without registering). Inline validation errors render in a red alert above the actions. Below the buttons: an info hint that gateway fallbacks are labeled handoff intents (not successful triggers), and a green-soft "How execution works" cost-model note.
Status flow (Trigger Status, right column): a "Refresh" ghost button reloads registered triggers. Empty state shows a power-icon and "Register or refresh a trigger before changing status." plus a 3-step numbered Automation route: 1 Read pricefeed, 2 Register trigger, 3 Enable or disable. Once a trigger exists, a Trigger Card shows Latest Trigger (shortened id like 0x03013f49…517fccab) and Next Execution. Compact actions: "Enable Trigger"/"Disable Trigger" (power icon, only enabled when the gateway returned a verified trigger) and "Copy Payload". If the trigger is a draft handoff-only intent, a warning block "Automation executor unavailable" explains it will NOT run on schedule, Next Execution reads "Not scheduled (draft)", and toggle stays disabled. A "Manage Triggers" list (when >1) shows each trigger as a row: name/short-id, an Enabled/Disabled state chip, an "Active" marker on the selected one, a tap-to-select main button, and a trash Delete button.
Bottom: a collapsible "Payload" details section (open by default) with a config strip — Network (mainnet), Datafeed Hash (0x…517fccab), Oracle Hash (0x…0db2d5ef) — and pretty-printed JSON blocks for the rendered payload and trigger request (or an empty-state line "Fetch a price, build a recipe, or register a trigger to inspect the payload.").

**Interface description.** Three stacked regions on a light canvas. (1) Hero band: white rounded card; left side has a round mint icon chip (#e4f8f0 fill, green bot/cpu glyph) next to an eyebrow "AUTOMATION ROUTE" (11px/700 uppercase, muted, .12em), title "Automation Copilot" (~24px/800, ink #1e1e2e, -0.02em) and subtitle "Create and operate price-triggered Morpheus automation triggers from one focused console." Right side has two boxed fact tiles: "CURRENT PRICE" (em-dash placeholder "—" until fetched, with caption "On-chain feed — verify freshness…") and "TRIGGER STATUS" with a colored status dot + label ("Ready" neutral / "Enabled" green / "Disabled" muted). (2) Two-column workspace, each a white rounded card with soft shadow. Left "Recipe Builder" panel: stacked labeled fields (Asset select, Target Price number input, Schedule input + three preset pills, Action Name select), then full-width green "Register Trigger" CTA, a two-up row of secondary outline pills (Fetch Price / Build Recipe), then two hint boxes (neutral inset + green-soft note). Right "Trigger Status" panel with header title + optional count chip + ghost "Refresh"; body is either the empty power-icon state with the numbered Automation route, or the trigger card + compact action buttons + (if applicable) Manage Triggers row list. (3) Full-width "Payload" collapsible footer card with a 3-cell config grid (NETWORK / DATAFEED HASH / ORACLE HASH, monospace short hashes) and JSON code blocks. Color identity: green-only accent system — brand #16c784 (icon chips, soft fills #e4f8f0, preset-active state), #0a8050 filled CTA white text, #0b5e3e deep green text on soft fills; status tones map green=ok/enabled, muted gray=off/neutral; alerts use red-soft (errors) and amber #fff3dd/#b9791a (executor-unavailable warning). Field surfaces are #f7f8fa insets with hairline borders; pills are fully rounded; cards radius 12–20px.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero with Current Price as '—' and Trigger Status 'Ready'. Recipe Builder pre-filled with defaults (NEO, target 20, Every 6h pill active, schedule 0 */6 * * *, Repay self-loan). Right panel shows the empty power-icon state 'Register or refresh a trigger before changing status.' with the 3-step numbered Automation route. Payload footer shows config strip with hashes but JSON empty-state line.
- **Recipe ready / price fetched** — User has fetched a live price: hero Current Price shows a value with the freshness caption; Build Recipe was tapped so the Payload section shows pretty-printed JSON blocks. Form fields edited (e.g. target price changed). Custom Action variant: Action Name = 'Custom action…' revealing the 'Custom Action Name' text input.
- **Submitting / loading** — Register Trigger (or Fetch Price / Build Recipe / Refresh) in loading state with spinner and disabled controls. Show the in-flight button states for each of the four async actions.
- **Verified trigger active** — Right panel shows the Trigger Card (Latest Trigger short-id, Next Execution time), enabled Enable/Disable + Copy Payload buttons, hero Trigger Status badge green 'Enabled'. With multiple triggers, the Manage Triggers list shows rows with Enabled/Disabled chips, an 'Active' marker, and trash Delete buttons.
- **Draft handoff (executor unavailable)** — Gateway fallback: amber warning block 'Automation executor unavailable' explaining the recipe will NOT run on schedule, Next Execution reads 'Not scheduled (draft)', Enable/Disable disabled, plus the 'verify before operate' note. Hero status stays neutral.
- **Error** — Validation or request failure: red-soft alert above the action buttons (e.g. 'Enter a target price greater than 0…', 'Enter a valid 5-field cron schedule…', or 'Failed to register trigger').

**Visual identity.** Green default Neo Soft theme used as a serious automation/console tool — single green accent hue (brand #16c784, deep-green text #0b5e3e, filled CTA #0a8050) with no secondary theme. Signature visual cues: a mint round bot/cpu icon chip in the hero, a colored status dot (the trigger on/off "power" semantics), a power-symbol empty-state, the three preset schedule pills (Hourly / Every 6h / Daily), and the numbered 1-2-3 Automation route (Read pricefeed → Register trigger → Enable or disable). Honesty/trust accents: amber warning for draft handoff intents and monospace short hashes (network, datafeed, oracle) reinforcing the on-chain oracle provenance.

## Oracle Compute Lab  `oracle-compute-lab`
*Category: oracle-console*

> Shape a Morpheus oracle compute job into a deterministic, copyable request preview — with sealed inputs kept out of the visible payload.

**Purpose.** (see above)

**Functional description.** No wallet required (walletRequired: false). The whole surface is one tab ("Compute"). Flow: (1) The user fills a 3-field form in the left column — Workflow select (options: "Risk score" [default], "Proof check", "Batch transform"); Privacy select (options: "Sealed" [default], "Public"); Input Payload textarea (defaults to {"asset":"GAS","window":"24h"}, placeholder {"asset":"GAS"}). (2) User clicks the primary "Build Preview" CTA (play-triangle icon). This is a LOCAL deterministic build — no network call. (3) On build, the right column swaps from its empty state to a result card showing: a status pill, a one-line summary, a 2-column grid of 5 rows, and a collapsible "Payload" disclosure with the raw JSON. Secondary actions: "Reset" (ghost, rotate-ccw icon) clears the form back to defaults and returns the result column to empty + zeroes the Requests counter; once a result exists, a "Copy" button (in the result header) copies the full payload JSON to clipboard (toast "Copied"). There is NO "Send live request" button here (no execute hook) — instead a persistent info notice reads "Preview only — dispatching requires a Morpheus runtime token." VALIDATION/RESULT LOGIC a designer must reflect: the lab JSON-parses the input. If VALID → status pill "Compute preview ready", summary "{Workflow} {Privacy} preview prepared" (e.g. "Risk score Sealed preview prepared"), the hero "Requests" counter increments by 1, and the hero "Digest" stat updates to the computed digest (a short 0x… hash). Rows shown: Workflow = the picked label; Privacy = Sealed/Public; "Input is valid JSON" = Yes; "Input Digest" = 0x… (fnv hash of the input); "Digest" = 0x… (hash of workflow|privacy|input). If INVALID JSON → status pill "Enter a valid JSON input payload" (treated as a WARNING, not success): no green toast, Requests counter does NOT increment, the hero Digest keeps its placeholder, and the "Input is valid JSON" row reads "No". PRIVACY effect on the payload JSON: Sealed mode redacts the raw input — payload contains inputPreview:"[sealed input redacted]", inputVisibility:"redacted", sealedInputRequired:true (the raw input string is NOT in the package); Public mode includes the literal input plus inputVisibility:"public". Both always include kind:"oracle.compute.request", workflow, privacy, inputValid, inputDigest, inputLength, digest, execution:"preview_only", dispatchReady:false.

**Interface description.** Light theme, single centered column max-width ~1080px (the rendered shot shows it left-of-center within a ~1365px frame — designer should center it). Three stacked bands on a #f7f8fb page:
1) HERO CARD (white, radius ~20px, soft shadow, 1px hairline border) split into two columns. Left: a 48px rounded-circle icon badge (currently a shield-check glyph) tinted in the app accent over a soft accent wash, then stacked copy — eyebrow "MORPHEUS COMPUTE" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em tracking, muted), title "Compute Preview Builder" (~22-24px/800, -0.02em), and a 2-line description "Shape compute work into a deterministic preview. Sealed mode keeps raw input out of visible payloads." Right: a vertically-centered meta stack of uppercase eyebrow-label + bold value pairs — "NETWORK Morpheus Mainnet", "MODE Compute preview builder (not dispatched)", "REQUESTS 0". (A "DIGEST" stat is defined and surfaces once a preview runs; placeholder "—".)
2) NOTICE BAR (full-width white pill card, hairline border, faint shadow): an info (i) icon in accent + text "Preview only — dispatching requires a Morpheus runtime token."
3) WORKSPACE: 2-column grid (~0.95fr / 1.05fr). LEFT = FORM CARD (white, radius ~20px) with stacked labeled controls: "Workflow" select (shows "Risk score", custom chevron), "Privacy" select (shows "Sealed"), "Input Payload" multiline textarea (shows {"asset":"GAS","window":"24h"}), then an action row: filled primary "Build Preview" button (play icon) + ghost "Reset" button (rotate icon). RIGHT = RESULT CARD (same card style). EMPTY STATE (default): vertically centered — 48px accent-tinted circular file-search icon, a caption-style uppercase deep-accent label "RUN THE FORM TO PREVIEW THE REQUEST PAYLOAD." and a muted hint "The decoded request and its digest will appear here so you can review it before dispatch." FILLED STATE: header row with a soft accent status pill ("Compute preview ready") + bold summary line, and a small "Copy" button top-right; then a 2-up grid of 5 inset stat tiles (each: uppercase muted micro-label + bold value) for Workflow / Privacy / Input is valid JSON / Input Digest / Digest; then a collapsible "PAYLOAD ⌄" disclosure revealing a monospace JSON <pre> block on a #f7f8fa inset.
IDENTITY NOTE: the manifest accentColor is CYAN #22d3ee (the badge/pill/empty-icon wash + focus ring render cyan, not the platform default green) — see the cyan shield badge and cyan info icon in the screenshot. Field labels are 13px/600 body ink; eyebrows and tile micro-labels follow the 11px/700 uppercase .12em system. Selects/inputs are #fff with a stronger hairline, radius ~10-12px, 44px min height, accent focus ring.
Mobile (≤860px / 390px): hero and workspace collapse to one column, action buttons go full-width and stack, result tiles become a single column.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Default load: hero (eyebrow MORPHEUS COMPUTE, title 'Compute Preview Builder', desc) + meta NETWORK Morpheus Mainnet / MODE 'Compute preview builder (not dispatched)' / REQUESTS 0; the 'Preview only — dispatching requires a Morpheus runtime token.' notice bar; left form pre-filled with Risk score / Sealed / {"asset":"GAS","window":"24h"}; right column empty state (cyan file-search icon, 'RUN THE FORM TO PREVIEW THE REQUEST PAYLOAD.' + hint). This is the canonical screen — matches the provided screenshot.
- **Filled / success result (Sealed)** — After Build Preview with valid JSON in Sealed mode: REQUESTS increments to 1, hero Digest stat shows a 0x… value; result card shows status pill 'Compute preview ready', summary 'Risk score Sealed preview prepared', Copy button, 5 stat tiles (Workflow=Risk score, Privacy=Sealed, Input is valid JSON=Yes, Input Digest=0x…, Digest=0x…), and an expanded Payload JSON showing inputPreview:'[sealed input redacted]', inputVisibility:'redacted', dispatchReady:false.
- **Filled / success result (Public)** — Same as success but Privacy=Public: summary '… Public preview prepared', Privacy tile reads Public, and the Payload JSON includes the literal raw input plus inputVisibility:'public' (no redaction) — useful to show the privacy-mode contrast.
- **Invalid JSON / warning** — Build Preview with malformed input: amber/warning treatment — status pill 'Enter a valid JSON input payload', no success toast, REQUESTS stays 0, hero Digest keeps the '—' placeholder, and the 'Input is valid JSON' row reads 'No'. Designer should mock a warning-toned status pill distinct from the success pill.
- **Copied confirmation** — After pressing Copy on a result: transient 'Copied' toast/feedback while the full payload JSON is on the clipboard (the button label/state briefly confirms).

**Visual identity.** App-specific CYAN accent (#22d3ee), an intentional secondary theme vs the platform default green — used for the hero icon badge wash, the result status pill, the empty-state file-search icon, select/input focus rings, and the info-notice icon; deep-accent variant for the uppercase 'waiting' caption. Signature visual cues: the shield-check hero badge (trust/seal), the file-search 'magnifying-glass-on-document' empty-state icon (inspect-before-dispatch), and the redaction motif — Sealed mode literally replaces the input with '[sealed input redacted]' in the payload, which is the app's core concept and should be made visually legible (e.g. a lock/redaction treatment on the payload preview). Otherwise standard Neo Soft light theme: white cards, #f7f8fb page, hairline borders, soft shadows, pill buttons, tabular-nums digests.

## Oracle HTTP Console  `oracle-http-console`
*Category: oracle-console*

> A focused request builder that shapes an HTTP oracle read — method, URL, JSON path, body — and computes its local digest before it's bound to an on-chain Morpheus callback.

**Purpose.** Oracle HTTP Console is a developer/operator tool for preparing a web-data read that a Morpheus oracle will later fetch on-chain. A user opens it to compose an HTTP request (GET/POST against a URL, with a JSONPath extraction selector and optional POST body), validate it locally, and get a deterministic request digest they can review and audit before binding it to a smart-contract callback. It is explicitly a PREVIEW BUILDER: it never calls the URL itself — it only computes the digest locally; the actual fetch runs on the Morpheus lane after the request is bound. This honesty is the product's whole point: teams compare the intended request against the submitted one via the matching digest.

**Functional description.** No wallet connection is required (walletRequired: false; a chain-mismatch warning may still appear). Single flow in one "HTTP" tab. The user fills a 4-field form in the left command column: (1) Method — a select with two options GET / POST (default GET); (2) URL — text input, seeded with the live default "https://oracle.meshmini.app/mainnet/health"; (3) JSON Path — text input, seeded "$.status", placeholder "$.status"; (4) Body — a textarea, placeholder "Optional POST body" (only meaningful/folded into the digest when Method = POST; a GET body is ignored). The primary action "Preview Request" (green filled, play icon) computes the result locally: it URL-validates the http(s) scheme, syntactically validates the JSONPath (must start with $, balanced brackets, no trailing/empty dot segments), then derives an 8-hex digest like 0x1a2b3c4d. On valid input the result column fills with status "HTTP request ready", a summary "{METHOD} oracle request prepared", and a stacked rows table: Method, URL, URL valid (Yes/No), JSON Path, Path valid (Yes/No), Digest. On invalid input it shows a warning status instead ("Enter a valid http(s) URL" or "Enter a valid JSON path (start with $)"), the URL valid / Path valid rows read "No", NO success toast fires, the Requests counter does NOT increment, and the Digest stat keeps its "—" placeholder. A "Copy" button (in the result head) copies the full JSON payload; an expandable "Payload" disclosure (<details>) reveals the raw payload JSON (kind: "oracle.http.request" with method, url, urlValid, jsonPath, pathValid, body, digest). Secondary action "Reset" (ghost, rotate icon) clears the result, restores the seeded field defaults, resets Last Status/Digest placeholders and the Requests tally to 0. There is NO live "Send" button here (no execute hook wired) — hence the standing preview-only notice. Each successful preview increments the hero "Requests" stat and updates the Digest/Last Status hero+sidebar stats.

**Interface description.** Rendered by the shared ConsoleToolPanel; centered single-column stack on a light page, ~1200px max content width, generous 20px gaps. TOP: a hero card (white #fff surface, radius-lg ~20px, soft shadow, no border, 24px padding) laid out as two columns. Left: a 46px rounded-square brand-green icon badge (soft #e4f8f0 fill, ShieldCheck glyph in #16c784) next to an intro block — eyebrow "HTTP DATA FEED" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em tracking, muted), title "HTTP Oracle Request" (~24-28px/800, ink #1e1e2e, -0.02em), and a 3-line body paragraph (#5b6478) explaining it is a preview builder that computes the digest locally and does not call the URL. Right: a hero-meta stat list, label-then-bold-value pairs stacked: "NETWORK Morpheus Mainnet", "MODE Request builder (preview)", "REQUESTS 0" (figure bold/tabular). BELOW HERO: a full-width thin info notice card (white pill-ish bar, Info icon in green) reading "Preview only — dispatching requires a Morpheus runtime token." WORKSPACE: a 2-column grid (left ~1.1fr min, right ~0.9fr min 320px), 20px gap, collapsing to single column at 860px. LEFT card (form, white, radius-lg, 24px pad, 16px gaps): labeled fields top-to-bottom — "Method" select (rounded #f7f8fa-ish field, chevron, value GET), "URL" text input (shows the meshmini health URL), "JSON Path" text input ("$.status"), "Body" textarea (placeholder "Optional POST body"); then an action row: filled green "Preview Request" pill button (play icon) + ghost "Reset" (rotate icon). RIGHT card (result/empty, white, radius-lg, 24px pad): in the EMPTY state shows a centered column — a soft mint-circle icon badge (FileSearch glyph), a small green pill heading "Run the form to preview the request payload.", and a muted hint "The decoded request and its digest will appear here so you can review it before dispatch." In the FILLED state it shows a result head (status badge chip + bold summary, plus a small "Copy" button), a single-column stacked rows table (each row a #f7f8fa inset, radius-md, label left muted + bold value right), and a collapsible "Payload" disclosure with a monospace <pre> JSON block. Identity color is brand green #16c784 / #0a8050; sidebar (host chrome) mirrors Network / Last Status / Digest.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Form pre-seeded (Method GET, URL https://oracle.meshmini.app/mainnet/health, JSON Path $.status, empty Body); preview-only notice visible; Requests = 0, Digest = — ; right column shows the empty state: mint FileSearch icon, green pill 'Run the form to preview the request payload.' and the muted 'will appear here…before dispatch' hint.
- **Filled / ready to preview** — User has edited URL/path/method (e.g. POST with a body); form fully populated, 'Preview Request' button emphasized; right column still empty awaiting the preview run.
- **Success / preview result** — Valid input → result card: green status badge 'HTTP request ready' + summary 'GET oracle request prepared'; stacked rows Method / URL / URL valid: Yes / JSON Path / Path valid: Yes / Digest 0x________; Copy button + expandable Payload JSON; hero Requests increments to 1 and Digest/Last Status stats update.
- **Validation warning** — Bad URL or JSONPath → warning status ('Enter a valid http(s) URL' or 'Enter a valid JSON path (start with $)'); rows show 'URL valid: No' / 'Path valid: No'; no success toast, Requests stays 0, Digest stays '—'. Mock the warning-colored status chip.
- **Payload expanded** — The 'Payload' <details> opened, revealing the monospace JSON ({ kind: 'oracle.http.request', method, url, urlValid, jsonPath, pathValid, body, digest }) for copy/audit.
- **Mobile 390px** — Single-column stack: hero (icon+intro then stat list), notice bar, form card, then result/empty card; pill buttons full-width-ish, fields stacked.

**Visual identity.** Default Neo Soft light theme with brand-green accent (#16c784 decorative for the hero ShieldCheck badge, the mint empty-state FileSearch icon, the green pill heading and info icon; #0a8050 filled green for the 'Preview Request' CTA with white text; #e4f8f0 soft mint fills). No secondary theme. Signature visual is the request-builder identity: the ShieldCheck oracle badge, the local request DIGEST (monospace 0x________ hex) as the auditable trust artifact, and the Yes/No URL-valid / Path-valid validation rows. Tabular-nums for the Requests figure and digest.

## Oracle NeoDID Console  `oracle-neodid-console`
*Category: oracle-console*

> A preview builder that assembles and locally fingerprints a NeoDID identity-verification request so you can review it before dispatching to the Morpheus oracle.

**Purpose.** Oracle NeoDID Console lets a developer prepare a reviewable NeoDID (decentralized identity) verification package before it is dispatched to the Morpheus oracle. It does NOT verify identity or call the verifier — it only computes a local digest of the assembled request so mismatched DID/claim/provider/callback combinations are caught while they're still readable. A user opens it to draft a `oracle.neodid.verify` request, confirm its fields and 0x digest, copy the JSON payload, and hand it off to a real dispatch path that requires a Morpheus runtime token.

**Functional description.** No wallet connection is required (walletRequired: false; a chain-warning banner may show). The single flow: (1) The user fills the left "command" form — DID text field (defaults to `did:neo:testnet:sample-user`), Provider dropdown (3 options: "NeoDID registry" / "Wallet signature" / "Social attestation"; default registry), Claim text field (defaults to `profile.kyc`), and an optional Callback Contract field (placeholder "Optional callback contract hash"). (2) Click the primary CTA "Preview Verification" (play-triangle icon). This runs `buildResult` purely client-side: it validates the DID against `^did:neo:...$` and, if present, the callback against a 0x hash160 (40 hex), then computes an FNV-1a digest like `0x1a2b3c4d` over `did|provider|claim|callback`. (3) The right panel swaps from its empty state to a result: a status pill ("Verification preview ready", or warnings "Enter a valid did:neo identifier" / "Callback must be a 0x hash160" / "Required fields missing"), a bold summary ("Claim 'profile.kyc' prepared for review"), a key/value rows table (DID, "DID format valid": Yes/No, Provider, Claim, optionally "Callback hash valid": Yes/No, Digest), and a collapsible "Payload" disclosure containing the pretty-printed JSON (`kind: "oracle.neodid.verify"`, did, provider, claim, callback, digest, `execution: "preview_only"`, `dispatchReady: false`). (4) Secondary actions: a "Copy" button (top-right of result) copies the JSON and flips to "Copied"; "Reset" (counter-clockwise icon) clears the form back to defaults, blanks the result, and resets the Requests counter to 0. Each successful preview increments the hero "Requests" stat and updates the "Digest" / "Last Status" stats. There is NO live-dispatch button here (no execute hook configured) — hence the persistent preview-only notice.

**Interface description.** Single-column stack on a light canvas, ~1200px content width, three stacked blocks. (1) HERO CARD (white, rounded ~20px, soft shadow): left side a circular violet-tinted badge with a shield-check icon, then eyebrow "IDENTITY ORACLE" (11px/700 uppercase, tracked, muted), title "NeoDID Verification Preview" (~26px/800 ink), and a multi-line description paragraph explaining it's a preview builder that computes a local digest and does not verify identity, advising to confirm provider id/claim against the live catalog (GET /neodid/providers). Right side a vertical meta list of label+value pairs: "NETWORK Morpheus Mainnet", "MODE Verification preview builder", "REQUESTS 0" (labels are small uppercase eyebrows, values bold). (2) NOTICE STRIP (full-width white pill row, info icon): "Preview only — dispatching requires a Morpheus runtime token." (3) WORKSPACE: two equal columns. LEFT FORM CARD: stacked labeled fields — "DID" text input (value did:neo:testnet:sample-user), "Provider (example — verify against live catalog)" select showing "NeoDID registry" with chevron, "Claim" text input (profile.kyc), "Callback Contract" input (empty, grey placeholder). Action row at bottom: filled green "Preview Verification" button (play icon) + ghost "Reset" button (rotate icon). RIGHT RESULT CARD: in the captured state it's the EMPTY state — centered file-search line icon in a soft circle, a green-text pill "Run the form to preview the request payload.", and a muted two-line hint "The decoded request and its digest will appear here so you can review it before dispatch." Cards are white with hairline borders (rgba(20,22,38,0.06)), ~16px radius, generous padding. NOTE the manifest declares a violet accent (#a78bfa) for chrome (badge tint, status pill, focus ring), but the rendered primary CTA is the platform green (#0a8050) — the design should reconcile to the Neo Soft green system with violet reserved only as the identity-oracle signature tint.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty result** — Form pre-filled with defaults (DID did:neo:testnet:sample-user, Provider NeoDID registry, Claim profile.kyc, empty Callback); right panel shows the empty state — file-search icon, green pill 'Run the form to preview the request payload.' and the muted hint. Hero Requests = 0, Digest = '—'. This is the captured screenshot state.
- **Filled / ready (success result)** — After Preview Verification with valid inputs: right panel shows status badge 'Verification preview ready', summary 'Claim 'profile.kyc' prepared for review', rows table (DID, DID format valid: Yes, Provider, Claim, Digest 0x........) and a collapsed 'Payload' disclosure with a Copy button. Hero Requests increments to 1, Digest stat shows the hash.
- **Payload expanded + copied** — The 'Payload' disclosure opened revealing pretty-printed JSON (kind oracle.neodid.verify, execution preview_only, dispatchReady false); Copy button has flipped to its 'Copied' confirmation label.
- **Validation warning** — Malformed input state: an amber/warning status pill instead of green — e.g. 'Enter a valid did:neo identifier' (bad DID) or 'Callback must be a 0x hash160' (bad callback) or 'Required fields missing' for empty DID/claim; validity rows read 'No'; no Requests increment, no success toast.
- **Reset to defaults** — After Reset: form returns to default values, result panel reverts to the empty state, Requests counter back to 0, Digest/Last Status restored to initial placeholders — visually identical to first-run.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the green brand system as the primary accent (filled CTA #0a8050 white text, soft chips #e4f8f0, green text #0b5e3e). Signature visual for THIS app = the "identity oracle" motif: a circular shield-check badge in a soft violet wash (#a78bfa @ ~12% as `--console-accent-soft`) marking it as the identity lane within the oracle console family. Secondary identity cue = the hexadecimal digest (e.g. 0x1a2b3c4d) and the validity Yes/No rows, reinforcing the "reviewable, fingerprinted request" concept. Empty/result state uses a file-search line icon mascot. Keep one accent hue dominant (green) with violet as a restrained per-app signal, not a second CTA color.

## Oracle Price Console  `oracle-price-console`
*Category: oracle-console*

> Pick an asset, read its price straight off the on-chain MorpheusDataFeed contract, and keep the entire request path inspectable — no private RPC, no off-chain proxy.

**Purpose.** read-only price oracle console

**Functional description.** No wallet needed (walletRequired: false) — the console is read-only. Primary flow: (1) The user lands with the request path already visible (Contract / Method / Quote) so the read is inspectable before any action. (2) In the right-hand "Query feed" panel, they pick an Asset from a dropdown — options come from availablePairs, seeded NEO / GAS / BTC and lazily extended from the contract's on-chain catalog (listPairs). Changing it dispatches updateAsset, which immediately updates the hero title (e.g. "GAS/USD"). (3) They press the filled green "Fetch Price" button (dispatches fetchPrice). The button shows a loading spinner and is disabled while in flight; the result column swaps to a quiet left-aligned "Loading…" card. (4) On success the result card shows the asset token chip + "NEO/USD" symbol + the price in large mono type (e.g. "$6.4210", 4 decimals), with a freshness pill: green "Fresh — updated 3m ago" when within the 1-hour threshold, amber "Stale feed — last update 4h ago" when older. A caption line shows the absolute on-chain feed time ("On-chain feed time: …"); a stale read also adds an amber note: "This feed has not updated within the last hour. Treat the price as historical, not live." (5) A read of 0/negative is treated as no price → the "Awaiting read" empty state stays. Secondary action: the hero "Feed" metric is a copy button — clicking copies the FULL feed contract hash to the clipboard (truncated label "0x03013f49…" → checkmark "Copied" cue for ~1.6s). Errors map to friendly banners under the button: feed not deployed, request timed out, no data for this pair, network error, or generic retry — never the raw FAULT text.

**Interface description.** Light "Neo Soft" console on a transparent page, ~1365px wide. HERO BAND: a rounded-xl card with a subtle white→lavender gradient (#ffffff → #f6f4ff) and soft shadow, split as a wide left copy column and a narrower right metrics column. Left: a 46px circular soft-green badge holding a green line-chart/pulse glyph, then eyebrow "LIVE ORACLE PRICE" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em, muted), a 32px/800 ink title that is the dynamic pair "NEO/USD" (tabular-nums, -0.02em), and a one-line subtitle "Choose an asset, read the on-chain feed, and keep the request path inspectable." Right: a stack (max ~340px, right-aligned) of three inset pill-rows on #f7f8fa with hairline inset border — "Feed | 0x03013f49…" (this row is a clickable copy button with a copy-icon cue, rounded-lg, hover lifts to white surface), "Network | mainnet", "Source | on-chain mainnet RPC", each label muted 12px/700 left, value ink 13px/800 right. WORKSPACE: a 2-column grid (~1.25fr / 0.75fr). LEFT result column holds: the result/empty card (same gradient + shadow as hero) — empty state is a quiet left-aligned row "Awaiting read" + hint "No price loaded yet. Pick an asset and fetch to read the on-chain feed.", loaded state is a 58px circular asset token chip (NEO = soft-green/#e4f8f0, GAS = pale-blue #dff7ff, BTC = peach #ffe5b8) + "NEO/USD" caption + big mono price + a right-aligned freshness pill (green soft for fresh, amber #fff3dd for stale). Below it sits the always-on "REQUEST PATH" reference card (white surface, inset hairline border) with eyebrow title and a definition list: "Contract | 0x03013f49…" (mono, full hash on hover), "Method | getLatest(pair)" (mono), "Quote | USD · 4 decimals". RIGHT column is the "Query feed" NeoCard (18px/700 title): a muted hint box "The request reads MorpheusDataFeed directly and never exposes a private RPC URL.", an "Asset" labeled select (NEO/GAS/BTC), and a full-width filled green "Fetch Price" CTA. Error banner (red-tint) appears under the CTA when present. Identity color is the green brand accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 filled CTA), with the lavender-tinted hero/result gradient as the signature surface.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / Awaiting read** — Default state on open. Hero shows NEO/USD with badge, eyebrow, subtitle, and the three metric pills (Feed/Network/Source). Left column shows the 'Awaiting read' empty card with hint 'No price loaded yet. Pick an asset and fetch to read the on-chain feed.' plus the always-visible REQUEST PATH card (Contract / Method getLatest(pair) / Quote USD · 4 decimals). Right Query feed panel shows hint, Asset = NEO, and the green Fetch Price CTA enabled. This matches the current screenshot.
- **Loading / fetching** — After pressing Fetch Price. CTA shows a spinner and is disabled; the left result slot becomes a quiet left-aligned 'Loading…' card (skeleton). Hero and request-path reference remain unchanged.
- **Success — fresh** — Result card shows the asset token chip (e.g. green NEO), 'NEO/USD' caption, large mono price '$6.4210', and a green freshness pill 'Fresh — updated 3m ago' with a green dot. A caption line below reads 'On-chain feed time: <timestamp>'. The Feed metric, when copied, briefly flips its copy icon to a checkmark ('Copied').
- **Success — stale** — Same loaded layout but the freshness pill is amber 'Stale feed — last update 4h ago' with an amber dot, plus an amber warning note card below: 'This feed has not updated within the last hour. Treat the price as historical, not live.' Demonstrates the honest-staleness identity.
- **Error** — Loaded/empty result card unchanged; a red-tint mono error banner appears under the Fetch Price CTA in the Query feed panel — variants: 'Price feed is not deployed on this network.', 'Feed request timed out. Please retry.', 'Feed has no data for this pair yet.', 'Network error. Check your connection and retry.', or 'Could not read the price feed. Please retry.'
- **Mobile (390px)** — Single column: hero collapses to stacked copy + full-width metric pills (radius-lg, 20px padding, title 28px), then the result/empty card (asset chip + price wrap, status pill full-width centered), the REQUEST PATH card, and finally the Query feed panel with Asset select + full-width Fetch Price CTA.

**Visual identity.** Green brand accent (Neo Soft default): #16c784 for the decorative hero badge, asset chip, and the "Fresh" freshness pill; #0a8050 filled "Fetch Price" CTA with white text. Signature visuals: a pulse/line-chart glyph in the circular soft-green hero badge; per-asset coin token chips (NEO soft-green, GAS pale-blue, BTC peach); a status pill with a colored dot that switches green→amber to honestly signal stale data; large JetBrains-Mono tabular price figures; a distinctive white→lavender (#f6f4ff) gradient on the hero and result cards. Trust/inspectability cues (truncated hash with copy affordance, mono request-path rows) are part of the identity.
