## Neo Sign Anything  `neo-sign-anything`
*Category: tool*

> A wallet-reviewed proof desk that turns any message (or a file's SHA-256 digest) into a copyable off-chain signature, or anchors it on-chain as a 0 GAS self-transfer.

**Purpose.** Neo Sign Anything lets a user cryptographically prove authorship of a message using their Neo address — without ever exposing the private key. The user opens it to either generate a shareable off-chain signature as evidence, or publish a permanent public on-chain record by broadcasting a 0 GAS self-transfer with the message attached in the data field. It also hashes files locally (SHA-256) so a document can be signed by its digest without ever uploading the file.

**Functional description.** Single workflow, no separate connect step (the wallet prompt is the connect step). Flow: (1) Type a message into the "Message composer" textarea OR click "Hash & load file" to pick a file — the file is hashed locally to SHA-256 and the digest "sha256:<hex>" is loaded into the message box (file never leaves the device). A live byte counter shows "N/1024 bytes"; both Sign and Broadcast buttons are disabled when the message is empty or exceeds 1024 bytes. (2a) Click "Sign Message" (primary) → wallet opens for review → returns a signature (and public key when available); the "Signed" stat increments. (2b) Click "Broadcast Message (On-chain)" (secondary) → wallet opens → sends a 0 GAS GAS-token self-transfer with the message in the data field → returns a transaction hash; the "Posted" stat increments. Sign and broadcast results for the SAME message coexist side by side; changing the message clears both stale artifacts. (3) From the "Proof output" panel the user can "Copy signature", "Copy verify bundle" (address + message + signature + public key as one JSON block — everything a verifier needs), "Copy transaction hash", or "View on explorer". On-chain behaviour: broadcast is public and permanent, value is 0 GAS but normal Neo network + system fees still apply (~0.01–0.05 GAS, more for larger payloads); signing is free.

**Interface description.** Three-region layout on a light canvas, green brand accent (#16c784 / #0a8050). TOP HERO CARD (white, rounded): a green-tinted icon badge (pen/feather quill glyph in soft #e4f8f0 circle), eyebrow "WALLET-REVIEWED PROOF DESK" (11px uppercase, muted), title "Neo Signature Desk" (~28px/800), subtitle "Prepare a message, request a wallet signature, or publish a 0 GAS self-transfer with the message attached for public proof." On the hero's right, two stacked stat tiles "SIGNED 0" and "POSTED 0" (uppercase label + large tabular figure). Below the hero, a collapsible disclosure bar "Signature flow" (green dot + chevron) that expands into a 3-step strip: 01 Write message / 02 Wallet review / 03 Copy proof, each with a one-line description. MAIN WORKSPACE = two columns. Left card "Message composer" with right-aligned byte counter "0/1024 bytes"; label "Message"; multi-line textarea (placeholder "Enter your message here..."); a file row with a ghost "Hash & load file" button + the SHA-256 note; an action grid of two buttons — filled-green-text soft "Sign Message" (primary) and outlined "Broadcast Message (On-chain)" (secondary); plus a green-dot wallet note "The wallet prompt opens when you sign or broadcast; no separate connect step is required here." Right card "Proof output" with status word top-right ("Waiting" → "Ready"). When empty it shows a centered shield-check line icon + hint "Sign or broadcast a message to see its proof here." Below sit two result boxes: "Signature" (value or "No signature yet", optional public-key meta, buttons "Copy signature" + "Copy verify bundle") and "Transaction Hash" (value or "No broadcast yet", "Copy transaction hash" + "View on explorer" link). FAR-RIGHT ASIDE = "Transaction safety" card with a status pill top-right ("Disconnected" gray / "Connected" green), reassurance copy about not handling private keys, then a 5-row signal grid (label left, mono value right): Sign route = chain.signMessage, Broadcast route = GAS.transfer self -> data, Transfer amount = 0 GAS, Message bytes = 0/1024 bytes, Privacy = No secrets. Below, a collapsible "On-chain broadcast" disclosure (chevron) revealing the broadcast explainer + the network-fee note. All cards are rounded 16/20px with soft shadows and hairline borders.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty (disconnected)** — Wallet not connected. Safety pill reads gray 'Disconnected'. Message textarea empty with placeholder, counter '0/1024 bytes', both action buttons disabled, green-dot wallet note visible. Proof output shows 'Waiting' status, the centered shield-check empty illustration + 'Sign or broadcast a message to see its proof here', and the two result boxes in their empty state ('No signature yet' / 'No broadcast yet') with copy buttons disabled. Stats SIGNED 0 / POSTED 0.
- **Filled / ready** — User has typed a message (e.g. ~80 bytes). Counter shows live 'N/1024 bytes', both 'Sign Message' and 'Broadcast Message (On-chain)' enabled. Safety grid 'Message bytes' row mirrors the same count. Proof output still 'Waiting' with empty illustration. Optional variant: message field shows a loaded file digest 'sha256:…' after using 'Hash & load file'.
- **Signing / broadcasting (loading)** — Wallet review in progress: the pressed button shows a loading spinner and is disabled (sibling button stays disabled during the action). Useful to mock both 'Sign Message' loading and 'Broadcast Message' loading. During broadcast the Transaction Hash field may show the pending state 'Transaction sent (ID pending)'.
- **Signature success** — Off-chain signature returned. Proof output status flips to 'Ready'; the empty illustration is gone. 'Signature' box shows a truncated hex (10 chars…6 chars), optional 'Public key: …' meta, enabled 'Copy signature' + 'Copy verify bundle' buttons, and the verify-bundle hint line. SIGNED stat increments to 1. Safety pill 'Connected' green.
- **Broadcast success** — On-chain anchor done. 'Transaction Hash' box shows a truncated tx hash with enabled 'Copy transaction hash' and a 'View on explorer' link. POSTED stat increments. Sign + broadcast boxes can both be populated simultaneously for the same message. Show a transient 'Copied' toast/feedback when a copy button is used.
- **Error** — Signing failed / broadcast failed (wallet rejected, message too long >1024 bytes, or network error). Show an inline error treatment — e.g. 'Message is too long for on-chain broadcast.' near the composer, or a 'Signing failed' / 'Broadcast failed' banner — while results remain in their prior state and buttons re-enable.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft default green identity (no secondary theme). Bright #16c784 for the decorative hero icon badge, soft #e4f8f0 chips/fills, #0a8050/#0b5e3e for green text and CTAs. Signature visual = a quill/pen-nib feather icon in a soft-green circular badge (the app's signing motif), reinforced by a shield-check line icon for the empty proof state and the green status dot on the wallet note. Mono/tabular treatment for routes, byte counters, and hash/signature values gives it a precise, cryptographic-tool feel. Trust cues throughout (Disconnected/Connected pill, 'No secrets', 0 GAS, local-hash notice) signal a safety-first utility rather than a flashy app.

## NeoDID Passport  `neodid-passport`
*Category: tool*

> An identity workbench that resolves a Morpheus NeoDID document, builds a portable credential payload, and optionally binds it to your wallet with an off-chain signature.

**Purpose.** NeoDID Passport is a developer/identity tool for turning a decentralized identifier (DID) into a portable, verifiable "passport" that one miniapp can hand to another or to a relying party. A user opens it to look up a Morpheus NeoDID document, package its key facts (subject, claim, audience, document metadata, digest) into a canonical JSON credential, and — when using the wallet provider — attach an off-chain wallet signature that proves control of an address. Nothing is broadcast on-chain; the output is a reviewable JSON credential plus an optional cryptographic proof.

**Functional description.** Single-tab "Passport" workflow with a fixed three-step route (1 Resolve DID -> 2 Build credential -> 3 Optional wallet proof). FLOW: (1) User fills the left "Morpheus NeoDID API" form: Subject DID text field (default `did:morpheus:neo_n3:service:neodid`, validated as a Morpheus DID — service/vault/aa forms); Proof Provider dropdown (Wallet signature [default] | GitHub attestation | Email attestation); Claim text field (default `wallet-ownership`); Audience text field (default `miniapp-neodid-passport`, optional). A contextual hint below the form changes by provider — wallet: "Wallet signatures do not broadcast a transaction. They bind the passport payload to the current wallet address."; github/email: "Email and GitHub providers prepare an unsigned attestation package for an external verifier." (2) Primary action "Resolve and Build" (green, with check icon) calls the live Morpheus NeoDID resolver, then assembles the credential and computes a SHA-256 payload digest. The right column flips from the empty state to a populated result: a head row ("Passport payload ready" + the resolved document id + a Copy Payload button), a 2x2 summary grid (Version, Services count, Payload Digest [shortened hash], Signature [shortened hash or "Not signed"]), and a scrollable pretty-printed JSON payload block. (3) Secondary action "Sign Passport" (with signature icon) is enabled only when a payload exists AND provider is Wallet; it requests an off-chain wallet signature, sets proof status to "Signed", and stamps the Signature field. The form-head status chip moves from "Not signed" (grey dot) to "Signed" (green dot + green soft fill). (4) "Reset" (ghost, rotate icon) restores default form values and clears the result. EDGE BEHAVIOURS designers must reflect: when provider is GitHub or Email an "Submit to an external verifier" note card appears with body copy and an "Open attestation docs" outbound link (external-link icon) — these never complete in-app; if the resolved document's versionId is "unversioned" a "Degraded" amber chip appears next to the document id plus an amber warning note ("Runtime attestation metadata unavailable — this passport lacks the TEE verification key a relying party may require."); validation/resolver failures render a red inline alert above the actions (e.g. "Enter a valid Morpheus NeoDID.", "NeoDID resolver did not return a usable document.", "Resolver unavailable in this standalone preview…"). The Copy Payload button copies the canonical JSON and shows "Copied". Hero metrics read live: Network = "Morpheus Testnet", Resolver = "NeoDID resolver", Passports = count (starts 0).

**Interface description.** Light "Neo Soft" console on white surfaces, vertical stack of four bands. HERO BAND (rounded white card, soft mint→transparent diagonal wash top-left, soft shadow): left side has a 48px circular soft-green icon badge holding a Fingerprint glyph, then an eyebrow "IDENTITY ROUTE" (11px/700 uppercase, green), title "NeoDID Passport Builder" (24px/800, -0.02em ink), a one-line description ("Resolve a Morpheus NeoDID document, build a portable credential payload, and optionally bind it to the connected wallet signature."), and a green soft-fill pill chip "Builds a portable credential and an off-chain wallet proof — nothing is broadcast on-chain."; right side is a 3-up metrics cluster on subtle-grey inset tiles: NETWORK / "Morpheus Testnet", RESOLVER / "NeoDID resolver", PASSPORTS / "0" (uppercase 11px labels, 14px bold values). ROUTE STRIP: three equal white cards with soft shadow, each a numbered circular badge (1/2/3, green soft fill) + line icon + label — "Resolve DID" (shield-check), "Build credential" (file-check), "Optional wallet proof" (signature). WORKSPACE: 2-column grid (~0.9fr form / ~1.1fr result, 24px gap). LEFT FORM CARD (white, radius-lg, shadow): form head row with eyebrow "MORPHEUS NEODID API" on the left and a status pill on the right ("Not signed" with grey dot / "Signed" with green dot + green fill); then stacked fields — Subject DID input, Proof Provider custom select (chevron affordance), Claim input, Audience input; a bordered subtle-grey hint box; then the action row of three pill buttons: "Resolve and Build" (filled green primary, lg), "Sign Passport" (secondary, lg, disabled until ready), "Reset" (ghost, lg). RIGHT RESULT CARD (white, radius-lg, shadow): in empty state a centered inset-grey panel with a faint file-check line icon, heading "Awaiting DID resolution" (15px/700 ink) and muted hint "Resolve a DID to inspect the document, digest, and proof package before handing it to another miniapp."; in filled state the result head + Copy Payload button, 2x2 summary tiles (subtle-grey insets, uppercase micro labels + tabular-nums values), and a monospace JSON payload block (JetBrains Mono 12px, subtle-grey, scroll, ~320–520px). Accent identity is a teal-leaning green (config accent #0ea5a3) reconciled to the standard Neo green token set in CSS (brand #16c784, deep green text #0b7d54, soft fill mint); secondary status colors are amber (#fffbeb / #92400e degraded) and red soft (danger alert). Borders are hairline; radii 12/16/20; soft shadows throughout.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Hero + route strip + workspace with the form pre-filled with default values (Subject DID `did:morpheus:neo_n3:service:neodid`, provider Wallet signature, claim `wallet-ownership`, audience `miniapp-neodid-passport`), wallet hint shown, status pill 'Not signed' (grey dot). Right column shows the centered 'Awaiting DID resolution' empty panel with file-check icon. Passports stat = 0.
- **Resolving / submitting** — After 'Resolve and Build' is pressed: the primary button shows its loading state; right column still on empty panel until the resolver returns. (Sign uses the same loading treatment on the secondary button.)
- **Built / ready (unsigned)** — Resolver succeeded: right column populated — head 'Passport payload ready' + resolved document id + Copy Payload button; 2x2 summary (Version, Services, Payload Digest = short hash, Signature = 'Not signed'); JSON payload block visible. 'Sign Passport' now enabled (wallet provider). Status pill still 'Not signed'.
- **Signed (success)** — After 'Sign Passport': form-head status pill switches to 'Signed' (green dot, green soft fill); the Signature summary tile shows the shortened signature hash; the wallet proof is embedded in the JSON payload. Designer should show the green 'success' visual emphasis.
- **External-provider note** — Provider switched to GitHub or Email: hint text changes to the attestation copy and an 'Submit to an external verifier' note card appears (title + body + 'Open attestation docs' outbound link with external-link icon). 'Sign Passport' is disabled (non-wallet provider).
- **Degraded passport (warning)** — Built passport whose document versionId is 'unversioned': an amber 'Degraded' chip sits beside the document id and an amber warning note appears ('Runtime attestation metadata unavailable — this passport lacks the TEE verification key a relying party may require.').
- **Error / validation** — Invalid DID, missing claim, or resolver failure: a red inline alert appears above the action buttons (e.g. 'Enter a valid Morpheus NeoDID.', 'NeoDID resolver did not return a usable document.', 'Resolver unavailable in this standalone preview…'); right column stays on empty state.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the standard Neo green accent, but with a deliberate teal lean (config accentColor #0ea5a3) reconciled in CSS to the brand green tokens — bright #16c784 for the icon badge / numbered route chips / soft mint fills, deep #0b7d54 for green text (eyebrow, off-chain chip, route labels), and #0a8050-class green for the filled primary CTA. Signature visual motif is identity/credential: a Fingerprint glyph in the hero icon badge, a numbered 3-step "identity route" rail (shield-check -> file-check -> signature icons), a status dot pill that goes grey 'Not signed' -> green 'Signed', and a monospace JSON 'passport' payload as the centerpiece artifact. Secondary semantic colors: amber (#fffbeb/#fde68a/#92400e) for the degraded/runtime warning, red soft for validation/resolver errors.

## NFT Factory  `nft-factory`
*Category: tool*

> Configure a NEP-11 collection from a governed on-chain template, then sign or deploy it — no NEF files, just parameters.

**Purpose.** NFT Factory is a focused, OneGate-loadable console for launching NEP-11 collections (collections, passes, soulbound badges, creator drops) through the platform Factory contract by supplying only initialization parameters — never raw NEF/manifest files. The user opens it to spin up a creator collection without writing or uploading a contract: fill a form, get a deterministic wallet-signable plan, then either deploy in-app (where a factory admin has preloaded the template artifact for the chosen network) or sign and export the plan for an admin to execute. The honest promise: only the Neo network fee, no app fee; royalty is a creator setting baked into the collection, not a platform charge.

**Functional description.** Single user flow on the "Factory" tab. 1) Pick Network in the form header (select: "Neo N3 Testnet" / "Neo N3 Mainnet") — this drives the whole console's network context and the artifact/honesty checks. 2) Fill the NEP-11 collection form: Collection name (3–64 chars), a row of Symbol (2–12 uppercase, letter-first) + Max supply (1–1,000,000), Royalty bps (0–1000, with a live hint "{bps} bps = {percent}% of each secondary sale, routed to the collection owner by marketplaces. Fixed at deploy — it cannot be changed later."), Base URI (HTTPS URL ending in "/"), Owner (Neo N3 address; a "Use my address" affix button appears when a wallet is connected and the field differs), and a Transferable NFT toggle whose label warns "off = soulbound, non-transferable forever". 3) Press the full-width primary CTA "Generate template plan" — this validates inputs and produces a stored plan with a Plan ID, a SHA-256 Plan digest, and a template-artifact status. 4) In the right column the user reviews: a live Preview NFT card, the "Template deployment plan" card (Plan ID, full Plan digest, Template artifact status, optional "≈ {amount} GAS" estimated fee, Blocking errors list, Warnings list, a collapsible "View plan payload (JSON)"), then three actions — "Copy template plan" (copies JSON), "Sign template plan" (wallet-signs the digest; enabled only after a plan is generated and publishable), and "Deploy via factory" (submits template ID + params; enabled only when the artifact is preloaded on the selected network and the plan hasn't already been executed). 5) Results render inline: wallet signature code (copyable), Transaction id + Deployed contract hash on success, or contextual hints ("Generate a template plan first.", an execute-blocked reason, "This exact plan was already submitted…"). Secondary panels: a "Deploy checklist" (3 ordered steps — Validate inputs / Select on-chain template / Deploy NEP-11 from template — each with a status chip), an "On-chain creations" panel (count, Refresh, list of past packages with template id, time, deployed hash + "Yours" tag, or "Record only — no contract deployed"), and a "OneGate launch URL" panel with a Copy link and the deploy-honesty note. A second "Activity" tab holds comments/reviews/activity feed.

**Interface description.** Two-region layout inside the shared "console" shell. TOP: a white hero card (radius 20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,.06)) as a 2-column grid — left a 48px mint icon badge (◆ glyph) + eyebrow "NFT FACTORY" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em tracking, muted) + H2 title "NFT Factory" (22px/800, -0.02em) + the long honest subtitle (14px/1.55, ~620px max, muted #5b6478); right a #f7f8fa inset status box (min 168px) holding a state pill (DRAFT neutral grey / READY mint #e4f8f0+#0fb174 / BLOCKED red #fde7e9+#ea3943), a "DIGEST" metalabel with the last-10 digest chars, and a small network label. BELOW: a 2-column workspace. LEFT column = one white "NEP-11 Collection" card containing the form: a styled Network select, then Collection name, a 2-up row (Symbol + Max supply), Royalty bps (with green hint text), Base URI, Owner (with inline "Use my address" pill affix), the Transferable NFT checkbox toggle, and a full-width lg primary green CTA "Generate template plan". RIGHT column = a stack of cards: (1) "Preview" card with a sample NFT — a square gradient art tile showing the symbol's initial letter, the name as "{name} #1", the symbol chip, and a 3-stat row (Max supply / Royalty / Transfer policy = Transferable|Soulbound); (2) "Template deployment plan" card (variant turns success-green or warning-amber after generate) with a key/value meta block, side-by-side "Blocking errors" / "Warnings" lists, the JSON disclosure, and the Copy / Sign / Deploy button trio plus result/signature/error blocks; (3) "Deploy checklist" ordered list with leading status chips (Pending/Ready/Manual/Blocked); (4) "On-chain creations" panel with a count bar + Refresh and an empty/loading/error/list StateView; (5) "OneGate launch URL" card with the URL, Copy link, and the small grey deploy-honesty paragraph. Identity is Neo Soft light: white cards on a near-white page, hairline borders, pill buttons/chips, mint-green accent (#16c784 decorative, #0a8050 filled CTA, #0fb174 success text), tabular-nums for digests/figures. Forms use #f7f8fa fields; mono code for digests/hashes/signatures.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty draft** — Wallet not connected or fresh form. Hero pill shows neutral grey DRAFT, digest shows a provisional last-10. Form has empty/seeded fields (Owner may be prefilled from wallet with no 'Use my address' affix). Preview shows 'Untitled collection' with a placeholder 'SYMBOL' tile and Max supply '—', Royalty 0%, Transferable. Deploy checklist chips all read 'Pending' in neutral draft palette (not red). Plan card is neutral (erobo), Sign/Deploy disabled. On-chain creations shows the empty StateView 'No packages recorded on-chain yet.'
- **Filled / ready** — Valid inputs entered. Preview NFT card updates live (initial letter art, '{name} #1', symbol chip, real max supply / royalty% / transfer policy). Royalty hint text visible under the bps field. CTA 'Generate template plan' active. Plan card not yet generated, so Sign/Deploy still disabled and the 'Generate a template plan first.' hint may appear if user tries to act.
- **Plan generated — publishable (READY)** — After Generate. Hero pill flips to mint READY, status box shows live digest. Plan card turns success-green: Plan ID, full Plan digest, Template artifact = 'Preloaded on-chain', '≈ {amount} GAS' estimated fee, 'No blocking issues.' / 'No warnings.', JSON disclosure available. Sign and Deploy buttons enabled. Deploy checklist chips show Ready/Manual.
- **Plan generated — blocked** — Validation or artifact problem. Hero pill red BLOCKED, plan card turns warning-amber. 'Blocking errors' lists human messages (e.g. 'Symbol must be 2-12 uppercase letters or digits and start with a letter.', 'Base URI must be an HTTPS URL ending with /.', or 'Template artifact not registered on-chain yet…'). Deploy disabled with a contextual blocked-reason hint; a 'Mainnet packages require signer, GAS, domain, and registry review' warning may show on mainnet.
- **Submitting (loading)** — Generate / Sign / Deploy in progress. The relevant button shows its loading spinner and is disabled; other actions disabled. On-chain creations panel may show its 'Loading on-chain records…' StateView during refresh.
- **Success / result** — After Sign: a wallet-signature code block with Copy. After Deploy: a result block with 'Transaction' txid (mono code) and 'Deployed contract' hash + Copy contract hash. Hero stays READY; the new package appears in 'On-chain creations' with a 'Yours' tag and deployed hash (or 'Record only — no contract deployed' for miniapp-style records). Re-submitting the same plan shows 'This exact plan was already submitted…' hint.
- **Error** — Failed signing/execution. A red error block renders the failure message below the action trio; On-chain creations can show its error StateView 'Could not load on-chain records.' with a Retry button.

**Visual identity.** Neo Soft light theme with the default mint-green brand accent (#16c784 decorative badges/chips, #0a8050 filled CTA white text, #0fb174 success text, #e4f8f0 soft success fill); manifest accentColor #16c784, density comfortable. Signature visual = the 48px mint icon badge with a ◆ diamond glyph in the hero, paired with the live NFT Preview card (square gradient art tile stamped with the symbol's initial letter, '{name} #1' caption, symbol chip, 3-stat strip). State is communicated by a single tri-state hero pill — neutral grey DRAFT, mint READY (#e4f8f0/#0fb174), red BLOCKED (#fde7e9/#ea3943) — and by the plan card shifting between neutral, success-green, and warning-amber. Plan digests, txids, contract hashes, and signatures render as monospaced code; figures and digests use tabular-nums. White cards (radius 20/14px) with hairline borders and soft shadows on a near-white page; #f7f8fa insets for the status box and form fields; pill buttons and tabs.

## ProfitAnchor Admin  `profitanchor-admin`
*Category: tool*

> Operator console for manually routing NEO between ProfitAnchor's 21 AA agents, retargeting their vote candidate, and syncing votes — three deliberate steps, no auto-rebalance.

**Purpose.** ProfitAnchor Admin is a back-office console for the single operator wallet that controls the ProfitAnchor product's pool of 21 abstract-account (AA) agents holding NEO. The operator opens it to do exactly three things by hand: move whole NEO between those agents, set which council candidate an agent votes for, and submit (sync) that vote on-chain. The candidate the agents vote for IS ProfitAnchor's profit policy — it determines the GAS rewards stakers earn — so the console deliberately exposes manual, auditable controls rather than any automated strategy. Non-operator wallets see the same layout in a read-only state.

**Functional description.** Connect the operator wallet (walletRequired). On connect, the console loads live data: tracked NEO total, agent count (e.g. "21 / 21"), the selected vote route, the GAS reserve, and per-agent balances/candidates into the directory. Three sequential, independent operations, each its own transaction:

STEP 1 — Move NEO (primary): pick "From agent" (number 1–21, default 1) and "To agent" (default 2), enter a whole "NEO amount" (suffix "NEO", min 1; NEO is indivisible). The two agents must differ ("Choose two different agents") and the amount is pre-validated against the source agent's live on-chain balance — when known, a hint reads "Move amount is checked against the source agent's on-chain NEO balance below. #1: <bal> NEO"; over-balance shows the alert "Amount exceeds the source agent's NEO balance." and disables submit. CTA "Submit move" → calls transferAgentNeo → "Agent transfer submitted". Footer rule: "No auto rebalance — you choose the route."

STEP 2 — Update candidate (secondary): pick "Agent" (1–21) and paste a "Candidate public key" (placeholder "02…", must be a 33-byte compressed key). CTA "Update candidate" → setAgentCandidate → "Candidate update submitted". Footer rule: "Candidate key is set explicitly."

STEP 3 — Sync vote (secondary): a "Current vote route" preview chip shows the selected agent; an "Agent" number input (1–21, auto-prefilled to the selected route until the operator edits it). When an agent is chosen, a warning note appears: title "Needs the agent witness" + body "Syncing this vote requires agent #{n} ({account}) to co-sign with its AA witness. If this session cannot provide that witness, the transaction will fail." CTA "Sync vote" → voteAgent → "Vote sync submitted". Footer rule: "Vote sync is a separate transaction."

Each CTA shows its own in-flight spinner; only one action runs at a time. If the connected wallet is not the operator, controls go read-only and a banner explains "Operator wallet required."

**Interface description.** Two-region layout on a light canvas: a main column (hero + three workflow cards) and a right sidebar (agent directory + operator-rule card). Green accent throughout (decorative #16c784, soft fill #e4f8f0, CTA filled #0a8050 white text, accent text #0a7a50).

HERO BAND (white rounded card): a 40px green-soft rounded icon badge (finance/settings glyph) on the left; beside it the uppercase eyebrow "PROFITANCHOR ADMIN", the 24–30px/800 title "Move. Target. Vote." and a muted subtitle ("Manual NEO routing only. Prepare every ProfitAnchor AA agent transfer, candidate update, and vote sync from one operator console."). Below sits a 4-up inline stat row, each as eyebrow label + bold tabular figure: SELECTED ROUTE (None), AGENTS (21 / 21), TRACKED NEO (0.00 NEO), RESERVE (0.00 GAS). A green-dot status caption underneath reads "Live route, agent count and reserves load once an operator connects." until data loads.

COMMAND GRID: three equal white workflow cards in a row (stack on mobile). Each card has a card title, a small uppercase "STEP 1/2/3" tag, a one-line description, its inputs (inset #f7f8fa fields with uppercase field labels), the contextual hint/note, a full-width pill CTA, and a tiny muted footer rule line. Card 1 "Move NEO" has a 3-field grid (From agent / To agent / NEO amount). Card 2 "Update candidate" has Agent + Candidate public key. Card 3 "Sync vote" has a "CURRENT VOTE ROUTE" preview block (green-soft, bold value), the Agent input, and a warm-amber witness note (#fff3dd fill, amber border/heading) — the one intentional non-green accent.

SIDEBAR: (a) "Agent directory" card with a green count pill ("21 / 21") in its heading; body is either the live list (rows of #N, NEO balance, Active/Inactive flag, truncated account code, "Candidate: <key|No candidate>"), or — at rest — three skeleton rows + caption "Live agent balances and candidates load once an operator connects." + "21 provisioned routes". (b) "Operator rule" card with heading + green "Manual only" pill, the rule text ("ProfitAnchor Admin does only three things…"), and a green-soft causality callout ("The candidate these agents vote for is ProfitAnchor's profit policy — it sets the GAS rewards stakers earn."). Cards use 16/20px radius and soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,.06).

**Key screens to mock.**
- **Resting / pre-connect (default)** — Wallet connected but live data not yet loaded (the rendered screenshot state). Hero stats all show None/21·21/0.00 NEO/0.00 GAS with the green-dot 'Live route... load once an operator connects' caption. All three workflow cards are fully armed with seeded defaults (1→2, amount 1, candidate empty, vote agent 1) and the amber witness note already visible under Sync vote. Sidebar directory shows 3 skeleton rows + 'Live agent balances... load once an operator connects.' + '21 provisioned routes'. Operator-rule card present.
- **Live / loaded operator** — Stats populated with real route, NEO total and GAS reserve. Move card shows the source-balance hint '#1: <bal> NEO'. Sidebar directory renders up to 21 live agent rows (#N, NEO balance, Active/Inactive flag, account code, candidate key). Current vote route chip shows the selected agent; vote input prefilled to it.
- **Read-only / non-operator** — Connected wallet is not the operator. A status banner appears above the command grid: 'Read-only' eyebrow, 'Operator wallet required' title, body explaining the controls are read-only for other wallets (includes expected operator address when known). Command grid is dimmed/disabled; all CTAs inert.
- **Validation / blocked input** — Move card with same From and To agent (or amount exceeding source balance): submit disabled, red 'Amount exceeds the source agent's NEO balance.' alert shown. Other inline errors: 'NEO is indivisible — enter a whole number', 'Candidate must be a 33-byte compressed public key', 'Agent ID must be a whole number from 1 to 21'.
- **Submitting / in-flight** — A CTA tapped — that single button shows its own loading spinner ('Submit move' / 'Update candidate' / 'Sync vote'); the other two CTAs disable while one action is in flight. Multi-second wallet round-trip.
- **Submitted / success** — Toast/confirmation after each action: 'Agent transfer submitted', 'Candidate update submitted', or 'Vote sync submitted'. Directory and hero stats refresh to reflect the new balances/route.

**Visual identity.** Green Neo Soft accent (decorative #16c784, soft fill #e4f8f0, CTA filled #0a8050 with white text, accent text/labels #0a7a50). Signature element is the numbered three-step operator workflow (STEP 1·2·3 tags on three side-by-side cards titled Move NEO / Update candidate / Sync vote) reinforcing "Move. Target. Vote." The one intentional off-green accent is the warm-amber witness warning (#fff3dd fill, amber #b9791a heading/border) for the AA co-sign caveat. Other identity cues: a 40px green-soft rounded icon badge in the hero, green count pills ("21 / 21", "Manual only"), and the green-soft yield-causality callout tying the vote candidate to staker GAS rewards.

## Timestamp Proof  `timestamp-proof`
*Category: tool*

> Hash any text into a SHA-256 proof journal and optionally anchor it to Neo N3 so anyone can verify the digest and time on-chain.

**Purpose.** Timestamp Proof lets a user create tamper-evident proof-of-existence records.

**Functional description.** No wallet is required to start (wallet only needed for the optional anchor). PRIMARY FLOW — Create Proof: user types/pastes into the "Enter content to timestamp" textarea (placeholder "Paste your text, document hash, or idea..."); the full-width green "Create Proof" button is disabled with a hint "Add some text above to enable timestamping." until there is text. On click it hashes locally (button shows "Creating..."/loading), saves a proof to device storage, clears the textarea, toasts "Proof saved to this device.", and the new entry appears in the Recent Proofs list (newest first, capped at 200). VERIFY FLOW — in the "Verify Proof" card the user types a "Proof lookup" value (placeholder "Proof ID, SHA-256 digest, or original content") and clicks the secondary "Verify Proof" button ("Verifying..." while loading). On hit it renders a green "Proof Found" result block with rows: Proof ID (#id, mono), Timestamp (locale datetime), SHA-256 digest (full mono hash), Status (pill: "Anchored on-chain" or "Local only"), and Content preview. A miss shows inline field error "Invalid Proof"; empty shows "No proof selected". ANCHOR FLOW — for a local-only proof a cost note appears ("Anchoring writes the digest into a public Neo transaction (small network fee). Local proofs stay private and free.") with an "Anchor on-chain" button; once anchored the result adds an "Anchor transaction" row (txid linking to the block explorer) plus a "How to verify on-chain" note. RECENT PROOFS list rows show #id, the digest (mono, truncated), the timestamp, an anchor-status pill, and per-row actions: Verify, Anchor (short, if local), copy-digest (⎘), copy-reference (⧉), delete (✕); a "Clear all" ghost button sits in the card header. Stats update live: Total Proofs, Anchored, Latest ID.

**Interface description.** Single centered column, max-width ~760px, ~20px vertical rhythm; light theme on a subtle page. TOP — hero card (white, radius 20, soft shadow): left a 46px mint-soft (#e4f8f0) circular badge containing an animated dashed "stamp seal" ring with a green sparkle/star mark; right a stack of eyebrow "PROOFS" (11px/700 uppercase, .12em, green #0fb174), title "Timestamp Proof" (26px/800, -0.02em, ink #1e1e2e), and subtitle "SHA-256 proof journal with optional on-chain anchor" (13px, #5b6478). NEXT — a 3-up stat-tile grid (white tiles, radius 14, soft shadow), each with an uppercase muted label + value: TOTAL PROOFS (0), ANCHORED (0), LATEST ID (— in green mono). Then the "Create Proof" NeoCard: field label "Enter content to timestamp", a multi-line textarea, a full-width green primary CTA (disabled = soft mint fill with green text; enabled = solid #16c784 with green glow shadow), and the disabled hint line. Then the "Verify Proof" NeoCard: "Proof lookup" text input + full-width secondary button, then either the empty line "No proof selected" or a mint-soft (#e4f8f0) result panel with an uppercase green "PROOF FOUND" label and label/value rows (values right-aligned, mono for hashes), status pills, and (for local proofs) a cost note + small green "Anchor on-chain" button. BOTTOM — when there are zero proofs, a subtle #f7f8fa empty card: centered 48px mint-soft circle with green sparkle icon, "No proofs yet" (muted, 600), and hint "Saved proof entries will appear here."; when populated, this becomes a "Recent Proofs" NeoCard with a "Clear all" ghost button in the header and a stacked list of inset (#f7f8fa, radius 14) proof rows. Identity color is mint/green throughout (single accent hue). Anchor pills: anchored = mint-soft fill + green text; local = grey inset with hairline border.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / empty** — Stats all 0 / LATEST ID = —. Create Proof card with empty textarea, disabled soft-mint CTA + 'Add some text above to enable timestamping.' hint. Verify card shows 'No proof selected'. Bottom shows the subtle empty card: mint circle + sparkle, 'No proofs yet', 'Saved proof entries will appear here.'
- **Ready to create** — Textarea filled with user content; CTA becomes solid green #16c784 with glow and is enabled; hint hidden.
- **Creating (loading)** — CTA shows spinner + 'Creating...', textarea content preserved until success; on success textarea clears and a 'Proof saved to this device.' toast fires.
- **Populated journal** — Stats updated (e.g. Total Proofs 3, Anchored 1, Latest ID #3). 'Recent Proofs' card with 'Clear all' ghost header button and inset rows: #id (green mono), digest (mono truncated), timestamp, anchor pill, and Verify / Anchor / copy-digest / copy-reference / delete actions.
- **Verify — Proof Found (local)** — Mint-soft result panel: 'PROOF FOUND' label, rows for Proof ID, Timestamp, SHA-256 digest, Status pill 'Local only', Content preview; cost note + small green 'Anchor on-chain' button.
- **Verify — Proof Found (anchored)** — Same panel with Status pill 'Anchored on-chain', an 'Anchor transaction' row with txid linking to the explorer, and a 'How to verify on-chain' note explaining the 'timestamp-proof:<digest>' data + block time.
- **Verify — Invalid** — Lookup input shows inline error state with 'Invalid Proof' message; no result panel.
- **Anchoring (loading)** — The 'Anchor on-chain' / row 'Anchor' button shows a spinner for the matching proof id while the on-chain self-transfer is broadcast.

**Visual identity.** Mint/green secondary theme within Neo Soft (intentional for timestamp-proof). Single accent hue: bright mint #16c784, strong green #0fb174 for text/icons, soft mint #e4f8f0 for badges/result panel/pills. Signature visual = an animated wax "stamp seal": a 36px circle with a slowly rotating dashed ring and a green sparkle/star (✦) mark that gently pulses (stamp-impression), reused at small size as the empty-state and stat icon. Anchor-status pills (mint 'Anchored on-chain' vs grey 'Local only') and green mono hash text reinforce the cryptographic/proof identity. Respects prefers-reduced-motion (animations off).

## Wallet Health  `wallet-health`
*Category: tool*

> A self-assessment security console for Neo N3 wallets: connect to read live NEO/GAS balances, work through a 6-point safety checklist, and export a health report.

**Purpose.** Wallet Health is a non-custodial security hygiene tool for Neo N3 users. A user opens it to gauge how safe their wallet setup is before moving assets or approving app requests — it surfaces connection/network status, live NEO and GAS balances, and a checklist-driven "safety score" (0–100%) mapped to a Low/Medium/High risk label. It is explicitly a SELF-ASSESSMENT, not an on-chain audit: the score comes from the user ticking off security best-practices (which persist locally on-device), and the only auto-evaluated item is whether the connected wallet holds enough GAS for fees. The output is a copyable/downloadable text report plus a "Next actions" remediation list.

**Functional description.** PRIMARY FLOW: (1) On open, the app is Disconnected — score ring shows a calm em-dash "—" with the caption "Connect to score", risk chip defaults to red "High risk", and the checklist progress reads "0/5 complete". (2) User taps the filled green "Connect Wallet" button (in the "Balance and connection" panel) → button shows "..." loading then becomes a disabled "Connected" state; the "Refresh balances" secondary button enables. (3) On connect the app reads the wallet address (truncated as 8 chars…6 chars), network label (Neo N3), and live NEO + GAS balances which fill the "NEO" and "GAS" tiles (were "—"). (4) The auto "Keep GAS for fees" checklist item evaluates: if GAS ≥ 0.1 it auto-checks ("Auto"), otherwise it stays unchecked and adds a "Top up GAS to avoid failed transactions." recommendation. (5) "Refresh balances" re-reads on-chain balances. CHECKLIST ACTION (right column, 6 items): five manual items each have a "Mark done" button that toggles to a green "Undo"; the GAS item is automatic and shows "Connect to check" (pending, before connect) or "Auto" (after). Each toggle recomputes: completed count, the safetyScore (= completed / evaluable-total × 100, rounded), the risk chip (≥80% Low / ≥50% Medium / <50% High), and the "Next actions" list (every unchecked manual item contributes its recommendation; if all clear, shows "All checks look good"). REPORT ACTIONS (bottom of "Next actions" panel): "Copy address" (disabled until connected), "Copy report" and "Download report" (.txt named wallet-health-<addr8>.txt). After each, an aria-live status line updates: "Report ready" → "Address copied" / "Report copied" / "Report downloaded" / "Action failed". The checklist is device-local (localStorage-backed) and never leaves the device; balances are read-only — the tool cannot read or revoke wallet approvals (called out explicitly in the permissions item and its recommendation).

**Interface description.** Two-column workspace on a #f7f8fa page (no separate hero band — the hero is the first card of the left column). LEFT COLUMN (~0.78fr, three stacked white rounded cards, radius 20px, soft shadow 0 8px 24px rgba(20,22,38,.06)): (A) HERO CARD — a circular score ring on the left (currently an empty thin ring with a centered em-dash "—"; when scored it fills proportionally with the number inside, tabular-nums), then a copy block: green uppercase eyebrow "NEO N3" (11px/700/.12em), title "Wallet Health" (22px/800/-0.02em ink), a muted caption ("Connect to score" or "Self-assessment score — based on your checklist, not an on-chain audit."), and a facts row = a pill risk chip with a colored status dot ("● High risk" red) · separator · "0/5 complete". (B) BALANCE AND CONNECTION CARD — section heading row "BALANCE AND CONNECTION" (left) with the address/status on the right ("Disconnected" or truncated address); a 3-up inset grid of #f7f8fa tiles labeled CONNECTION / NEO / GAS each with a bold value ("Disconnected", "—", "—"); a primary-actions row = filled deep-green "Connect Wallet" + soft-green "Refresh balances"; a muted hint line "Connect a Neo N3 wallet to refresh live balances. The safety checklist stays local on this device." (C) NEXT ACTIONS CARD — heading "NEXT ACTIONS" with a count badge ("5") on the right; a list of recommendation rows, each an inset #f7f8fa pill with a small green dot bullet (e.g. "Backup your seed phrase immediately.", "Review approvals in your wallet app — this tool can't read or revoke them for you.", "Only sign on a trusted, private device.", "Move large balances to a hardware wallet.", "Enable 2FA on exchange accounts tied to this wallet."); a divider, then a small-button action row "Copy address" (soft-green primary) / "Copy report" / "Download report" (outline secondary), and a tiny status line "Report ready". RIGHT COLUMN (~0.42fr, sticky): SAFETY CHECKLIST card — heading "SAFETY CHECKLIST" with "0/5 complete" on the right; six rows each = bold item title + muted description on the left and an action control on the right (outline "Mark done" pill for the 5 manual items; the GAS item shows "● Connect to check" / "Auto"). Items: "Backup phrase stored", "Keep GAS for fees", "Review app permissions in your wallet", "Trusted device only", "Hardware wallet for large funds", "Enable 2FA where possible". COLOR/IDENTITY: standard Neo Soft green — #16c784 bright accents (eyebrow, dots, soft chips), #0a8050 filled CTA white text, #e4f8f0 soft fills; ink #1e1e2e titles, #5b6478 body, muted #6b7180; risk chip is semantic (red High / amber Medium / green Low). Mobile 390px = single column, hero ring scales down, checklist stacks below.

**Key screens to mock.**
- **First-run / Disconnected (empty)** — Default state. Score ring shows em-dash '—' with caption 'Connect to score'; risk chip red 'High risk' · '0/5 complete'. Balance tiles all read '—' / 'Disconnected'. 'Connect Wallet' enabled (filled green), 'Refresh balances' disabled (faded). Next actions list pre-populated with the 5 manual-item recommendations. GAS checklist row shows '● Connect to check'; report 'Copy address' disabled.
- **Connecting / Refreshing (loading)** — Connect Wallet button shows loading label ('...'/spinner) and is disabled; or after connect, Refresh balances shows the loading state. Balance tiles may briefly hold prior/placeholder values. Designer should show the busy/disabled treatment of the primary and secondary buttons.
- **Connected & scored (filled / ready)** — Wallet connected: NEO/GAS tiles show real balances, CONNECTION tile reads 'Connected', heading-right shows truncated address (8…6). Score ring filled with a real % and number; risk chip recolored to match (e.g. amber 'Medium risk' / green 'Low risk'); progress updates e.g. '3/5 complete'. GAS item auto-checked ('Auto'). Some checklist rows toggled to green 'Undo'. Next actions list shrinks as items are completed.
- **All clear (best state)** — All evaluable items done → score ~100%, green 'Low risk' chip, '5/5 complete' (plus auto GAS). Next actions list collapses to a single positive row 'All checks look good'. Score ring fully filled green.
- **Report action feedback (success / error)** — After Copy address / Copy report / Download report, the bottom status line updates to 'Address copied' / 'Report copied' / 'Report downloaded' (aria-live). Error variant shows 'Action failed'. Show the subtle confirmation microstate near the report action row.

**Visual identity.** Standard Neo Soft green identity (no secondary theme). Signature visual = the circular SAFETY SCORE RING in the hero: a thin-stroke progress ring that fills proportionally (0–100%) with a large tabular-nums number centered inside; in the disconnected/zero state it renders as a calm empty ring with an em-dash "—" (deliberately NOT a stark red "0") plus the "Connect to score" caption. Reinforced by the semantic risk chip with a colored status dot (red High / amber Medium / green Low) and the small green dot bullets on the Next actions list. Brand green #16c784 for accents/eyebrow, #0a8050 for the filled Connect CTA, soft #e4f8f0 chips.
